冻结源码证据；M/P身份见 source-snapshot.json。不是运行证据。

### M:AGENTS.md
   1 # Ordessa — workspace instructions for agents
   2 
   3 This directory is the **Ordessa product monorepo**. One repository holds the
   4 product's active source; the previous multi-repo workspace organisation has
   5 been retired (history and restore paths: `docs/reference-index.md`,
   6 `docs/migration/`).
   7 
   8 ## What this repo is
   9 
  10 - Product/repo name: **Ordessa**. Remote identity: `github.com/mmm-05610/ordessa`
  11   (renamed from `mmm-05610/agent-box` on 2026-09-29; GitHub redirects the old
  12   URL) — see `docs/migration/remote-plan.md` before any publish work; **no push
  13   without explicit user authorisation**.
  14 - `packages/pacthold` (Python `pacthold`) — governance kernel and plugin SDK.
  15   Zero product dependencies. Nothing here may import a plugin, app or product.
  16 - `apps/server` (Python `ordessa_server`) — Ordessa Server. Depends on
  17   `pacthold`, composes the plugin root, owns wire/transport.
  18 - `plugins/harness` (Python `ordessa_harness`, dist `ordessa-harness`) — agent
  19   harness lifecycle, ACP adaptation, and the Go ACP bridge at
  20   `adapters/acp-adapter`. Bridge internals (build flags, binary paths, Pi CLI)
  21   are **harness-internal**; upper layers consume start/connect/close only.
  22 - `packages/desktop-platform/*` — extension api/loader/host, native bridge,
  23   contracts. Independent npm packages under one umbrella.
  24 - `apps/desktop` — Electron app shell (its own `AGENTS.md` has the desktop
  25   host rules). `plugins/*` — desktop plugins. `products/desktop` — assembly
  26   manifest for the default product build.
  27 - Profile / Provider / Workboard continue as **independent repositories
  28   outside this monorepo** (archived refs kept here, live trees listed in
  29   `docs/reference-index.md`). They are not part of this baseline; no
  30   placeholder implementations for them belong in `main`.
  31 
  32 ## Rules
  33 
  34 1. **Feature work branches from the fixed baseline** named in
  35    `docs/baseline.md`; integration commits to `main` go through review.
  36 2. **Python packages** keep their own `pyproject.toml` and install editable
  37    (`packages/pacthold`, `apps/server`, `plugins/harness`). **JS/TS** uses npm
  38    workspaces at the root. Do not cross-wire package managers.
  39 3. **Import boundaries are load-bearing**: `pacthold` must not import
  40    `ordessa_server`/`ordessa_harness`; plugins import platform contracts,
  41    never host internals; the product must not assemble bridge-internal paths.
  42    Boundary tests exist — keep them passing.
  43 4. **No binaries in git.** Build the bridge via its packaging script; pin
  44    toolchain versions as recorded in `docs/baseline.md`.
  45 5. **Data compatibility**: on-disk formats (data-root layout, DB migrations,
  46    deployment JSON, extension ids) keep their existing identifiers. Renames
  47    require an explicit note in `docs/migration/` and user confirmation if
  48    user data is affected.
  49 6. Old names (`agent_box`, `agent-box`) remain only where they are upstream
  50    identifiers, URLs, licenses or history documents — listed in
  51    `docs/naming.md`.
  52 7. **Credentials and user data never enter this repo.** In-place run-data
  53    directories kept for live services are environment exceptions, listed in
  54    `docs/known-issues.md` and ignored explicitly — they are not part of the
  55    source tree, and `.gitignore` is not a substitute for physical removal.
  56 8. Do not kill/restart user-facing services. The live-service registry and
  57    "may I touch it" status live in `docs/known-issues.md` §services.
  58 9. Real-model test calls require prior user authorisation (scope + cost).
  59    Controlled tests must not fake green: no assertion deletion, no skips to
  60    hide failures, no reviving legacy compat chains.
  61 10. The baseline carries registered known issues (`docs/known-issues.md`);
  62     "not perfect" does not block development, but every known failure must be
  63     honestly registered there.
  64 
  65 ## Entry points
  66 
  67 | I want | Go to |
  68 | --- | --- |
  69 | Build/run/test commands, toolchain versions | `docs/baseline.md` |
  70 | Component map and dependency direction | `docs/architecture.md` |
  71 | Registered known issues & untested scope | `docs/known-issues.md` |
  72 | Where old code/history lives, how to restore | `docs/reference-index.md` |
  73 | Naming map (old→new) | `docs/naming.md` |
  74 | How this monorepo was assembled | `docs/migration/` |

### M:apps/desktop/AGENTS.md
   1 # Minimal Ordessa Desktop
   2 
   3 This is the clean Lumino desktop skeleton, not the historical Hermes client.
   4 Host API v2 is intentionally small: lifecycle, scope, one root UI. No pages/layout in host.
   5 This CP candidate admits commands, workbench, connections, sessions, the Ordessa Server connector and conversation through products/desktop/extensions.json.
   6 Their shared Token contracts are built separately from packages/desktop-platform/contracts/foundation
   7 (platform: commands, the Workbench public API, the generic Connections platform API) and
   8 plugins/agent/contracts (Agent-domain carrier `ordessa.agent-contracts`). The platform umbrella never
   9 re-export a domain API; consumers import the `@extensions/<id>/contract.js` shared module, not a path.
  10 Product defaults live in products/desktop/extensions.json, never business imports in app.tsx.
  11 User extensions.json is a complete explicit override, including an empty list; never overwrite it.
  12 All service registration APIs bind to the caller's ResourceScope and auto-release on exit.
  13 Business extensions are discovered from a configured local directory and explicitly enabled.
  14 Examples are independent build inputs, not host imports or application dependencies.
  15 Do not import or copy legacy business code unless requested.
  16 Keep the host domain-neutral. Lumino owns plugin dependency resolution and lifecycle.
  17 Use explicit package exports; do not add a second plugin scheduler or expose its raw registry.
  18 No credentials, real backend requests, pushes, or publishing main changes.
  19 Tests run serially. Electron smoke uses an isolated temporary userData and test-only --no-sandbox.
  20 Normal development must not silently disable sandboxing.

### M:packages/desktop-platform/extension-api/src/index.ts
   1 import { Token, type IPlugin } from '@lumino/coreutils'
   2 import type { IDisposable } from '@lumino/disposable'
   3 import type { ComponentType } from 'react'
   4 import { Contributions } from './contributions'
   5 
   6 export class OwnedResources {
   7   private items: IDisposable[] = []
   8   isDisposed = false
   9   add<T extends IDisposable>(item: T): T {
  10     if (this.isDisposed) {
  11       item.dispose()
  12       throw Error('Resource scope is closed')
  13     }
  14     this.items.push(item)
  15     return item
  16   }
  17   dispose() {
  18     if (this.isDisposed) return
  19     this.isDisposed = true
  20     const errors: unknown[] = []
  21     for (const item of this.items.splice(0).reverse()) {
  22       try { item.dispose() } catch (error) { errors.push(error) }
  23     }
  24     if (errors.length) throw new AggregateError(errors, 'Extension cleanup failed')
  25   }
  26 }
  27 export interface RootView { id: string; component: ComponentType }
  28 export interface ResourceScope {
  29   readonly isDisposed: boolean
  30   add<T extends IDisposable>(item: T): T
  31 }
  32 // Host is private to product composition; plugins receive the narrower context.
  33 export interface Host { roots: Contributions<RootView> }
  34 export interface PluginContext {
  35   readonly root: { mount(view: RootView): IDisposable }
  36   readonly resources: ResourceScope
  37 }
  38 export type Plugin<T = unknown> = IPlugin<PluginContext, T>
  39 /** Convenience only: ownership/rollback is enforced by the runtime for ALL plugins. */
  40 export function scoped<T>(definition: Omit<Plugin<T>, 'activate'> & {
  41   activate: (host: PluginContext, owned: PluginContext['resources'], ...services: any[]) => T | Promise<T>
  42 }): Plugin<T> {
  43   return { ...definition, activate: (host, ...services) => definition.activate(host, host.resources, ...services) }
  44 }
  45 export { Token, Contributions }
  46 export type { IDisposable }
  47 export const HOST_API_VERSION = '2'

### M:packages/desktop-platform/extension-host/src/runtime.ts
   1 import { PluginRegistry, type Token } from '@lumino/coreutils'
   2 import { Contributions, OwnedResources, type Host, type RootView, type Plugin, type PluginContext } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   3 import { CommandSourceToken, DiagnosticsContributionToken, HarnessAvailabilityToken, KeybindingServiceToken, LoggerToken, SettingsContributionToken, ThemeServiceToken, WirePortToken } from '../../contracts/foundation/src/product/tokens'
   4 export { scoped, OwnedResources } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   5 export type { Host, RootView, Plugin } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   6 export interface PluginState { id: string; phase: 'registered' | 'starting' | 'active' | 'stopped' | 'failed'; error?: string }
   7 
   8 // 平台服务类型从公开契约模块内联取（相对路径），使 runtime.ts 不依赖任何构建期别名。
   9 type Logger = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/logging').Logger
  10 type ThemeService = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/theme').ThemeService
  11 type SettingsContribution = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/settings-diagnostics').SettingsContribution
  12 type DiagnosticsContribution = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/settings-diagnostics').DiagnosticsContribution
  13 type CommandSource = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/commands-keybindings').CommandSource
  14 type KeybindingService = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/commands-keybindings').KeybindingService
  15 type WirePort = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/wire-port').WirePort
  16 type HarnessAvailability = import('../../contracts/foundation/src/product/harness-availability').HarnessAvailability
  17 
  18 export interface PlatformServiceMap {
  19   logger?: Logger; theme?: ThemeService; settings?: SettingsContribution
  20   diagnostics?: DiagnosticsContribution; commands?: CommandSource; keybindings?: KeybindingService
  21   wire?: WirePort; harness?: HarnessAvailability
  22 }
  23 
  24 /**
  25  * 平台服务注册为**宿主自带的提供者插件**：lumino 只认 `provides`（没有 application 兜底），
  26  * 因此每个 token 一个内部 provider，插件用 `requires` 拿到对应实例。
  27  * 缺席的服务不注册 → 解析失败是显式的 `No provider`，而不是一个 undefined 悄悄流下去。
  28  */
  29 function registerHostServices(registry: PluginRegistry<Host>, services: PlatformServiceMap) {
  30   const pairs: [Token<unknown>, unknown][] = [
  31     [LoggerToken, services.logger], [ThemeServiceToken, services.theme],
  32     [SettingsContributionToken, services.settings], [DiagnosticsContributionToken, services.diagnostics],
  33     [CommandSourceToken, services.commands], [KeybindingServiceToken, services.keybindings],
  34     [WirePortToken, services.wire], [HarnessAvailabilityToken, services.harness],
  35   ]
  36   for (const [token, service] of pairs) {
  37     if (service === undefined) continue
  38     registry.registerPlugin({ id: `ordessa.host.${token.name}`, provides: token, activate: () => service as never })
  39   }
  40 }
  41 /**
  42  * `services` 是宿主注入的平台服务（C-03/04/05/06/07/08）。缺席的服务**不注册**：
  43  * 插件若把它列进 `requires` 会得到显式的 `No provider`，列进 `optional` 则拿到 null
  44  * （README §3 缺席语义），两者都不会把 undefined 悄悄传下去。
  45  */
  46 export function runtime(plugins: Plugin<any>[], services: PlatformServiceMap = {}) {
  47   const providers = new Set(), ids = new Set<string>()
  48   for (const plugin of plugins) {
  49     if (ids.has(plugin.id)) throw Error('Duplicate plugin: ' + plugin.id)
  50     ids.add(plugin.id)
  51     if (plugin.provides && providers.has(plugin.provides)) throw Error('Duplicate provider: ' + plugin.provides.name)
  52     if (plugin.provides) providers.add(plugin.provides)
  53   }
  54   // lumino 按 `application[token.name]` 解析服务，故宿主对象带 token 名字键；
  55   // 这些键不进入 `Host` 的公开形状（插件看到的公开面仍是 C-0x 契约本身）。
  56   const host: Host = { roots: new Contributions<RootView>() }
  57   const registry = new PluginRegistry<Host>()
  58   registry.application = host
  59   registerHostServices(registry, services)
  60   const failures: { id: string; error: string }[] = []
  61   const states = new Map<string, PluginState>(), listeners = new Set<() => void>()
  62   let snapshot: PluginState[] = []
  63   function update(id: string, phase: PluginState['phase'], error?: string) {
  64     states.set(id, { id, phase, ...(error ? { error } : {}) })
  65     snapshot = [...states.values()]
  66     listeners.forEach(listener => listener())
  67   }
  68   function failed(id: string, error: unknown) {
  69     const message = String(error)
  70     if (!failures.some(f => f.id === id && f.error === message)) failures.push({ id, error: message })
  71     update(id, 'failed', message)
  72   }
  73   for (const plugin of plugins) {
  74     let owned: OwnedResources | undefined
  75     let active: { context: PluginContext; services: any[] } | undefined
  76     const close = () => { const resources = owned; owned = undefined; resources?.dispose() }
  77     try {
  78       registry.registerPlugin({
  79         ...plugin,
  80         async activate(_host, ...services) {
  81           update(plugin.id, 'starting')
  82           const resources = new OwnedResources()
  83           owned = resources
  84           const context: PluginContext = Object.freeze({
  85             root: Object.freeze({ mount(view: RootView) {
  86               if (resources.isDisposed) throw Error('Plugin scope is closed')
  87               if (host.roots.getSnapshot().length) throw Error('Root view already mounted')
  88               return resources.add(host.roots.add(view))
  89             } }),
  90             resources: Object.freeze({ get isDisposed() { return resources.isDisposed }, add: resources.add.bind(resources) }),
  91           })
  92           active = { context, services }
  93           try {
  94             const result = await plugin.activate(context, ...services)
  95             update(plugin.id, 'active')
  96             return result
  97           } catch (error) {
  98             let cause = error
  99             try { close() } catch (cleanup) { cause = new AggregateError([error, cleanup], 'Activation and cleanup failed') }
 100             failed(plugin.id, cause)
 101             active = undefined
 102             throw cause
 103           }
 104         },
 105         async deactivate() {
 106           const errors: unknown[] = []
 107           const previous = active
 108           active = undefined
 109           // Close first: even callbacks during async cleanup cannot register again.
 110           try { close() } catch (error) { errors.push(error) }
 111           try { if (previous) await plugin.deactivate?.(previous.context, ...previous.services) } catch (error) { errors.push(error) }
 112           if (errors.length) {
 113             const error = new AggregateError(errors, 'Deactivation cleanup failed')
 114             failed(plugin.id, error)
 115             throw error
 116           }
 117           update(plugin.id, 'stopped')
 118         },
 119       })
 120       update(plugin.id, 'registered')
 121     } catch (error) { failed(plugin.id, error) }
 122   }
 123   async function activate(id: string) {
 124     if (states.get(id)?.phase !== 'active') update(id, 'starting')
 125     try { return await registry.activatePlugin(id) }
 126     catch (error) { failed(id, error); throw error }
 127   }
 128   return {
 129     host, failures, activate,
 130     getSnapshot: () => snapshot,
 131     subscribe: (listener: () => void) => { listeners.add(listener); return () => { listeners.delete(listener) } },
 132     deactivate: (id: string) => registry.deactivatePlugin(id),
 133     async start() {
 134       // Lumino still resolves real dependencies. Unrelated autoStart plugins start concurrently.
 135       await Promise.allSettled(plugins.filter(p => p.autoStart === true).map(p => activate(p.id)))
 136     },
 137   }
 138 }

### M:packages/desktop-platform/extension-loader/src/index.ts
   1 import * as api from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   2 import type { Catalog, Diagnostic } from './manifest'
   3 export type { Catalog, Diagnostic, ExtensionDescriptor, Manifest } from './manifest'
   4 type Module = { default?: unknown }
   5 export async function loadExtensions(catalog: Catalog, importModule: (url: string) => Promise<Module> = url => import(/* @vite-ignore */ url), timeoutMs = 5000) {
   6   const plugins: api.Plugin<any>[] = []
   7   const failures: Diagnostic[] = [...catalog.failures]
   8   const results = await Promise.all(catalog.extensions.map(async extension => {
   9     const id = extension.manifest.id
  10     try {
  11       const plugin = await bounded(async () => {
  12         const module = await importModule(extension.url)
  13         if (typeof module.default !== 'function') throw Error('Entry must export a default plugin factory')
  14         // The second argument is this extension's opaque product configuration
  15         // (absent when the product configured nothing); the host does not read it.
  16         const plugin = await module.default(api, extension.config)
  17         if (!plugin || plugin.id !== id || typeof plugin.activate !== 'function') throw Error('Plugin identity or activate is invalid')
  18         return plugin
  19       }, timeoutMs)
  20       return plugin
  21     } catch (error) { failures.push({ id, error: String(error) }) }
  22   }))
  23   plugins.push(...results.filter((p): p is api.Plugin<any> => p !== undefined))
  24   // Refuse all conflicting providers, rather than let directory order choose a winner.
  25   const ids = new Map<string, number>(), providers = new Map<object, number>()
  26   for (const p of plugins) {
  27     ids.set(p.id, (ids.get(p.id) ?? 0) + 1)
  28     if (p.provides) providers.set(p.provides, (providers.get(p.provides) ?? 0) + 1)
  29   }
  30   return {
  31     failures,
  32     plugins: plugins.filter(p => {
  33       if (ids.get(p.id)! > 1 || (p.provides && providers.get(p.provides)! > 1)) {
  34         failures.push({ id: p.id, error: 'Duplicate plugin or service provider' })
  35         return false
  36       }
  37       return true
  38     }),
  39   }
  40 }
  41 
  42 function bounded<T>(operation: () => Promise<T>, timeoutMs: number): Promise<T> {
  43   return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
  44     const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error('Module/factory loading timed out; late result ignored')), timeoutMs)
  45     Promise.resolve().then(operation).then(resolve, reject).finally(() => clearTimeout(timer))
  46   })
  47 }

### M:packages/desktop-platform/extension-host/src/main/extension-protocol.ts
   1 import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises'
   2 import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'
   3 import path from 'node:path'
   4 import { confinedFile, type Discovery } from './extensions'
   5 const mime: Record<string, string> = { '.js': 'text/javascript', '.css': 'text/css', '.json': 'application/json', '.png': 'image/png', '.svg': 'image/svg+xml', '.woff2': 'font/woff2' }
   6 export function protocolHandler(rendererRoot: string, discovery: Discovery) {
   7   const imports: Record<string, string> = {
   8     react: '/shared/react.js', 'react/jsx-runtime': '/shared/jsx-runtime.js',
   9     'react-dom': '/shared/react-dom.js', 'react-dom/client': '/shared/react-dom-client.js',
  10     '@ordessa/extension-api': '/shared/api.js',
  11     '@ordessa/ui-components/api': '/shared/ui-components-api.js',
  12   }
  13   for (const id of discovery.installed.keys()) imports['@extensions/' + id + '/'] = '/extensions/' + id + '/'
  14   return async (request: { url: string; method: string }): Promise<Response> => {
  15     try {
  16       const url = new URL(request.url)
  17       if (request.method !== 'GET' || url.protocol !== 'ordessa:' || url.host !== 'desktop' || url.username || url.password || url.search) return new Response('Denied', { status: 403 })
  18       if (url.pathname === '/' || url.pathname === '/index.html') {
  19         const nonce = randomBytes(18).toString('base64')
  20         const map = JSON.stringify({ imports }).replaceAll('<', '\\u003c')
  21         const html = '<!doctype html><html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><link rel="stylesheet" href="/main.css"><title>Ordessa Desktop</title><script nonce="' + nonce + '" type="importmap">' + map + '</script></head><body><div id="root"></div><script type="module" src="/main.js"></script></body></html>'
  22         return new Response(html, { headers: {
  23           'content-type': 'text/html; charset=utf-8',
  24           'content-security-policy': "default-src 'none'; script-src 'self' 'nonce-" + nonce + "'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; base-uri 'none'; frame-src 'none'; object-src 'none'",
  25         } })
  26       }
  27       const relative = decodeURIComponent(url.pathname.slice(1))
  28       let root = rendererRoot, file = relative
  29       if (relative.startsWith('extensions/')) {
  30         const [, id, ...parts] = relative.split('/')
  31         const extension = discovery.installed.get(id)
  32         if (!extension) return new Response('Not enabled', { status: 403 })
  33         root = extension.root; file = parts.join('/')
  34       }
  35       const contentType = mime[path.extname(file)]
  36       if (!contentType) return new Response('Unsupported file', { status: 415 })
  37       const bytes = await readFile(await confinedFile(root, file))
  38       return new Response(new Uint8Array(bytes), { headers: { 'content-type': contentType, 'x-content-type-options': 'nosniff', 'cache-control': 'no-store' } })
  39     } catch { return new Response('Not found or outside allowed root', { status: 404 }) }
  40   }
  41 }

### M:packages/desktop-platform/connections/src/index.ts
   1 // T027 — @ordessa/connections platform implementation (contract C6 §3/§5).
   2 // Protocol-neutral: registration, kind-identity opens, per-scope handles,
   3 // cancellation, late-resolve cleanup and close settlement. Imports stay behind
   4 // the dependency wall: only @ordessa/extension-api types and relative paths.
   5 import type { IDisposable, ResourceScope } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   6 import type {
   7   CloseFailureRecord,
   8   CloseOutcome,
   9   ConnectionEndpoint,
  10   ConnectionHandle,
  11   ConnectionInstanceSnapshot,
  12   ConnectionKind,
  13   ConnectionOpenOptions,
  14   ConnectionState,
  15   Connections,
  16   ConnectionsSettlementReport,
  17   ConnectionsSnapshot,
  18   Connector,
  19   LateOpenClosure,
  20   RegisteredConnectorSnapshot,
  21   ScopedConnectorRegistration,
  22 } from '../api/connections'
  23 
  24 /** One connector bound to its registrant scope; unmounting the scope removes it (§5.2). */
  25 interface Registration {
  26   readonly connector: Connector<unknown>
  27   readonly owner: ResourceScope
  28   readonly live: Set<InstanceRecord>
  29   removed: boolean
  30 }
  31 
  32 /** Platform record for one open attempt / one connection instance. Never pooled. */
  33 interface InstanceRecord {
  34   readonly instanceId: string
  35   readonly connectorId: string
  36   readonly registration: Registration
  37   readonly listeners: Set<() => void>
  38   state: ConnectionState
  39   error?: string
  40   endpoint?: ConnectionEndpoint<unknown>
  41   pendingClose?: Promise<CloseOutcome>
  42   /** True once the open became undeliverable (caller/registrant unmount, abort, connector failure). */
  43   invalidated: boolean
  44   invalidReason?: string
  45   controller: AbortController
  46   /** Rejects the in-flight open promise when the open is invalidated (§5.2/§5.3). */
  47   failOpen: (error: Error) => void
  48 }
  49 
  50 /** One-line neutral error summary for snapshots/reports; never payloads or credentials. */
  51 function summarize(error: unknown): string {
  52   const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
  53   return message.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 500) || 'Unknown error'
  54 }
  55 
  56 /** Binds an idempotent disposal action to a caller/registrant-owned scope. */
  57 function addDisposer(scope: ResourceScope, action: () => void): IDisposable {
  58   let disposed = false
  59   return scope.add({
  60     get isDisposed() { return disposed },
  61     dispose() { if (disposed) return; disposed = true; action() },
  62   })
  63 }
  64 
  65 /**
  66  * Fixed capability entry (contract §3). The service lifetime is the extension
  67  * scope: disposing it removes every registration and releases live instances;
  68  * the platform itself never owns backend executions (§5.2).
  69  */
  70 export function createConnections(lifetime: ResourceScope): Connections {
  71   const registrations = new Map<string, Registration>()
  72   const records = new Map<string, InstanceRecord>()
  73   const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
  74   const pendingSettlements = new Set<Promise<void>>()
  75   const lateOpenClosures: LateOpenClosure[] = []
  76   const closeFailures: CloseFailureRecord[] = []
  77   let instanceSeq = 0
  78   let serviceClosed = false
  79 
  80   function publish(record?: InstanceRecord): void {
  81     for (const listener of [...listeners]) listener()
  82     if (record) for (const listener of [...record.listeners]) listener()
  83   }
  84 
  85   function track(cleanup: Promise<unknown>): void {
  86     let entry: Promise<void>
  87     entry = Promise.resolve(cleanup).then(() => { pendingSettlements.delete(entry) })
  88     pendingSettlements.add(entry)
  89   }
  90 
  91   function snapshotOf(record: InstanceRecord): ConnectionInstanceSnapshot {
  92     const snapshot: { instanceId: string; connectorId: string; state: ConnectionState; error?: string } = {
  93       instanceId: record.instanceId, connectorId: record.connectorId, state: record.state,
  94     }
  95     if (record.error !== undefined) snapshot.error = record.error
  96     return Object.freeze(snapshot)
  97   }
  98 
  99   // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 100   // §5.4/§5.5 — close ownership: coalescing, honest failure, no auto retry.
 101   // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 102 
 103   /**
 104    * `fromScopeCleanup` marks releases started by a synchronous scope dispose or
 105    * registrant unmount: their failures land in the settlement report because no
 106    * caller is waiting for them (§5.5). An explicit handle.close is reported to
 107    * its awaiter directly and never auto-retried.
 108    */
 109   function beginClose(record: InstanceRecord, fromScopeCleanup: boolean): Promise<CloseOutcome> {
 110     if (record.state === 'closed') return Promise.resolve({ status: 'closed' })
 111     if (record.pendingClose) return record.pendingClose
 112     if (record.state === 'close_failed') {
 113       // Never an automatic loop: cleanup paths join the previous failure;
 114       // only an explicit close() starts another closeLocal (§5.4).
 115       if (fromScopeCleanup) return Promise.resolve({ status: 'close_failed', error: record.error ?? 'Local release failed' })
 116     }
 117     if (!record.endpoint) return Promise.resolve({ status: 'close_failed', error: `No connection endpoint for ${record.connectorId}` })
 118     record.state = 'closing'
 119     record.error = undefined
 120     publish(record)
 121     const outcome: Promise<CloseOutcome> = (async (): Promise<CloseOutcome> => {
 122       try {
 123         await record.endpoint!.closeLocal()
 124         record.state = 'closed'
 125         record.pendingClose = undefined
 126         records.delete(record.instanceId)
 127         record.registration.live.delete(record)
 128         publish(record)
 129         return { status: 'closed' }
 130       } catch (error) {
 131         record.state = 'close_failed'
 132         record.error = summarize(error)
 133         record.pendingClose = undefined
 134         // The record stays visible for host diagnosis (§5.6) but leaves the
 135         // registration's live set so scope teardown never re-calls closeLocal.
 136         record.registration.live.delete(record)
 137         // §5.5 records every failed local release, whoever started it: an
 138         // explicit close reports to its awaiter AND remains in the cumulative
 139         // report, because a rejected closeLocal is never swallowed.
 140         closeFailures.push({ connectorId: record.connectorId, instanceId: record.instanceId, error: record.error })
 141         publish(record)
 142         return { status: 'close_failed', error: record.error }
 143       }
 144     })()
 145     record.pendingClose = outcome
 146     track(outcome)
 147     return outcome
 148   }
 149 
 150   // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 151   // §5.2/§5.3 — invalidation of not-yet-delivered opens and late cleanup.
 152   // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 153 
 154   function invalidateOpening(record: InstanceRecord, reason: string): void {
 155     if (record.invalidated || record.state !== 'opening') return
 156     record.invalidated = true
 157     record.invalidReason = reason
 158     try { record.controller.abort() } catch { /* signal already aborted */ }
 159     record.registration.live.delete(record)
 160     records.delete(record.instanceId)
 161     record.failOpen(new Error(reason))
 162     publish(record)
 163   }
 164 
 165   /** A late/aborted open that still resolved: release exactly once, never surface (§5.3). */
 166   async function closeLateEndpoint(record: InstanceRecord, endpoint: ConnectionEndpoint<unknown>): Promise<void> {
 167     try {
 168       await endpoint.closeLocal()
 169       lateOpenClosures.push({ connectorId: record.connectorId, instanceId: record.instanceId, closed: true })
 170     } catch (error) {
 171       lateOpenClosures.push({ connectorId: record.connectorId, instanceId: record.instanceId, closed: false, error: summarize(error) })
 172     }
 173   }
 174 
 175   function releaseOnCallerScopeGone(record: InstanceRecord): void {
 176     if (record.state === 'opening') invalidateOpening(record, `Caller scope closed during open of connector ${record.connectorId}`)
 177     else if (record.state === 'connected' || record.state === 'closing') {
 178       // §5.2: the platform-owned signal is aborted by the caller scope closing,
 179       // whether or not the open had landed.
 180       record.controller.abort()
 181       void beginClose(record, true)
 182     }
 183     // closed / close_failed: nothing more a scope teardown may start (§5.4).
 184   }
 185 
 186   function releaseOnRegistrantGone(record: InstanceRecord): void {
 187     if (record.state === 'opening') invalidateOpening(record, `Registrant scope for connector ${record.connectorId} unmounted during open`)
 188     else if (record.state === 'connected' || record.state === 'closing') {
 189       record.controller.abort()
 190       void beginClose(record, true)
 191     }
 192   }
 193 
 194   // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 195   // §3 surface.
 196   // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 197 
 198   function removeRegistration(registration: Registration): void {
 199     if (registration.removed) return
 200     registration.removed = true
 201     if (registrations.get(registration.connector.id) === registration) registrations.delete(registration.connector.id)
 202     for (const record of [...registration.live]) releaseOnRegistrantGone(record)
 203     publish()
 204   }
 205 
 206   function forScope(owner: ResourceScope): ScopedConnectorRegistration {
 207     return {
 208       add<T>(connector: Connector<T>): IDisposable {
 209         if (serviceClosed) throw new Error('Connections service is closed')
 210         if (owner.isDisposed) throw new Error(`Owner scope is closed; cannot register connector ${connector.id}`)
 211         if (registrations.has(connector.id)) throw new Error(`Duplicate connector id: ${connector.id}`)
 212         const registration: Registration = { connector: connector as Connector<unknown>, owner, live: new Set(), removed: false }
 213         registrations.set(connector.id, registration)
 214         const link = addDisposer(owner, () => removeRegistration(registration))
 215         publish()
 216         return link
 217       },
 218     }
 219   }
 220 
 221   async function open<T>(
 222     scope: ResourceScope,
 223     id: string,
 224     kind: ConnectionKind<T>,
 225     options?: ConnectionOpenOptions,
 226   ): Promise<ConnectionHandle<T>> {
 227     // ALL gates before connector.open is ever reached (CN-02, §5.1).
 228     if (serviceClosed) throw new Error('Connections service is closed')
 229     if (scope.isDisposed) throw new Error(`Caller scope is closed; cannot open connector ${id}`)
 230     const registration = registrations.get(id)
 231     if (!registration || registration.removed) throw new Error(`No connector registered for id: ${id}`)
 232     if (registration.owner.isDisposed) {
 233       removeRegistration(registration)
 234       throw new Error(`Registrant scope for connector ${id} is closed`)
 235     }
 236     // Identity is the registered kind object compared with ===, never names (§3).
 237     if ((registration.connector.kind as unknown) !== (kind as unknown)) {
 238       throw new Error(`Connection kind mismatch for connector ${id}`)
 239     }
 240     if (options?.signal?.aborted) throw new Error(`Open of connector ${id} was aborted before it started`)
 241 
 242     const record: InstanceRecord = {
 243       instanceId: `${id}#${(instanceSeq += 1)}`,
 244       connectorId: id,
 245       registration,
 246       listeners: new Set(),
 247       state: 'opening',
 248       invalidated: false,
 249       controller: new AbortController(),
 250       failOpen: () => { /* replaced by the guard below */ },
 251     }
 252     const guard = new Promise<never>((_, reject) => { record.failOpen = reject })
 253     addDisposer(scope, () => releaseOnCallerScopeGone(record))
 254     records.set(record.instanceId, record)
 255     registration.live.add(record)
 256     publish(record)
 257 
 258     // Platform-owned signal: aborts on caller abort, caller scope close or registrant unmount.
 259     const external = options?.signal
 260     const onExternalAbort = () => invalidateOpening(record, `Open of connector ${id} aborted by caller signal`)
 261     if (external) external.addEventListener('abort', onExternalAbort, { once: true })
 262 
 263     const opened = registration.connector.open(record.controller.signal)
 264     let endpoint: ConnectionEndpoint<unknown>
 265     try {
 266       endpoint = await Promise.race([opened, guard])
 267     } catch (error) {
 268       if (external) external.removeEventListener('abort', onExternalAbort)
 269       if (!record.invalidated) {
 270         // The connector itself failed: there is no endpoint to release.
 271         record.invalidated = true
 272         record.invalidReason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
 273         try { record.controller.abort() } catch { /* already aborted */ }
 274         record.registration.live.delete(record)
 275         records.delete(record.instanceId)
 276         publish(record)
 277       }
 278       // A resolve that arrives after cancellation must still be cleaned up once (§5.3).
 279       track(opened.then(
 280         late => closeLateEndpoint(record, late),
 281         () => { /* connector rejection already delivered above */ },
 282       ))
 283       throw error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error))
 284     }
 285     if (external) external.removeEventListener('abort', onExternalAbort)
 286     if (record.invalidated) {
 287       // Invalidation and the connector resolve raced in the same turn: the late
 288       // endpoint never enters the usable view and is closed exactly once (§5.3).
 289       track(closeLateEndpoint(record, endpoint))
 290       throw new Error(record.invalidReason ?? `Open of connector ${id} was invalidated before delivery`)
 291     }
 292     record.endpoint = endpoint
 293     record.state = 'connected'
 294     publish(record)
 295 
 296     const handle: ConnectionHandle<T> = {
 297       instanceId: record.instanceId,
 298       connectorId: record.connectorId,
 299       value: endpoint.value as T,
 300       getSnapshot: () => snapshotOf(record),
 301       subscribe: listener => { record.listeners.add(listener); return () => { record.listeners.delete(listener) } },
 302       close: () => beginClose(record, false),
 303     }
 304     return handle
 305   }
 306 
 307   function getSnapshot(): ConnectionsSnapshot {
 308     const connectors: readonly RegisteredConnectorSnapshot[] = Object.freeze(
 309       [...registrations.values()].map(registration => Object.freeze({
 310         id: registration.connector.id,
 311         title: registration.connector.title,
 312         kindName: registration.connector.kind.displayName,
 313       })),
 314     )
 315     const instances: readonly ConnectionInstanceSnapshot[] = Object.freeze([...records.values()].map(snapshotOf))
 316     return Object.freeze({ connectors, instances })
 317   }
 318 
 319   function subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
 320     listeners.add(listener)
 321     return () => { listeners.delete(listener) }
 322   }
 323 
 324   async function whenSettled(): Promise<ConnectionsSettlementReport> {
 325     while (pendingSettlements.size > 0) await Promise.all([...pendingSettlements])
 326     return Object.freeze({
 327       lateOpenClosures: Object.freeze(lateOpenClosures.map(entry => Object.freeze({ ...entry }))),
 328       closeFailures: Object.freeze(closeFailures.map(entry => Object.freeze({ ...entry }))),
 329     })
 330   }
 331 
 332   addDisposer(lifetime, () => {
 333     serviceClosed = true
 334     for (const registration of [...registrations.values()]) removeRegistration(registration)
 335     for (const record of [...records.values()]) {
 336       if (record.state === 'opening') invalidateOpening(record, 'Connections service closed during open')
 337     }
 338     publish()
 339   })
 340 
 341   return { forScope, open, getSnapshot, subscribe, whenSettled }
 342 }
