  24 /** One reported subscriber failure; never carries props, components or bindings. */
  25 export interface UiSubscriberFailure {
  26   readonly kind: 'ui-subscriber-failure'
  27   readonly componentId: string
  28   readonly major: number
  29   readonly message: string
  30   readonly cause: unknown
  31 }
  32 
  33 /** Service options; `onDiagnostics` receives every swallowed subscriber throw. */
  34 export interface UiComponentsServiceOptions {
  35   readonly onDiagnostics?: (failure: UiSubscriberFailure) => void
  36 }
  37 
  38 /** Internal surface relied on by the later assembly task and by the U01–U08 tests. */
  39 export interface UiComponentsService extends IDisposable {
  40   readonly uiComponents: UiComponents
  41   inspectRequirements(): readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[]
  42 }
  43 
  44 /**
  45  * Read-back of the opaque {@link UiRegistration} handle: `registration()` in
  46  * api/ui-components.ts freezes `{ key, providerId, component }` and casts it,
  47  * so the fields are recovered here by cast. The public branded surface is not
  48  * weakened: callers still cannot assemble a UiRegistration by hand.
  49  */
  50 interface RegistrationData {
  51   readonly key: UiComponentKey<any>
  52   readonly providerId: string
  53   readonly component: unknown
  54 }
  55 
  56 function readRegistration(item: UiRegistration): RegistrationData {
  57   return item as unknown as RegistrationData
  58 }
  59 
  60 /** One committed implementation contribution. Removal is guarded by record identity. */
  61 interface RegistrationRecord {
  62   readonly key: UiComponentKey<any>
  63   readonly providerId: string
  64   readonly component: unknown
  65   readonly generation: number
  66 }
  67 
  68 /** Address of one (id, major) slot. Separated by NUL, which typed key ids do not carry. */
  69 function slotKey(id: string, major: number): string {
  70   return `${id}\u0000${major}`
  71 }
  72 
  73 function sameStringArray(a: readonly number[] | undefined, b: readonly number[] | undefined): boolean {
  74   if (a === b) return true
  75   if (a === undefined || b === undefined) return false
  76   return a.length === b.length && a.every((v, i) => v === b[i])
  77 }
  78 
  79 /** Structural equality so unchanged recomputation keeps the old snapshot object (U08). */
  80 function sameSnapshot(a: UiAvailability, b: UiAvailability): boolean {
  81   if (a.status !== b.status) return false
  82   switch (a.status) {
  83     case 'disposed':
  84       return true
  85     case 'ready': {
  86       const other = b as typeof a
  87       return a.providerId === other.providerId && a.generation === other.generation
  88     }
  89     case 'missing': {
  90       const other = b as typeof a
  91       return a.reason === other.reason
  92         && a.selectedProviderId === other.selectedProviderId
  93         && sameStringArray(a.seenMajors, other.seenMajors)
  94     }
  95   }
  96 }
  97 
  98 function sanitizeCause(cause: unknown): string {
  99   if (cause instanceof Error) return cause.message
 100   const text = String(cause)
 101   return text.length > 200 ? `${text.slice(0, 200)}...` : text
 102 }
 103 
 104 const DISPOSED_SNAPSHOT: UiAvailability = Object.freeze({ status: 'disposed' as const })
 105 
 106 /** Consumer-owned, revocable binding (UI-05). Never exposes the component itself. */
 107 class BindingImpl<P> implements UiBinding<P>, IDisposable {
 108   current: UiAvailability
 109   isDisposedFlag = false
 110   readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>()
 111 
 112   /** Symbol-keyed capability for the React layer only; not part of the public UiBinding type. */
 113   readonly [uiBindingInternals]: UiBindingInternals
 114 
 115   constructor(
 116     private readonly service: ServiceCore,
 117     readonly key: UiComponentKey<P>,
 118     readonly required: boolean,
 119   ) {
 120     this.current = service.computeSnapshot(key)
 121     this[uiBindingInternals] = {
 122       required,
 123       resolve: availability => service.resolveComponent(key, availability),
 124     }
 125   }
 126 
 127   get isDisposed(): boolean { return this.isDisposedFlag }
 128 
 129   getSnapshot = (): UiAvailability => this.current
 130 
 131   subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
 132     if (this.isDisposedFlag) return () => undefined
 133     this.listeners.add(listener)
 134     return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener) }
 135   }
 136 
 137   dispose(): void {
 138     if (this.isDisposedFlag) return
 139     this.isDisposedFlag = true
 140     this.service.releaseBinding(this)
 141     this.current = DISPOSED_SNAPSHOT
 142     this.service.notify([this])
 143   }
 144 
 145   /** Service teardown path: same effect as dispose() without re-entering release. */
 146   forceDispose(): void {
 147     if (this.isDisposedFlag) return
 148     this.isDisposedFlag = true
 149     this.current = DISPOSED_SNAPSHOT
 150     this.service.notify([this])
 151   }
 152 }
 153 
 154 /** Handle over one committed record; identity-guarded so an old handle cannot evict a newer record (U05). */
 155 class RecordHandle implements IDisposable {
 156   private released = false
 157   constructor(private readonly service: ServiceCore, private readonly record: RegistrationRecord) {}
 158   get isDisposed(): boolean { return this.released }
 159   dispose(): void {
 160     if (this.released) return
 161     this.released = true
 162     this.service.removeRecord(this.record)
 163   }
 164 }
 165 
 166 /** One-shot handle covering a whole published batch; disposes each member handle. */
 167 class BatchHandle implements IDisposable {
 168   private released = false
 169   constructor(private readonly handles: readonly IDisposable[]) {}
 170   get isDisposed(): boolean { return this.released }
 171   dispose(): void {
 172     if (this.released) return
 173     this.released = true
 174     for (const handle of this.handles) handle.dispose()
 175   }
 176 }
 177 
 178 /** Everything below is per-service state; there is no module-level registry (U07). */
 179 class ServiceCore implements UiComponentsService {
 180   /** (id, major) -> selected providerId, copied and frozen at construction (UI-03). */
 181   private readonly selection = new Map<string, string>()
 182   /** (id, major) -> the one key object accepted by this host; per instance, never global (U04/U07). */
 183   private readonly keyIdentities = new Map<string, UiComponentKey<any>>()
 184   /** (id, major) -> providerId -> record. Different providers coexist (UI-03). */
 185   private readonly slots = new Map<string, Map<string, RegistrationRecord>>()
 186   private readonly bindings = new Set<BindingImpl<any>>()
 187   private generation = 0
 188   private closed = false
 189   isDisposed = false
 190   readonly uiComponents: UiComponents
 191 
 192   constructor(selection: UiSelection, private readonly options?: UiComponentsServiceOptions) {
 193     for (const entry of selection) {
 194       const slot = slotKey(entry.componentId, entry.major)
 195       if (this.selection.has(slot)) {
 196         throw new UiComponentsError('UI_SELECTION_DUPLICATE', `selection has two entries for ${entry.componentId} major ${entry.major}`)
 197       }
 198       this.selection.set(slot, entry.providerId)
 199     }
 200     this.uiComponents = {
 201       forScope: (scope: ResourceScope): UiScope => ({
 202         register: <P>(implementation: UiImplementation<P>): IDisposable =>
 203           this.install(scope, [{
 204             key: implementation.key,
 205             providerId: implementation.providerId,
 206             component: implementation.component,
 207           }]),
 208         registerBatch: (items: readonly UiRegistration[]): IDisposable =>
 209           this.install(scope, items.map(readRegistration)),
 210         bind: <P>(key: UiComponentKey<P>, bindOptions: { required: boolean }): UiBinding<P> =>
 211           this.bind(scope, key, bindOptions),
 212       }),
 213     }
 214   }
 215 
 216   /** Validate the whole new batch, then publish atomically; on failure nothing of it is published (U03). */
 217   private install(scope: ResourceScope, items: readonly RegistrationData[]): IDisposable {
 218     this.assertUsable(scope)
 219     const batchKeys = new Map<string, UiComponentKey<any>>()
 220     const batchProviders = new Set<string>()
 221     for (const item of items) {
 222       if (typeof item.providerId !== 'string' || item.providerId.length === 0) {
 223         throw new RangeError('UI registration requires a providerId')
 224       }
 225       if (!item.component) throw new RangeError('UI registration requires a component')
 226       const slot = slotKey(item.key.id, item.key.major)
 227       const known = this.keyIdentities.get(slot)
 228       if (known !== undefined && known !== item.key) {
 229         throw new UiComponentsError('UI_KEY_IDENTITY_CONFLICT', `${item.key.id} major ${item.key.major} is already represented by another key instance in this host`)
 230       }
 231       const batchKey = batchKeys.get(slot)
 232       if (batchKey !== undefined && batchKey !== item.key) {
 233         throw new UiComponentsError('UI_KEY_IDENTITY_CONFLICT', `batch carries two distinct keys for ${item.key.id} major ${item.key.major}`)
 234       }
 235       batchKeys.set(slot, item.key)
 236       const providerSlot = `${slot}\u0000${item.providerId}`
 237       if (batchProviders.has(providerSlot) || this.slotOf(item.key)?.has(item.providerId)) {
 238         throw new UiComponentsError('UI_PROVIDER_DUPLICATE', `${item.key.id} major ${item.key.major} already has a registration from ${item.providerId}`)
 239       }
 240       batchProviders.add(providerSlot)
 241     }
 242     // Pure validation above touched no state; claim keys and publish now.
 243     const handles: IDisposable[] = []
 244     for (const item of items) {
 245       const slot = slotKey(item.key.id, item.key.major)
 246       if (!this.keyIdentities.has(slot)) this.keyIdentities.set(slot, item.key)
 247       const record: RegistrationRecord = {
 248         key: item.key,
 249         providerId: item.providerId,
 250         component: item.component,
 251         generation: ++this.generation,
 252       }
 253       let providers = this.slots.get(slot)
 254       if (!providers) { providers = new Map(); this.slots.set(slot, providers) }
 255       providers.set(record.providerId, record)
 256       handles.push(new RecordHandle(this, record))
 257     }
 258     this.publish()
 259     const handle = handles.length === 1 ? handles[0] as IDisposable : new BatchHandle(handles)
 260     try {
 261       return scope.add(handle)
 262     } catch (error) {
 263       // Scope closed concurrently with the commit: roll the batch back and report closure.
 264       handle.dispose()
 265       if (scope.isDisposed) {
 266         throw new UiComponentsError('UI_SCOPE_CLOSED', `scope closed while publishing: ${sanitizeCause(error)}`)
 267       }
 268       throw error
 269     }
 270   }
 271 
 272   private bind<P>(scope: ResourceScope, key: UiComponentKey<P>, options: { required: boolean }): UiBinding<P> {
 273     this.assertUsable(scope)
 274     if (typeof options?.required !== 'boolean') throw new RangeError('UI binding requires a boolean `required`')
 275     const slot = slotKey(key.id, key.major)
 276     const known = this.keyIdentities.get(slot)
 277     if (known !== undefined && known !== key) {
 278       throw new UiComponentsError('UI_KEY_IDENTITY_CONFLICT', `${key.id} major ${key.major} is already represented by another key instance in this host`)
 279     }
 280     if (known === undefined) this.keyIdentities.set(slot, key)
 281     const binding = new BindingImpl<P>(this, key, options.required)
 282     try {
 283       scope.add(binding)
 284     } catch (error) {
 285       if (scope.isDisposed) {
 286         throw new UiComponentsError('UI_SCOPE_CLOSED', `scope closed while binding: ${sanitizeCause(error)}`)
 287       }
 288       throw error
 289     }
 290     this.bindings.add(binding)
 291     return binding
 292   }
 293 
 294   /** Closed scope (or disposed service) rejects register/registerBatch/bind synchronously (UI-04). */
 295   private assertUsable(scope: ResourceScope): void {
 296     if (this.closed) throw new UiComponentsError('UI_SCOPE_CLOSED', 'ui-components service is disposed')
 297     if (scope.isDisposed) throw new UiComponentsError('UI_SCOPE_CLOSED', 'resource scope is closed; registration/binding rejected')
 298   }
 299 
 300   private slotOf(key: UiComponentKey<any>): Map<string, RegistrationRecord> | undefined {
 301     return this.slots.get(slotKey(key.id, key.major))
 302   }
 303 
 304   /** Identity-guarded removal: only removes the map entry if it is still this exact record. */
 305   removeRecord(record: RegistrationRecord): void {
 306     const providers = this.slotOf(record.key)
 307     if (providers?.get(record.providerId) === record) {
 308       providers.delete(record.providerId)
 309       if (providers.size === 0) this.slots.delete(slotKey(record.key.id, record.key.major))
 310       this.publish()
 311     }
 312   }
 313 
 314   releaseBinding(binding: BindingImpl<any>): void {
 315     this.bindings.delete(binding)
 316   }
 317 
 318   computeSnapshot(key: UiComponentKey<any>): UiAvailability {
 319     const slot = slotKey(key.id, key.major)
 320     const selected = this.selection.get(slot)
 321     if (selected === undefined) {
 322       return Object.freeze({ status: 'missing' as const, reason: 'unselected' as const })
 323     }
 324     const record = this.slots.get(slot)?.get(selected)
 325     if (record !== undefined) {
 326       return Object.freeze({ status: 'ready' as const, providerId: selected, generation: record.generation })
 327     }
 328     // Selected provider absent. A different major of the same id may be installed — report it (UI-06).
 329     const prefix = `${key.id}\u0000`
 330     const majors = [...this.slots.keys()]
 331       .filter(address => address.startsWith(prefix))
 332       .map(address => Number(address.slice(prefix.length)))
 333       .filter(major => major !== key.major)
 334       .sort((a, b) => a - b)
 335     return Object.freeze(majors.length > 0
 336       ? { status: 'missing' as const, reason: 'provider-unavailable' as const, selectedProviderId: selected, seenMajors: Object.freeze(majors) }
 337       : { status: 'missing' as const, reason: 'provider-unavailable' as const, selectedProviderId: selected })
 338   }
 339 
 340   /** The component behind a ready availability, or `undefined` if the generation no longer matches. */
 341   resolveComponent(key: UiComponentKey<any>, availability: Extract<UiAvailability, { status: 'ready' }>): ComponentType<unknown> | undefined {
 342     const record = this.slots.get(slotKey(key.id, key.major))?.get(availability.providerId)
 343     if (record === undefined || record.generation !== availability.generation) return undefined
 344     return record.component as ComponentType<unknown>
 345   }
 346 
 347   /** Recompute once for every live binding, then notify exactly the changed ones (UI-06, U08). */
 348   private publish(): void {
 349     const changed: BindingImpl<any>[] = []
 350     for (const binding of [...this.bindings]) {
 351       const next = this.computeSnapshot(binding.key)
 352       if (!sameSnapshot(binding.current, next)) {
 353         binding.current = next
 354         changed.push(binding)
 355       }
 356     }
 357     this.notify(changed)
 358   }
 359 
 360   /** A throwing subscriber is reported via onDiagnostics and cannot break the commit nor other subscribers. */
 361   notify(bindings: readonly BindingImpl<any>[]): void {
 362     for (const binding of bindings) {
 363       for (const listener of [...binding.listeners]) {
 364         try {
 365           listener()
 366         } catch (cause) {
 367           const failure: UiSubscriberFailure = Object.freeze({
 368             kind: 'ui-subscriber-failure' as const,
 369             componentId: binding.key.id,
 370             major: binding.key.major,
 371             message: sanitizeCause(cause),
 372             cause,
 373           })
 374           try {
 375             this.options?.onDiagnostics?.(failure)
 376           } catch { /* a throwing diagnostics sink must not break notification either */ }
 377         }
 378       }
 379     }
 380   }
 381 
 382   /** Only currently-missing required bindings (UI-11); never props or components. */
 383   inspectRequirements(): readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[] {
 384     const diagnostics: UiRequirementDiagnostic[] = []
 385     for (const binding of this.bindings) {
 386       const snapshot = binding.current
 387       if (!binding.required || binding.isDisposed || snapshot.status !== 'missing') continue
 388       diagnostics.push(Object.freeze({
 389         componentId: binding.key.id,
 390         major: binding.key.major,
 391         ...(snapshot.selectedProviderId !== undefined ? { selectedProviderId: snapshot.selectedProviderId } : {}),
 392         reason: snapshot.reason,
 393         ...(snapshot.seenMajors !== undefined ? { seenMajors: snapshot.seenMajors } : {}),
 394       }))
 395     }
 396     return Object.freeze(diagnostics)
 397   }
 398 
 399   dispose(): void {
 400     if (this.isDisposed) return
 401     this.isDisposed = true
 402     this.closed = true
 403     this.slots.clear()
 404     this.keyIdentities.clear()
 405     const live = [...this.bindings]
 406     this.bindings.clear()
 407     for (const binding of live) binding.forceDispose()
 408   }
 409 }
 410 
 411 /**
 412  * Assembly entry (contract §3): copies and deep-freezes the product selection,
 413  * validates duplicate (componentId, major), and builds one host-scoped service.
 414  * There is no API to change the selection while running (UI-03, U02).
 415  */
 416 export function createUiComponentsService(
 417   selection: UiSelection,
 418   options?: UiComponentsServiceOptions,
 419 ): UiComponentsService {
 420   const frozen = Object.freeze(selection.map(entry => Object.freeze({
 421     componentId: entry.componentId,
 422     major: entry.major,
 423     providerId: entry.providerId,
 424   })))
 425   return new ServiceCore(frozen, options)
 426 }

### M:packages/workbench/src/model.ts
   1 import type { IDisposable, ResourceScope } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   2 import type {
   3   Workbench, WorkbenchComposition, WorkbenchModule, WorkbenchOverlay, WorkbenchOverlayOpenOptions,
   4   WorkbenchSettingsSection, View, UIContribution, Region,
   5 } from '@extensions/ordessa.contracts/contract.js'
   6 import { registry } from '../shared/registry'
   7 
   8 /** The settings page is Workbench-owned; it is not a contribution of any plugin. */
   9 export const SETTINGS_OVERLAY_ID = 'ordessa.workbench.settings'
  10 
  11 export type OverlayPresentation = 'popover' | 'dialog' | 'page'
  12 export interface OverlayInstance {
  13   key: number
  14   overlayId: string
  15   anchor?: HTMLElement
  16   /** Settings page only: the section to locate. */
  17   sectionId?: string
  18 }
  19 
  20 export function createWorkbench(lifetime: ResourceScope) {
  21   const views = registry<View>(lifetime), ui = registry<UIContribution>(lifetime)
  22   const modules = registry<WorkbenchModule>(lifetime)
  23   const overlays = registry<WorkbenchOverlay>(lifetime)
  24   const sections = registry<WorkbenchSettingsSection>(lifetime)
  25   type Selection = Partial<Record<Region | 'full-page', string>>
  26   let selected: Selection = {}
  27   let layout: { placements: Record<string, Region>; collapsed: Partial<Record<Region, boolean>> } = { placements: {}, collapsed: {} }
  28   let overlayStack: readonly OverlayInstance[] = []
  29   let nextInstance = 1
  30   const regionOf = (view: View) => view.presentation === 'region' ? layout.placements[view.id] ?? view.region : undefined
  31   const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
  32   const publish = () => listeners.forEach(f => f())
  33   const unsubscribe = views.subscribe(() => {
  34     const ids = new Set(views.getSnapshot().map(v => v.id))
  35     selected = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(selected).filter(([, id]) => ids.has(id!)))
  36     layout = { ...layout, placements: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(layout.placements).filter(([id]) => ids.has(id))) }
  37     publish()
  38   })
  39   // Unregistered modules lose their navigation entry and any selection that
  40   // referenced their views; modules that remain keep their selection.
  41   let claimedViews = new Set<string>()
  42   const unsubscribeModules = modules.subscribe(() => {
  43     const next = new Set<string>()
  44     for (const m of modules.getSnapshot()) { next.add(m.homeViewId); if (m.sidebarViewId) next.add(m.sidebarViewId) }
  45     const dropped = [...claimedViews].filter(id => !next.has(id))
  46     claimedViews = next
  47     if (!dropped.length) return
  48     const gone = new Set(dropped)
  49     selected = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(selected).filter(([, id]) => !gone.has(id)))
  50     publish()
  51   })
  52   // Unregistered overlays close immediately; the shell restores focus on unmount.
  53   const unsubscribeOverlays = overlays.subscribe(() => {
  54     const ids = new Set(overlays.getSnapshot().map(o => o.id))
  55     const kept = overlayStack.filter(i => i.overlayId === SETTINGS_OVERLAY_ID || ids.has(i.overlayId))
  56     if (kept.length === overlayStack.length) return
  57     overlayStack = kept
  58     publish()
  59   })
  60   lifetime.add({ isDisposed: false, dispose() { unsubscribe(); unsubscribeModules(); unsubscribeOverlays(); listeners.clear() } })
  61   const activateModule = (id: string) => {
  62     if (lifetime.isDisposed) throw Error('Workbench is closed')
  63     const module = modules.getSnapshot().find(m => m.id === id)
  64     if (!module) throw Error(`Module unavailable: ${id}`)
  65     // Both bindings are verified before any selection changes: an absent or
  66     // misplaced view is a visible error, never a fallback to another view.
  67     const snapshot = views.getSnapshot()
  68     const check = (viewId: string, region: Region, role: string): View => {
  69       const view = snapshot.find(v => v.id === viewId)
  70       if (!view) throw Error(`Module "${id}" ${role} view is not registered: ${viewId}`)
  71       if (view.presentation !== 'region' || regionOf(view) !== region) throw Error(`Module "${id}" ${role} view must be a ${region}-region view: ${viewId}`)
  72       return view
  73     }
  74     const home = check(module.homeViewId, 'main', 'home')
  75     const sidebar = module.sidebarViewId === undefined ? undefined : check(module.sidebarViewId, 'left', 'sidebar')
  76     const next = { ...selected, main: home.id }
  77     // A module sidebar never outlives its module: a sidebar-less activation clears any
  78     // registered module's sidebar, while a user-opened non-module view keeps its place.
  79     if (sidebar) next.left = sidebar.id
  80     else if (next.left !== undefined && modules.getSnapshot().some(m => m.sidebarViewId === next.left)) delete next.left
  81     selected = next
  82     if (sidebar) layout = { ...layout, collapsed: { ...layout.collapsed, left: false } }
  83     publish()
  84   }
  85   const closeInstance = (key: number) => {
  86     if (!overlayStack.some(i => i.key === key)) return
  87     overlayStack = overlayStack.filter(i => i.key !== key)
  88     publish()
  89   }
  90   const openOverlay = (id: string, options?: WorkbenchOverlayOpenOptions): IDisposable => {
  91     if (lifetime.isDisposed) throw Error('Workbench is closed')
  92     const overlay = overlays.getSnapshot().find(o => o.id === id)
  93     if (!overlay) throw Error(`Overlay unavailable: ${id}`)
  94     if (overlay.presentation !== 'popover' && overlay.presentation !== 'dialog' && overlay.presentation !== 'page') throw Error(`Invalid overlay presentation: ${String(overlay.presentation)}`)
  95     const anchor = options?.anchor
  96     // A popover without a live anchor fails explicitly instead of silently not showing.
  97     if (overlay.presentation === 'popover' && (!anchor || !anchor.isConnected)) throw Error(`Popover requires a connected anchor: ${id}`)
  98     const key = nextInstance++
  99     overlayStack = [...overlayStack, { key, overlayId: id, anchor }]
 100     publish()
 101     // isDisposed reads the stack: every close path (dispose, Escape, contributor
 102     // close, contributor unload) flips it, and a late dispose is a harmless no-op.
 103     return {
 104       get isDisposed() { return !overlayStack.some(i => i.key === key) },
 105       dispose() { closeInstance(key) },
 106     }
 107   }
 108   const openSettings = (sectionId?: string) => {
 109     if (lifetime.isDisposed) throw Error('Workbench is closed')
 110     if (sectionId !== undefined && !sections.getSnapshot().some(s => s.id === sectionId)) throw Error(`Settings section unavailable: ${sectionId}`)
 111     const existing = overlayStack.find(i => i.overlayId === SETTINGS_OVERLAY_ID)
 112     const entry: OverlayInstance = { key: existing?.key ?? nextInstance++, overlayId: SETTINGS_OVERLAY_ID, sectionId }
 113     overlayStack = [...overlayStack.filter(i => i !== existing), entry]
 114     publish()
 115   }
 116   const closeTopOverlay = () => {
 117     const top = overlayStack[overlayStack.length - 1]
 118     if (top) closeInstance(top.key)
 119   }
 120   const composition: WorkbenchComposition = {
 121     forScope: scope => ({
 122       addModule: module => modules.add(scope, module),
 123       addOverlay: overlay => overlays.add(scope, overlay),
 124       addSettingsSection: section => sections.add(scope, section),
 125     }),
 126     activateModule,
 127     openOverlay,
 128     openSettings,
 129   }
 130   const service: Workbench = {
 131     forScope: scope => ({
 132       addView: view => {
 133         if (view.presentation !== 'full-page' && (view.presentation !== 'region' || !['left', 'right', 'bottom', 'main', 'top'].includes(view.region))) throw Error('Invalid view placement')
 134         return views.add(scope, view)
 135       },
 136       addUI: item => {
 137         if (!['navigation', 'toolbar', 'statusbar'].includes(item.slot) || !['component', 'command'].includes(item.kind) || (item.kind === 'component' && item.slot !== 'statusbar')) throw Error('Invalid UI contribution')
 138         return ui.add(scope, item)
 139       },
 140     }),
 141     composition,
 142     open(id) {
 143       if (lifetime.isDisposed) throw Error('Workbench is closed')
 144       const view = views.getSnapshot().find(v => v.id === id)
 145       if (!view) throw Error(`View unavailable: ${id}`)
 146       const target = view.presentation === 'full-page' ? 'full-page' : regionOf(view)!
 147       selected = { ...selected, [target]: id }
 148       if (target !== 'full-page') layout = { ...layout, collapsed: { ...layout.collapsed, [target]: false } }
 149       publish()
 150     },
 151     close(id) { selected = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(selected).filter(([, value]) => value !== id)); publish() },
 152   }
 153   return { service, views, ui, modules, overlays, sections, composition, regionOf, getSelection: () => selected,
 154     getOverlayStack: () => overlayStack, closeOverlayInstance: closeInstance, closeTopOverlay,
 155     getLayout: () => layout,
 156     collapse(region: Region, collapsed: boolean) {
 157       if (lifetime.isDisposed || region === 'main' || layout.collapsed[region] === collapsed) return
 158       layout = { ...layout, collapsed: { ...layout.collapsed, [region]: collapsed } }; publish()
 159     },
 160     move(id: string, region: Region) {
 161       if (lifetime.isDisposed) throw Error('Workbench is closed')
 162       const view = views.getSnapshot().find(v => v.id === id)
 163       if (!view || view.presentation !== 'region' || !['left', 'right', 'bottom', 'main', 'top'].includes(region)) throw Error('Invalid view move')
 164       const from = regionOf(view)!
 165       if (from === region) { service.open(id); return }
 166       selected = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(selected).filter(([, value]) => value !== id))
 167       layout = { placements: { ...layout.placements, [id]: region }, collapsed: { ...layout.collapsed, [region]: false } }
 168       const replacement = views.getSnapshot().find(v => v.id !== id && regionOf(v) === from)
 169       if (!selected[from] && replacement) selected[from] = replacement.id
 170       selected = { ...selected, [region]: id }; publish()
 171     },
 172     resetLayout() {
 173       if (lifetime.isDisposed) return
 174       layout = { placements: {}, collapsed: {} }
 175       selected = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(selected).filter(([key]) => key === 'full-page'))
 176       for (const view of views.getSnapshot()) if (view.presentation === 'region' && !selected[view.region]) selected[view.region] = view.id
 177       publish()
 178     },
 179     subscribe: (f: () => void) => { listeners.add(f); return () => { listeners.delete(f) } },
 180   }
 181 }
 182 export type WorkbenchModel = ReturnType<typeof createWorkbench>

### M:packages/workbench/api/workbench.ts
   1 import { Token, type ResourceScope, type IDisposable } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   2 import type { ComponentType } from 'react'
   3 export type Region = 'left' | 'right' | 'bottom' | 'main' | 'top'
   4 interface ViewBase { id: string; title: string; order?: number; component: ComponentType }
   5 export type View = ViewBase & ({ presentation: 'region'; region: Region } | { presentation: 'full-page' })
   6 
   7 /** A product module owns its navigation entry and its default workspace views. */
   8 export interface WorkbenchModule {
   9   id: string
  10   title: string
  11   order?: number
  12   icon?: ComponentType
  13   homeViewId: string
  14   sidebarViewId?: string
  15 }
  16 
  17 /** Overlay content is owned by its contributor; the Workbench owns dismissal. */
  18 export interface WorkbenchOverlayContentProps { close(): void }
  19 export type WorkbenchOverlay = { id: string; title: string; order?: number; component: ComponentType<WorkbenchOverlayContentProps> } & (
  20   { presentation: 'popover' } |
  21   { presentation: 'dialog' | 'page' }
  22 )
  23 export type WorkbenchOverlayOpenOptions = { anchor?: HTMLElement }
  24 
  25 export interface WorkbenchSettingsSection {
  26   id: string
  27   title: string
  28   order?: number
  29   component: ComponentType
  30 }
  31 
  32 /**
  33  * Versioned composition capability. Its presence means the whole registration
  34  * surface is implemented; consumers must reject absence rather than silently
  35  * substituting legacy navigation or view IDs. Registrations are scope-owned.
  36  */
  37 export interface WorkbenchComposition {
  38   forScope(scope: ResourceScope): {
  39     addModule(module: WorkbenchModule): IDisposable
  40     addOverlay(overlay: WorkbenchOverlay): IDisposable
  41     addSettingsSection(section: WorkbenchSettingsSection): IDisposable
  42   }
  43   activateModule(id: string): void
  44   openOverlay(id: string, options?: WorkbenchOverlayOpenOptions): IDisposable
  45   openSettings(sectionId?: string): void
  46 }
  47 
  48 export type UIContribution = { id: string; order?: number } & (
  49   { kind: 'command'; slot: 'navigation' | 'toolbar' | 'statusbar'; command: string; label?: string; section?: 'primary' | 'utility'; icon?: ComponentType } |
  50   { kind: 'component'; slot: 'statusbar'; component: ComponentType }
  51 )
  52 export interface Workbench {
  53   forScope(scope: ResourceScope): { addView(view: View): IDisposable; addUI(item: UIContribution): IDisposable }
  54   open(id: string): void
  55   close(id: string): void
  56   /** Absent in the baseline Workbench; required by modules using composition. */
  57   readonly composition?: WorkbenchComposition
  58 }
  59 /** The single construction site of the Workbench DI token in this repository (contract C4). */
  60 export const WorkbenchToken = new Token<Workbench>('ordessa.workbench.v1')

### M:apps/desktop/renderer/main.tsx
   1 import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
   2 import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client'
   3 import { runtime } from '@ordessa/extension-host'
   4 import type { UiRequirementDiagnostic } from '@ordessa/ui-components/api'
   5 import type { CommandOutcome, Fault } from '@extensions/ordessa.contracts/contract.js'
   6 import { App } from './app'
   7 import { WindowChrome } from './window-chrome'
   8 import { installedExtensions } from './extensions'
   9 import { buildHostServices, listPalette, type DesktopHostBridge } from './host-services'
  10 import { AboutPanel, CommandPalette, FaultScreen, SettingsPage, type AboutInfo } from './desktop-ui'
  11 import './host.css'
  12 // Foundations styles are loaded explicitly by the assembly (C8 F05): importing the
  13 // package itself injects nothing, so a product that does not want them has none.
  14 import '@ordessa/ui/styles.css'
  15 const root = createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!)
  16 const empty = runtime([])
  17 root.render(<><WindowChrome /><App host={empty.host} /></>)
  18 const bridge = (): DesktopHostBridge | undefined => window.desktopHost
  19 // A UI assembly built by the product reports its own missing required components;
  20 // the host only asks for that generic optional capability, it knows no component name.
  21 function requirementsOf(plugins: unknown[]): readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[] {
  22   return plugins.flatMap(plugin => typeof (plugin as { inspectRequirements?: () => readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[] }).inspectRequirements === 'function'
  23     ? (plugin as { inspectRequirements: () => readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[] }).inspectRequirements() : [])
  24 }
  25 function Desktop({ desktop, failures, requirements }: {
  26   desktop: ReturnType<typeof runtime>, failures: {id: string; error: string}[], requirements: readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[]
  27 }) {
  28   const states = useSyncExternalStore(desktop.subscribe, desktop.getSnapshot)
  29   return <>
  30     <WindowChrome />
  31     {failures.map((failure, index) => <p role="alert" key={index}>{failure.id}: {failure.error}</p>)}
  32     {requirements.map((requirement) => <p role="alert" key={`${requirement.componentId}/${requirement.major}`}>
  33       required ui component unavailable: {requirement.componentId} major {requirement.major}
  34       {requirement.selectedProviderId ? ` (selected ${requirement.selectedProviderId})` : ' (not selected)'}
  35     </p>)}
  36     {states.filter(s => s.phase === 'failed').map(s => <p role="alert" key={s.id}>{s.id}: {s.error}</p>)}
  37     {states.filter(s => s.phase === 'starting').map(s => <p role="status" key={s.id}>{s.id}: 启动中</p>)}
  38     <App host={desktop.host} />
  39   </>
  40 }
  41 
  42 /**
  43  * 宿主外壳：故障屏优先（PA-12）；设置/关于/命令面板都是**按需打开的浮层**，
  44  * 默认不进入 DOM——产品的页面内容不能被宿主 chrome 污染，既有页面的文本契约保持不变。
  45  */
  46 function HostShell({ desktop, about, services, failures, requirements }: {
  47   desktop: ReturnType<typeof runtime>
  48   about: AboutInfo | null
  49   services: ReturnType<typeof buildHostServices>
  50   failures: { id: string; error: string }[]
  51   requirements: readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[]
  52 }) {
  53   const [faults, setFaults] = useState<readonly Fault[]>([])
  54   const [overlay, setOverlay] = useState<'palette' | 'settings' | 'about' | null>(null)
  55   const [outcome, setOutcome] = useState<CommandOutcome | null>(null)
  56   const [updateState, setUpdateState] = useState<{ state: string; version?: string; reason?: string; received?: number; total?: number }>({ state: 'idle' })
  57   useEffect(() => {
  58     const host = bridge()
  59     if (!host) return
  60     // 宿主能力缺席是**正常**路径（README §3）：探测失败按"无故障"处理，不拖垮启动。
  61     void host.faults().then(raw => setFaults((raw as Fault[]).filter(fault => typeof fault?.reason === 'string'))).catch(() => setFaults([]))
  62   }, [])
  63   const entries = useMemo(() => listPalette(services.commands, services.keybindings, { hostReady: true }), [services])
  64   const run = useCallback(async (id: string) => { setOutcome(await services.commands.execute(id)) }, [services])
  65   // 键盘可达（PA-20）：Mod+Shift+P 打开命令面板，Esc 关闭。
  66   useEffect(() => {
  67     const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
  68       if ((event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey) && event.shiftKey && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'p') {
  69         event.preventDefault()
  70         setOverlay(current => (current === 'palette' ? null : 'palette'))
  71       } else if (event.key === 'Escape') setOverlay(null)
  72     }
  73     window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  74     return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  75   }, [])
  76   return <>
  77     <Desktop desktop={desktop} failures={failures} requirements={requirements} />
  78     <FaultScreen faults={faults} onExportDiagnostics={() => void bridge()?.exportDiagnostics()} onOpenLogs={() => void bridge()?.openLogs()} />
  79     <nav className="od-host-chrome" aria-label="宿主入口">
  80       <button type="button" data-testid="open-palette" onClick={() => setOverlay('palette')}>命令面板</button>
  81       <button type="button" data-testid="open-settings" onClick={() => setOverlay('settings')}>设置</button>
  82       <button type="button" data-testid="open-about" onClick={() => setOverlay('about')}>关于</button>
  83     </nav>
  84     {overlay === 'palette'
  85       ? <CommandPalette open entries={entries} outcome={outcome} onRun={run} onClose={() => setOverlay(null)} />
  86       : null}
  87     {overlay === 'settings'
  88       ? <SettingsPage dataRoot="…" logLevel="info" updateState={updateState}
  89         onLogLevel={() => undefined} onOpenLogs={() => void bridge()?.openLogs()}
  90         onExportDiagnostics={() => void bridge()?.exportDiagnostics()}
  91         onCheckUpdate={() => void bridge()?.checkUpdate().then(state => setUpdateState(state as typeof updateState))}
  92         onDownloadUpdate={() => void bridge()?.downloadUpdate().then(state => setUpdateState(state as typeof updateState))}
  93         onApplyUpdate={() => void bridge()?.applyUpdate().then(state => setUpdateState(state as typeof updateState))}
  94         contributed={services.settings.getSnapshot()} onClose={() => setOverlay(null)} />
  95       : null}
  96     {overlay === 'about' && about ? <AboutPanel about={about} onOpenLicense={() => void bridge()?.openLicense()} /> : null}
  97   </>
  98 }
  99 async function start() {
 100   const host = bridge()
 101   const services = buildHostServices({ bridge: host })
 102   const about = host ? await host.about().catch(() => null) as AboutInfo | null : null
 103   const loaded = await installedExtensions()
 104   const desktop = runtime(loaded.plugins, services)
 105   const shell = (requirements: readonly UiRequirementDiagnostic[]) =>
 106     <HostShell desktop={desktop} about={about} services={services} failures={loaded.failures} requirements={requirements} />
 107   root.render(shell([]))
 108   document.documentElement.dataset.ready = 'true'
 109   await desktop.start().catch(() => undefined)
 110   // Required-component reporting runs after activation, once (contract C7 §3).
 111   root.render(shell(requirementsOf(loaded.plugins)))
 112   document.documentElement.dataset.settled = 'true'
 113 }
 114 void start().catch(error => root.render(<><p role="alert">Startup failed: {String(error)}</p><App host={empty.host} /></>))

### M:apps/desktop/renderer/host-services.ts
   1 /**
   2  * 渲染进程的平台服务装配（PA-16/19/21/22 的接线点）。
   3  *
   4  * 关键约束：
   5  *   · 令牌只在主进程：这里的 wire 只是一层 IPC 代理，插件与界面拿不到令牌字段；
   6  *   · 日志服务不直接写盘：渲染进程把记录交给主进程 sink 脱敏，避免渲染侧另起一套格式；
   7  *   · 缺席的服务用**缺席实现**（C-03 §6 / C-05 §5 / C-08 §4），不返回 null 让界面崩。
   8  */
   9 import { createAbsentHarnessAvailability, createCommandSource, createKeybindingService, createThemeService, listPalette, type AbsentHarnessAvailability, type CommandSourceHost, type KeybindingServiceHost, type ThemeService } from '@ordessa/extension-host/platform'
  10 import type { CommandOutcome, DiagnosticProvider, Logger, SettingsSection, WirePort, WireResult } from '@extensions/ordessa.contracts/contract.js'
  11 import { NEUTRAL_TOKENS, createNeutralThemeService } from '@ordessa/extension-host/platform'
  12 
  13 /** preload 暴露的宿主桥（见 electron/preload.ts）。 */
  14 export interface DesktopHostBridge {
  15   about(): Promise<unknown>
  16   openLicense(): Promise<boolean>
  17   faults(): Promise<unknown[]>
  18   dataRoot(): Promise<{ dataRoot: string; logsDir: string }>
  19   openLogs(): Promise<boolean>

  21   writeSetting(id: string, value: unknown): Promise<unknown>
  22   settingFragments(known: string[]): Promise<unknown[]>
  23   setLogLevel(level: string): Promise<string>
  24   logHealth(): Promise<{ dropped: number; writable: boolean }>
  25   exportDiagnostics(): Promise<unknown>
  26   wireCall(method: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<unknown>
  27   wireReady(): Promise<boolean>
  28   wireScope(): Promise<string | null>

  31   downloadUpdate(): Promise<unknown>

  35 declare global {
  36   interface Window {
  37     desktopHost?: DesktopHostBridge
  38     __ordessaTheme?: { resolved(): 'light' | 'dark'; subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void }
  39   }
  40 }
  41 
  42 /** 渲染进程日志：结构与主进程同构，脱敏仍由主进程 sink 强制。 */
  43 export function createBridgeLogger(sink: (level: 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error', msg: string, fields?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>) => void, scope = 'host.desktop'): Logger {
  44   const make = (prefix: string): Logger => ({
  45     debug: (msg, fields) => sink('debug', msg, { ...fields, scope: prefix }),
  46     info: (msg, fields) => sink('info', msg, { ...fields, scope: prefix }),
  47     warn: (msg, fields) => sink('warn', msg, { ...fields, scope: prefix }),
  48     error: (msg, fields) => sink('error', msg, { ...fields, scope: prefix }),
  49     child: child => make(`${prefix}.${child}`),
  50   })
  51   return make(scope)
  52 }
  53 
  54 /** C-03 §4：渲染侧只做代理，任何异常都降级为 `Unknown`，不冒泡到界面。 */
  55 export function createBridgeWirePort(host: DesktopHostBridge | undefined): WirePort {
  56   if (!host) return { ready: false, scope: null, call: async () => ({ kind: 'Unknown', requestId: 'absent', reason: 'port-absent' }) as WireResult }
  57   return {
  58     ready: true,
  59     scope: null,
  60     async call(method, params) {
  61       try { return await host.wireCall(method, params) as WireResult }
  62       catch { return { kind: 'Unknown', requestId: 'local', reason: 'transport-unreachable' } }
  63     },
  64   }
  65 }
  66 
  67 export interface HostServices {
  68   theme: ThemeService
  69   commands: CommandSourceHost
  70   keybindings: KeybindingServiceHost
  71   wire: WirePort
  72   logger: Logger
  73   settings: { register(section: SettingsSection): () => void; getSnapshot(): readonly SettingsSection[] }
  74   diagnostics: { register(provider: DiagnosticProvider): () => void; getSnapshot(): readonly DiagnosticProvider[] }
  75   /** C-08：插件线交付 Harness 提供者之前，宿主注入**缺席实现**（诚实缺席，不假绿）。 */
  76   harness: AbsentHarnessAvailability
  77 }
  78 
  79 export interface BuildHostServicesOptions {
  80   bridge?: DesktopHostBridge
  81   mode?: 'light' | 'dark' | 'system'
  82   prefersDark?: () => boolean
  83   onCommandError?: (commandId: string, error: unknown) => void
  84   /** C-08：插件提供者的实现由后续插件线注入；缺省即缺席实现。 */
  85   harness?: AbsentHarnessAvailability
  86 }
  87 
  88 /** 一次装配：主题、命令、快捷键、wire、日志、设置分区与诊断片段的登记点。 */
  89 export function buildHostServices(options: BuildHostServicesOptions = {}): HostServices {
  90   const bridge = options.bridge
  91   const theme = options.mode ? createThemeService({ mode: options.mode, prefersDark: options.prefersDark }) : createNeutralThemeService()
  92   const keybindings = createKeybindingService()
  93   const commands = createCommandSource({ keybindings, onError: options.onCommandError })
  94   const sections: SettingsSection[] = []
  95   const providers: DiagnosticProvider[] = []
  96   return {
  97     theme,
  98     commands,
  99     keybindings,
 100     harness: options.harness ?? createAbsentHarnessAvailability(),
 101     wire: createBridgeWirePort(bridge),
 102     logger: createBridgeLogger(() => { /* 渲染侧日志在构建期接主进程 sink；未接时静默丢弃而非阻塞 */ }),
 103     settings: {
 104       register(section) {
 105         sections.push(section)
 106         return () => { const index = sections.indexOf(section); if (index >= 0) sections.splice(index, 1) }
 107       },
 108       getSnapshot: () => [...sections],
 109     },
 110     diagnostics: {
 111       register(provider) {
 112         providers.push(provider)
 113         return () => { const index = providers.indexOf(provider); if (index >= 0) providers.splice(index, 1) }
 114       },
 115       getSnapshot: () => [...providers],
 116     },
 117   }
 118 }

   1 /**

  14 export interface CallerFacts {
  15   /** 事件发送方是否就是主窗口的 webContents。 */

### M:packages/desktop-platform/native-bridge/src/index.ts
   1 import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto'
   2 import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'
   3 import type { BrowserWindow } from 'electron'
   4 import { ipcMain } from 'electron'
   5 import { confinedFile, type Discovery } from '@ordessa/extension-host/main'
   6 
   7 export interface NativeConnection {
   8   send(frame: unknown): Promise<unknown>
   9   close(): Promise<void>
  10 }
  11 export interface NativeTransport {
  12   open(onFrame: (frame: unknown) => void): Promise<NativeConnection>
  13 }
  14 
  15 /** Only enabled extensions with a confined native entry can provide a transport. */
  16 export function installNativeBridge(win: BrowserWindow, discovery: Discovery) {
  17   const live = new Map<string, { adapterId: string; connection: NativeConnection }>()
  18   let disposed = false
  19   const trusted = (event: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent) => {
  20     if (disposed || event.sender !== win.webContents || event.senderFrame !== win.webContents.mainFrame ||
  21         event.senderFrame.url !== 'ordessa://desktop/index.html') throw Error('Untrusted native transport caller')
  22   }
  23   const validateFrame = (frame: unknown) => {
  24     const serialized = JSON.stringify(frame)
  25     if (typeof serialized !== 'string' || serialized.length > 1024 * 1024) throw Error('Invalid native transport frame')
  26   }
  27   const open = async (event: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent, adapterId: string) => {
  28     trusted(event)
  29     if (typeof adapterId !== 'string') throw Error('Invalid adapter')
  30     const installed = discovery.installed.get(adapterId)
  31     if (!installed?.manifest.native) throw Error('Native adapter unavailable')
  32     const filename = await confinedFile(installed.root, installed.manifest.native)
  33     const module = await import(pathToFileURL(filename).href) as { default?: () => NativeTransport }
  34     if (typeof module.default !== 'function') throw Error('Invalid native adapter entry')
  35     const transport = module.default()
  36     if (!transport || typeof transport.open !== 'function') throw Error('Invalid native transport')
  37     const instanceId = randomUUID()
  38     const early: unknown[] = []
  39     const deliver = (frame: unknown) => {
  40       if (disposed || win.isDestroyed()) return
  41       try {
  42         validateFrame(frame)
  43         if (!live.has(instanceId)) {
  44           if (early.length >= 256) throw Error('Native transport startup buffer exceeded')
  45           early.push(frame)
  46           return
  47         }
  48         win.webContents.send('agent-native:event', { instanceId, frame })
  49       } catch { win.webContents.send('agent-native:event', { instanceId, error: 'Invalid native transport frame' }) }
  50     }
  51     const connection = await transport.open(deliver)
  52     if (disposed) { await connection.close(); throw Error('Native bridge closed') }
  53     live.set(instanceId, { adapterId, connection })
  54     for (const frame of early) deliver(frame)
  55     return instanceId
  56   }
  57   const send = async (event: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent, instanceId: string, frame: unknown) => {
  58     trusted(event)
  59     validateFrame(frame)
  60     const item = live.get(instanceId)
  61     if (!item) throw Error('Native instance unavailable')
  62     const result = await item.connection.send(frame)
  63     if (result !== undefined) validateFrame(result)
  64     return result
  65   }
  66   const close = async (event: Electron.IpcMainInvokeEvent, instanceId: string) => {
  67     trusted(event)
  68     const item = live.get(instanceId)
  69     if (!item) return
  70     live.delete(instanceId)
  71     await item.connection.close()
  72   }
  73   ipcMain.handle('agent-native:open', open)
  74   ipcMain.handle('agent-native:send', send)
  75   ipcMain.handle('agent-native:close', close)
  76   const dispose = async () => {
  77     if (disposed) return
  78     disposed = true
  79     ipcMain.removeHandler('agent-native:open')
  80     ipcMain.removeHandler('agent-native:send')
  81     ipcMain.removeHandler('agent-native:close')
  82     const pending = [...live.values()]
  83     live.clear()
  84     await Promise.allSettled(pending.map(item => item.connection.close()))
  85   }
  86   win.once('closed', () => { void dispose() })
  87   return { dispose }
  88 }

### M:plugins/connectors/acp/src/host.ts
   1 import type * as acp from '@agentclientprotocol/sdk'
   2 import type { AgentWorkspaceInfo } from '@extensions/ordessa.agent-contracts/contract.js'
   3 import type { AgentNativeBridge } from '../../../../apps/desktop/renderer/agent-native'
   4 import type { AcpChannelHandle, AcpChannelSpec } from './channel'
   5 import type { AcpControlledSubmission, AcpPermissionDecision, AcpSubmissionAdmission } from './submission'
   6 
   7 /**
   8  * The renderer half of the desktop wiring: the ONLY module in this plugin that speaks the
   9  * `agentNative` bridge frames. It turns one orchestration-issued Server|Harness pair into the
  10  * same `AcpChannelSpec` the reviewed tests inject, so the production path and the target tests
  11  * drive one and the same client. Frame names here are this plugin's internal host contract; the
  12  * real Server seam (`acp.channel.open`, `workspaces.*`, `/wire/v1/acp-channel/{connectionId}`) is
  13  * owned entirely by native.ts, and a missing Server API surfaces as a refused frame, never as a
  14  * silently invented channel.
  15  */
  16 
  17 const VALUE = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | undefined =>
  18   value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Record<string, unknown> : undefined
  19 const STR = (value: unknown): string | undefined => typeof value === 'string' && value ? value : undefined
  20 const unwrap = (error: unknown): string =>
  21   (error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)).replace(/^Error invoking remote method '[^']+':\s*(?:Error:\s*)?/, '')
  22 
  23 export interface AcpOrchestrationEntry {
  24   /** Orchestration-issued identity for this Server|Harness pair (never a plugin id). */
  25   serverInstanceId: string
  26   harness: { id: string; title: string }
  27 }
  28 export interface AcpOrchestration {
  29   /** False means this Server does not offer the ACP channel API yet — the honest no-op state. */
  30   offered: boolean
  31   entries: AcpOrchestrationEntry[]
  32 }
  33 
  34 export function parseAcpIdentity(value: unknown): AcpOrchestration {
  35   const raw = VALUE(value)
  36   if (typeof raw?.offered !== 'boolean') throw new Error('ACP orchestration identity is incomplete')
  37   if (!raw.offered) return { offered: false, entries: [] }
  38   if (!Array.isArray(raw.entries)) throw new Error('ACP orchestration identity has no entries')
  39   const entries = raw.entries.map(item => {
  40     const entry = VALUE(item)
  41     const serverInstanceId = STR(entry?.serverInstanceId), harness = VALUE(entry?.harness)
  42     const id = STR(harness?.id), title = STR(harness?.title)
  43     if (!serverInstanceId || !id || !title) throw new Error('ACP orchestration entry is incomplete')
  44     return { serverInstanceId, harness: { id, title } }
  45   })
  46   return { offered: true, entries }
  47 }
  48 
  49 /** One bridge instance answers for the whole Server: identity first, connectors derive per pair. */
  50 export async function requestAcpOrchestration(adapterId: string, bridge: AgentNativeBridge) {
  51   const instanceId = await bridge.open(adapterId).catch((error: unknown) => { throw new Error(unwrap(error)) })
  52   try {
  53     const identity = parseAcpIdentity(await bridge.send(instanceId, { method: 'acp/identity' }))
  54     return { instanceId, orchestration: identity }
  55   } catch (error) {
  56     await bridge.close(instanceId).catch(() => undefined)
  57     throw new Error(unwrap(error))
  58   }
  59 }
  60 
  61 const parseProjects = (value: unknown): readonly AgentWorkspaceInfo[] => {
  62   const items = VALUE(value)?.items
  63   if (!Array.isArray(items)) throw new Error('ACP project list is malformed')
  64   return items.map(item => {
  65     const raw = VALUE(item)
  66     const id = STR(raw?.id), normalizedPath = STR(raw?.normalizedPath)
  67     if (!id || !normalizedPath) throw new Error('ACP project record is incomplete')
  68     return { id, normalizedPath }
  69   })
  70 }
  71 const parseBinding = (value: unknown, expectId: string): AgentWorkspaceInfo => {
  72   const raw = VALUE(value)
  73   const id = STR(raw?.id), normalizedPath = STR(raw?.normalizedPath)
  74   if (!id || !normalizedPath) throw new Error(`ACP project binding is incomplete: ${expectId}`)
  75   return { id, normalizedPath }
  76 }
  77 
  78 /**
  79  * The pair-bound spec: every call is one bridge frame against the orchestration instance, and
  80  * each acquired channel gets a message-stream bridge — inbound ACP frames arrive as `acp/message`
  81  * events routed by connectionId, outbound writes ride `acp/channel/send`, and `release()` is the
  82  * explicit backend stand-down. A transport death arrives as `acp/down` and only errors the stream:
  83  * it is never a release (the client's own rules decide what that means).
  84  */
  85 export function hostChannelSpec(adapterId: string, bridge: AgentNativeBridge, instanceId: string, entry: AcpOrchestrationEntry): AcpChannelSpec {
  86   const inboxes = new Map<string, { message(message: acp.AnyMessage): void; down(reason: string): void }>()
  87   // The subscription outlives individual channels: events for released connections find no inbox
  88   // and are dropped. The plugin scope closes the instance itself.
  89   const unsubscribe = bridge.subscribe(event => {
  90     if (event.instanceId !== instanceId) return
  91     const frame = VALUE(event.frame)
  92     const connectionId = STR(frame?.connectionId)
  93     if (!connectionId) return
  94     const inbox = inboxes.get(connectionId)
  95     if (!inbox) return
  96     // The ACP message rides nested: the wrapper's own `method` names the bridge event, and the
  97     // agent's JSON-RPC frame must reach the SDK untouched.
  98     if (frame?.method === 'acp/message') {
  99       const message = VALUE(frame.params)?.frame
 100       if (message) inbox.message(message as acp.AnyMessage)
 101     }
 102     else if (frame?.method === 'acp/down') inbox.down(unwrap(String(VALUE(frame.params)?.reason ?? 'channel closed')))
 103   })
 104   const call = async (method: string, params: Record<string, unknown>) => {
 105     try { return await bridge.send(instanceId, { method, params }) }
 106     catch (error) { throw new Error(unwrap(error)) }
 107   }
 108   return {
 109     serverInstanceId: entry.serverInstanceId,
 110     harness: entry.harness,
 111     listProjects: async () => parseProjects(await call('acp/projects', { instanceId: entry.serverInstanceId })),
 112     openProject: async id => parseBinding(await call('acp/openProject', { instanceId: entry.serverInstanceId, id }), id),
 113     // The add rides the Server's existing `workspaces.open` upsert (native.ts owns that call and
 114     // validates the path); a Server without the capability refuses the frame honestly, and the UI
 115     // keeps the entry disabled.
 116     addProject: async path => parseBinding(await call('acp/addProject', { instanceId: entry.serverInstanceId, path }), 'added'),
 117     async acquireChannel(projectId) {
 118       const opened = VALUE(await call('acp/channel/open', { instanceId: entry.serverInstanceId, projectId }))
 119       const connectionId = STR(opened?.connectionId)
 120       if (!connectionId) throw new Error(`ACP channel open returned no connection id for project ${projectId}`)
 121       const binding = parseBinding(opened?.binding, projectId)
 122       let controller!: ReadableStreamDefaultController<acp.AnyMessage>
 123       const downListeners = new Set<(reason: string) => void>()
 124       let downReason: string | undefined
 125       const markDown = (reason: string) => {
 126         if (downReason !== undefined) return
 127         downReason = reason
 128         for (const listener of [...downListeners]) listener(reason)
 129         downListeners.clear()
 130       }
 131       const readable = new ReadableStream<acp.AnyMessage>({ start: c => { controller = c } })
 132       inboxes.set(connectionId, {
 133         message: message => { controller.enqueue(message) },
 134         down: reason => { markDown(reason); try { controller.error(new Error(reason)) } catch { /* already ended */ } },
 135       })
 136       const stream: acp.Stream = {
 137         readable,
 138         writable: new WritableStream<acp.AnyMessage>({
 139           write: async message => { await call('acp/channel/send', { connectionId, frame: message }) },
 140           close: () => { inboxes.delete(connectionId); markDown('channel stream closed') },
 141         }),
 142       }
 143       return {
 144         connectionId, binding, stream,
 145         subscribeDown(listener) {
 146           if (downReason !== undefined) listener(downReason)
 147           else downListeners.add(listener)
 148           return () => { downListeners.delete(listener) }
 149         },
 150         authorizeSubmission: async (submission: AcpControlledSubmission): Promise<AcpSubmissionAdmission> => {
 151           const answer = VALUE(await call('acp/channel/authorizeSubmission', { connectionId, submission }))
 152           if (answer?.kind === 'accepted' && answer.submissionId === submission.submissionId) {
 153             return { kind: 'accepted', submissionId: submission.submissionId }
 154           }
 155           if (answer?.kind === 'refused' && STR(answer.code) && STR(answer.reason)) {
 156             return { kind: 'refused', code: answer.code as string, reason: answer.reason as string }
 157           }
 158           if (answer?.kind === 'unknown' && STR(answer.operationId) && STR(answer.reason)) {
 159             return { kind: 'unknown', operationId: answer.operationId as string, reason: answer.reason as string }
 160           }
 161           throw new Error('ACP backend admission answer is malformed')
 162         },
 163         authorizePermission: async (decision: AcpPermissionDecision): Promise<AcpSubmissionAdmission> => {
 164           const answer = VALUE(await call('acp/channel/authorizePermission', { connectionId, decision }))
 165           if (answer?.kind === 'accepted' && answer.submissionId === decision.interactionId) {
 166             return { kind: 'accepted', submissionId: decision.interactionId }
 167           }
 168           if (answer?.kind === 'refused' && STR(answer.code) && STR(answer.reason)) {
 169             return { kind: 'refused', code: answer.code as string, reason: answer.reason as string }
 170           }
 171           if (answer?.kind === 'unknown' && STR(answer.operationId) && STR(answer.reason)) {
 172             return { kind: 'unknown', operationId: answer.operationId as string, reason: answer.reason as string }
 173           }
 174           throw new Error('ACP backend permission answer is malformed')
 175         },
 176         async release() {
 177           inboxes.delete(connectionId)
 178           markDown('ACP channel released')
 179           try { controller.error(new Error('ACP channel released')) } catch { /* already ended */ }
 180           await call('acp/channel/release', { connectionId })
 181         },
 182       } satisfies AcpChannelHandle
 183     },
 184   }
 185 }

### M:plugins/connectors/acp/src/client.ts
   1 import * as acp from '@agentclientprotocol/sdk'
   2 import type {
   3   AgentCapabilities, AgentClient, AgentInteraction, AgentMessage, AgentReleaseState, AgentReleaseStatus,
   4   AgentSessionInfo, AgentSnapshot, AgentToolCall, AgentWorkspaceInfo, InteractionAnswer, RunStatus,
   5 } from '@extensions/ordessa.agent-contracts/contract.js'
   6 import type { AcpChannelHandle, AcpChannelSpec } from './channel'
   7 import { parseNativeCommands, type NativeCommandCatalog } from './commands'
   8 import { type AcpControlledSubmission, validateControlledSubmission } from './submission'
   9 import { referenceScheme, samePreparedReference, validPreparedReference, type AcpAttachmentCapabilities, type AcpAttachmentTarget,
  10   type AcpPreparedAttachment } from './attachments'
  11 
  12 /**
  13  * The single ACP wire→`AgentSnapshot` mapping for this product (no other module speaks protocol).
  14  *
  15  * Channel model (reviewed seam, tests/integration/acp-connector/fixtures/target-seam.ts): selecting the
  16  * Server/Harness reads projects only — no channel. `openWorkspace` binds one project, acquires its
  17  * managed channel and runs exactly one `initialize` on it. A draft stays frontend-only until
  18  * `createAndSend`, which lazily runs `session/new` + `session/prompt` and resolves once the Harness
  19  * has ACCEPTED the first send (any inbound event referencing the native session: a `session/update`,
  20  * a reverse request, or the prompt response) — never waiting for the whole turn, and rejecting (not
  21  * re-issuing) when the link dies before that acceptance.
  22  *
  23  * Every native fact is namespaced by its channel: two project channels that mint the same native
  24  * session id keep separate keys inside this one client (raw id while unconflicted, channel-scoped
  25  * once another channel claims it). A transport drop is never a cancel and never a release; a view
  26  * switch is neither; only `dispose()` calls the handle's explicit release.
  27  */
  28 
  29 const STOP_REASON: Record<acp.StopReason, RunStatus> = {
  30   end_turn: 'completed', cancelled: 'cancelled', max_turn_requests: 'failed', refusal: 'failed', max_tokens: 'unknown',
  31 }
  32 const TOOL_STATUS: Record<acp.ToolCallStatus, AgentToolCall['status']> = {
  33   pending: 'running', in_progress: 'running', completed: 'completed', failed: 'failed',
  34 }
  35 const OPEN_RUN = (status: RunStatus) => status === 'starting' || status === 'running' || status === 'stop-requested'
  36 const KNOWN_SESSION_UPDATES = new Set([
  37   'user_message_chunk', 'agent_message_chunk', 'agent_thought_chunk', 'tool_call',
  38   'tool_call_update', 'plan', 'plan_update', 'plan_removed', 'available_commands_update',
  39   'current_mode_update', 'config_option_update', 'session_info_update', 'usage_update',
  40   'notice', 'compaction_update', 'compaction_summary_chunk',
  41 ])

  43 interface Channel {
  44   readonly projectId: string
  45   readonly binding: AgentWorkspaceInfo
  46   readonly handle: AcpChannelHandle
  47   conn: acp.ClientSideConnection
  48   capabilities: acp.AgentCapabilities
  49   /** Native session id → this client's key, scoped to the channel that owns it. */
  50   readonly nativeToKey: Map<string, string>
  51   commandsDown: boolean
  52   unsubscribeDown?: () => void
  53   creatingSessions: number
  54   readonly pendingCommands: Map<string, readonly import('./commands').NativeCommand[] | null>
  55 }
  56 interface Route { channel: Channel; nativeId: string; key: string }
  57 /** `runId` is the run that was live when the native request arrived ('' when none was): the
  58  * answer belongs to THAT run, never to whatever the session happens to run later. */
  59 interface InteractionRecord { item: AgentInteraction; settle: (response: acp.RequestPermissionResponse) => void; runId: string }
  60 interface FirstSend { settle: () => void; fail: (error: unknown) => void }
  61 
  62 export class AcpClient implements AgentClient {
  63   isDisposed = false
  64   private readonly spec: AcpChannelSpec
  65   private state: AgentSnapshot
  66   private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>()
  67   private readonly channels = new Map<string, Channel>()
  68   private readonly opening = new Map<string, Promise<Channel>>()
  69   /** Handles acquired but not yet registered as channels: between `acquireChannel` returning and
  70    * the channel landing in `channels`, this set — not `channels` — is what makes dispose() able to
  71    * reach the handle, so a late arrival can never be orphaned unreleased. */
  72   private readonly unownedHandles = new Set<AcpChannelHandle>()
  73   /** Confirmed releases only — an attempt that failed never joins this set (see `releaseHandle`). */
  74   private readonly releasedHandles = new WeakSet<AcpChannelHandle>()
  75   private readonly releasing = new WeakMap<AcpChannelHandle, Promise<void>>()
  76   /** The release lifecycle ledger: an entry exists from the moment its attempt is IN-FLIGHT (an
  77    * unanswered release is a STATE, never an absence of evidence) through a FAILED refusal; the
  78    * backend's confirmation removes it — and the removal is itself announced. Failed entries stay
  79    * here retryable until a later attempt confirms them. */
  80   private readonly releaseLedger = new Map<string, { handle: AcpChannelHandle; status: 'in-flight' | 'failed'; reason?: string }>()
  81   private readonly releaseWatchers = new Set<(state: AgentReleaseState) => void>()
  82   /** The host-visible diagnostic half of the release lifecycle: contract `AgentClient.releaseFailures`,
  83    * read together with `releaseStates()`/`subscribeReleaseStates()` and the `retryReleases()` entry point. */
  84   get releaseFailures(): { connectionId: string; reason: string }[] {
  85     return [...this.releaseLedger].filter(([, record]) => record.status === 'failed')
  86       .map(([connectionId, record]) => ({ connectionId, reason: record.reason ?? '' }))
  87   }
  88   releaseStates = (): AgentReleaseState[] =>
  89     [...this.releaseLedger].map(([connectionId, record]) =>
  90       ({ connectionId, status: record.status, ...(record.reason === undefined ? {} : { reason: record.reason }) }))
  91   /** Announcements are delivered even after dispose(): an evicted client's asynchronous answers
  92    * are exactly what the host must keep receiving, and this notification — not polling — is how a
  93    * release-state change reaches the host's published surface. */
  94   subscribeReleaseStates = (listener: (state: AgentReleaseState) => void) => {
  95     this.releaseWatchers.add(listener)
  96     return () => { this.releaseWatchers.delete(listener) }
  97   }
  98   private announceRelease(handle: AcpChannelHandle, status: AgentReleaseStatus, reason?: string) {
  99     if (status === 'confirmed') this.releaseLedger.delete(handle.connectionId)
 100     else this.releaseLedger.set(handle.connectionId, { handle, status, ...(reason === undefined ? {} : { reason }) })
 101     const state: AgentReleaseState = { connectionId: handle.connectionId, status, ...(reason === undefined ? {} : { reason }) }
 102     for (const watcher of [...this.releaseWatchers]) watcher(state)
 103     this.maybeDrainReleases()
 104   }
 105   /** The host's forget-signal: this promise settles only when the client provably can never
 106    * announce another release state — disposed, no acquire/initialize attempt still in flight to
 107    * stand a handle down, and the backend has confirmed every stand-down ever started. An empty
 108    * ledger alone is NOT the condition: a late handle has not entered it yet. Checked after every
 109    * event that could complete the drain; one-way, and never rejects. */
 110   private settleReleases!: () => void
 111   private releasesDrained = false
 112   readonly releasesSettled: Promise<void> = new Promise(resolve => { this.settleReleases = resolve })
 113   private maybeDrainReleases() {
 114     if (this.releasesDrained || !this.isDisposed || this.releaseLedger.size > 0
 115       || this.unownedHandles.size > 0 || this.opening.size > 0) return
 116     this.releasesDrained = true
 117     this.settleReleases()
 118   }
 119   private readonly routes = new Map<string, Route>()
 120   private readonly nativeCommands = new Map<string, NativeCommandCatalog>()
 121   private readonly activeCommandSession = new Map<string, string>()
 122   private readonly buckets = new Map<string, AgentMessage[]>()
 123   /** The one message each session's chunkless stream is currently filling. `ContentChunk.messageId`
 124    * is optional in the pinned protocol, and a real Harness (Pi) omits it — consecutive chunks then
 125    * belong to the SAME message, so they accumulate into this slot instead of minting a card each.
 126    * The slot is a per-session projection state (the session key already carries its channel), and
 127    * it closes at every boundary that proves a new message started: the client's own turn lifecycle
 128    * (`startTurn`/`finishTurn` — the authoritative turn signal, since ACP allows one in-flight
 129    * prompt per session and the protocol names no turn-start event) and any tool event (content
 130    * before and after a tool call is independent). A chunk that DOES carry a messageId groups by
 131    * that native id (R8) and never joins or takes the slot. */
 132   private readonly openStreams = new Map<string, { id: string; role: 'user' | 'assistant' }>()
 133   private readonly sessionList: { items: AgentSessionInfo[]; state: 'unknown' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'partial' | 'error' } =
 134     { items: [], state: 'unknown' }
 135   private readonly runs = new Map<string, { id: string; sessionId: string; status: RunStatus }>()
 136   private readonly interactions = new Map<string, InteractionRecord>()
 137   private readonly firstSends = new Map<string, FirstSend>()
 138   /** A renderer identity crosses the backend only once per client lifetime, including uncertain
 139    * authorization calls. Reusing it after an error could consume the same permit twice. */
 140   private readonly usedControlledSubmissionIds = new Set<string>()
 141   private readonly pendingControlledSessions = new Set<string>()
 142   private readonly projects: AgentWorkspaceInfo[] = []
 143   private selectedWorkspaceId: string | undefined
 144   private sequence = 0
 145   /** `AgentClient.addWorkspace`, present exactly when the host orchestration supplies the
 146    * registration op (see AcpChannelSpec.addProject): absent means the UI must disable the entry,
 147    * never fake an add. The flow matches the CP connector — register, re-read the Server's list, and
 148    * select the authoritative project id. */
 149   readonly addWorkspace?: (path: string) => Promise<AgentWorkspaceInfo>

 151   private constructor(spec: AcpChannelSpec, connectorId: string, projects: readonly AgentWorkspaceInfo[]) {
 152     this.spec = spec
 153     if (spec.addProject) {
 154       const addProject = spec.addProject.bind(spec)
 155       this.addWorkspace = async (path: string) => {
 156         this.assertLive()
 157         const added = await addProject(path)
 158         await this.refreshWorkspaces()
 159         return this.openWorkspace(added.id)
 160       }
 161     }
 162     this.projects.push(...projects)
 163     this.state = {
 164       connection: {
 165         id: connectorId, title: `ACP · ${spec.harness.title}`, status: 'connected',
 166         capabilities: { history: 'unsupported', reasoning: 'supported', tools: 'supported', stop: 'supported',
 167           interactions: 'supported', models: 'unsupported', modes: 'unsupported', workspaces: 'supported' },
 168         serverInstanceId: spec.serverInstanceId,
 169       },
 170       sessions: [], sessionList: 'unknown', messages: {}, runs: {}, interactions: [], options: [],
 171       workspaces: { state: 'ready', items: [...projects] },
 172     }

 175   /** Reading the project list is orchestration work, not an ACP channel: nothing is acquired here. */
 176   static async connect(spec: AcpChannelSpec, connectorId: string): Promise<AcpClient> {
 177     const projects = await spec.listProjects()
 178     return new AcpClient(spec, connectorId, projects)
 179   }
