 181   getSnapshot = () => this.state
 182   /** The ACP event's own connection and native session scope, never a synthesized brand menu. */
 183   getNativeCommands = (sessionKey: string): NativeCommandCatalog => {
 184     const route = this.routes.get(sessionKey)
 185     if (!route) return Object.freeze({ kind: 'absent' })
 186     const channel = route.channel
 187     if (this.isDisposed || channel.commandsDown || this.channels.get(channel.projectId) !== channel)
 188       return Object.freeze({ kind: 'unknown', reason: 'channel-down' })
 189     if (!channel.handle.subscribeDown)
 190       return Object.freeze({ kind: 'unknown', reason: 'unobservable' })
 191     if (this.activeCommandSession.get(channel.handle.connectionId) !== sessionKey)
 192       return Object.freeze({ kind: 'unknown', reason: 'stale-session' })
 193     return this.nativeCommands.get(sessionKey) ?? Object.freeze({ kind: 'absent' })
 194   }
 195   subscribe = (listener: () => void) => { this.listeners.add(listener); return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener) } }
 196 
 197   private publish(patch: Partial<AgentSnapshot> = {}) {
 198     if (this.isDisposed) return
 199     this.state = {
 200       ...this.state,
 201       sessions: [...this.sessionList.items],
 202       sessionList: this.sessionList.state,
 203       messages: Object.fromEntries(this.buckets),
 204       runs: Object.fromEntries(this.runs),
 205       interactions: [...this.interactions.values()].map(record => record.item),
 206       workspaces: {
 207         state: 'ready', items: [...this.projects],
 208         ...(this.selectedWorkspaceId ? { selectedWorkspaceId: this.selectedWorkspaceId } : {}),
 209       },
 210       ...patch,
 211     }
 212     for (const listener of [...this.listeners]) listener()
 213   }
 214   private assertLive() { if (this.isDisposed) throw new Error('ACP connection disposed') }
 215   private nextId(prefix: string) { return `${prefix}_${++this.sequence}` }
 216 
 217   // ——— channel lifecycle ————————————————————————————————————————————————
 218 
 219   private async ensureChannel(projectId: string): Promise<Channel> {
 220     this.assertLive()
 221     const live = this.channels.get(projectId)
 222     if (live) return live
 223     const pending = this.opening.get(projectId)
 224     if (pending) return await pending
 225     // The opening entry is cleared on success AND failure: a failed task left in the map would pin
 226     // the project to the old error forever, so a retry could never recover a dropped handshake.
 227     const task = this.acquireAndInitialize(projectId)
 228       // The entry stays until the task itself settles — after dispose() it is the drain
 229       // attestation's witness that this attempt can still stand a late handle down.
 230       .finally(() => {
 231         if (this.opening.get(projectId) === task) this.opening.delete(projectId)
 232         this.maybeDrainReleases()
 233       })
 234     this.opening.set(projectId, task)
 235     return task
 236   }
 237 
 238   /** Release distinguishes three announced states, not one: CONFIRMED (never re-attempted — dispose()
 239    * and a failing open race on the same channel, and the backend counts every release call),
 240    * IN-FLIGHT (announced the instant the attempt starts; concurrent callers join the one request,
 241    * they do not issue a second), and FAILED — the handle stays retryable, the reason is recorded in
 242    * `releaseFailures`, and the rejection reaches every awaited caller instead of being swallowed.
 243    * No silent auto-retry: a failed handle is retried only by an explicit release — dispose()'s own
 244    * attempt, or the host's `retryReleases()`. */
 245   private releaseHandle(handle: AcpChannelHandle): Promise<void> {
 246     if (this.releasedHandles.has(handle)) return Promise.resolve()
 247     const pending = this.releasing.get(handle)
 248     if (pending) return pending
 249     // IN-FLIGHT is announced synchronously, before the first await: from this instant until the
 250     // backend answers, "no failure recorded" never means "nothing outstanding".
 251     this.announceRelease(handle, 'in-flight')
 252     const attempt = (async () => {
 253       try {
 254         await handle.release()
 255         this.releasedHandles.add(handle)
 256         this.announceRelease(handle, 'confirmed')
 257       } catch (error) {
 258         const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
 259         this.announceRelease(handle, 'failed', reason)
 260         throw error
 261       } finally {
 262         this.releasing.delete(handle)
 263       }
 264     })()
 265     this.releasing.set(handle, attempt)
 266     return attempt
 267   }
 268   /** A release whose outcome this site cannot surface (it has its own error to throw, or dispose is
 269    * sync): the failure is recorded and the handle kept retryable — here it must not become an
 270    * unhandled rejection, which is not the same thing as swallowing it. */
 271   private standDownObserved(handle: AcpChannelHandle): Promise<void> {
 272     return this.releaseHandle(handle).catch(() => undefined)
 273   }
 274 
 275   /** The host's explicit retry entry point (the optional `AgentClient.retryReleases` member): one
 276    * pass over every OUTSTANDING release — an in-flight attempt is joined and awaited, a failed one
 277    * gets exactly one further attempt, a confirmed one is no longer in the ledger at all — and a
 278    * refusal propagates to the caller instead of looping or swallowing. Nothing is booked as
 279    * confirmed except the backend's own answer, announced via `subscribeReleaseStates`. It is
 280    * deliberately reachable after dispose(): the outstanding handles live in the ledger, not in
 281    * the cleared channel maps, and the bridge that stands them down is owned by the plugin's
 282    * scope, not by this client. */
 283   async retryReleases(): Promise<void> {
 284     let refusal: unknown
 285     for (const record of [...this.releaseLedger.values()]) {
 286       try { await this.releaseHandle(record.handle) }
 287       catch (error) { refusal ??= error }
 288     }
 289     if (refusal !== undefined) throw refusal
 290   }
 291 
 292   /** The project query locates the acquire; the CHANNEL handle's binding is the authority for
 293    * every cwd this client sends (reviewed seam: `handle.binding.normalizedPath` is the
 294    * `session/new` cwd). `openProject` runs first (an unoffered project throws `project-invalid`
 295    * before any channel or wire traffic), then exactly one `initialize`. */
 296   private async acquireAndInitialize(projectId: string): Promise<Channel> {
 297     const opened = await this.spec.openProject(projectId)
 298     if (!this.projects.some(item => item.id === opened.id)) this.projects.push(opened)
 299     const handle = await this.spec.acquireChannel(opened.id)
 300     // From here on, this attempt owns the handle: dispose() mid-handshake reaches it through
 301     // unownedHandles (it is not in `channels` yet), and whoever gets there first releases it once.
 302     this.unownedHandles.add(handle)
 303     // A handle bound to another project is stood down, never trusted for its path.
 304     if (handle.binding.id !== projectId) {
 305       this.unownedHandles.delete(handle)
 306       await this.standDownObserved(handle)
 307       throw new Error(`ACP channel moved the bound project: asked ${projectId}, bound ${handle.binding.id}`)
 308     }
 309     const binding = handle.binding
 310     if (this.isDisposed) {
 311       this.unownedHandles.delete(handle)
 312       await this.standDownObserved(handle)
 313       throw new Error('ACP connection disposed')
 314     }
 315     const channel: Channel = {
 316       projectId: binding.id, binding, handle,
 317       conn: undefined as unknown as acp.ClientSideConnection,
 318       capabilities: {}, nativeToKey: new Map(), commandsDown: false,
 319       creatingSessions: 0, pendingCommands: new Map(),
 320     }
 321     if (handle.subscribeDown) channel.unsubscribeDown = handle.subscribeDown(() => {
 322       channel.commandsDown = true
 323       this.invalidateChannelCommands(channel, 'channel-down')
 324     })
 325     const observedStream: acp.Stream = {
 326       ...handle.stream,
 327       readable: handle.stream.readable.pipeThrough(new TransformStream<acp.AnyMessage, acp.AnyMessage>({
 328         transform: (frame, controller) => {
 329           this.observeCommandFrame(channel, frame)
 330           controller.enqueue(frame)
 331         },
 332       })),
 333     }
 334     channel.conn = new acp.ClientSideConnection(() => ({
 335       sessionUpdate: async params => { this.onSessionUpdate(channel, params) },
 336       requestPermission: async params => this.onRequestPermission(channel, params),
 337     }), observedStream)
 338     try {
 339       const init = await channel.conn.initialize({ protocolVersion: acp.PROTOCOL_VERSION, clientCapabilities: {} })
 340       // Registration after a mid-initialize dispose would leave a live channel nobody owns.
 341       if (this.isDisposed) throw new Error('ACP connection disposed')
 342       channel.capabilities = init.agentCapabilities ?? {}
 343     } catch (error) {
 344       channel.unsubscribeDown?.()
 345       // The handshake error is the caller's; a failed release behind it stays recorded and retryable.
 346       await this.standDownObserved(handle)
 347       throw error
 348     } finally {
 349       this.unownedHandles.delete(handle)
 350     }
 351     this.channels.set(binding.id, channel)
 352     this.publishHistory()
 353     return channel
 354   }
 355 
 356   private publishHistory() {
 357     const supported = [...this.channels.values()].some(channel =>
 358       channel.capabilities.loadSession === true || channel.capabilities.sessionCapabilities?.list != null)
 359     const capabilities: AgentCapabilities = { ...this.state.connection.capabilities, history: supported ? 'supported' : 'unsupported' }
 360     this.publish({ connection: { ...this.state.connection, capabilities } })
 361   }
 362 
 363   // ——— per-channel session keying ————————————————————————————————————————
 364 
 365   /** Raw native id while it is unclaimed inside this client; channel-scoped once a second channel
 366    * mints the same id. Routing of every inbound event and outbound op goes through this map, so
 367    * two channels' same-named sessions never share messages, runs, approvals or cancels. */
 368   private keyFor(channel: Channel, nativeId: string): string {
 369     const known = channel.nativeToKey.get(nativeId)
 370     if (known) return known
 371     let key = nativeId
 372     if (this.routes.has(key)) key = `${channel.handle.connectionId}:${nativeId}`
 373     while (this.routes.has(key)) key = `${channel.handle.connectionId}:${nativeId}#${++this.sequence}`
 374     channel.nativeToKey.set(nativeId, key)
 375     this.routes.set(key, { channel, nativeId, key })
 376     return key
 377   }
 378   private routeOf(sessionKey: string): Route {
 379     const route = this.routes.get(sessionKey)
 380     if (!route) throw new Error(`ACP session unavailable: ${sessionKey}`)
 381     return route
 382   }
 383 
 384   private activateCommandSession(channel: Channel, key: string) {
 385     const connectionId = channel.handle.connectionId
 386     for (const [sessionKey, route] of this.routes) {
 387       if (route.channel === channel && sessionKey !== key)
 388         this.nativeCommands.set(sessionKey, Object.freeze({ kind: 'unknown', reason: 'stale-session' }))
 389     }
 390     this.activeCommandSession.set(connectionId, key)
 391     this.nativeCommands.delete(key)
 392     const nativeId = this.routes.get(key)?.nativeId
 393     const pending = nativeId === undefined ? undefined : channel.pendingCommands.get(nativeId)
 394     if (nativeId !== undefined && channel.pendingCommands.has(nativeId)) {
 395       channel.pendingCommands.delete(nativeId)
 396       this.nativeCommands.set(key, pending === null
 397         ? Object.freeze({ kind: 'unknown', reason: 'malformed' })
 398         : Object.freeze({ kind: 'available', connectionId, nativeSessionId: nativeId,
 399           commands: pending! }))
 400     }
 401   }
 402 
 403   private invalidateChannelCommands(channel: Channel, reason: 'channel-down' | 'malformed') {
 404     channel.pendingCommands.clear()
 405     for (const [key, route] of this.routes) if (route.channel === channel)
 406       this.nativeCommands.set(key, Object.freeze({ kind: 'unknown', reason }))
 407     this.publish()
 408   }
 409 
 410   /** Inspect the raw same-channel frame before the SDK's skip-invalid-items deserializer. */
 411   private observeCommandFrame(channel: Channel, frame: acp.AnyMessage) {
 412     const raw = frame && typeof frame === 'object' ? frame as unknown as Record<string, unknown> : null
 413     if (!raw) { this.invalidateChannelCommands(channel, 'malformed'); return }
 414     if (raw.method !== 'session/update') return
 415     const params = raw.params && typeof raw.params === 'object' && !Array.isArray(raw.params)
 416       ? raw.params as Record<string, unknown> : null
 417     const update = params?.update && typeof params.update === 'object' && !Array.isArray(params.update)
 418       ? params.update as Record<string, unknown> : null
 419     if (!update || typeof update.sessionUpdate !== 'string'
 420         || !KNOWN_SESSION_UPDATES.has(update.sessionUpdate)) {
 421       this.invalidateChannelCommands(channel, 'malformed')
 422       return
 423     }
 424     if (update.sessionUpdate !== 'available_commands_update') return
 425     const nativeId = params?.sessionId
 426     if (typeof nativeId !== 'string' || !nativeId) {
 427       this.invalidateChannelCommands(channel, 'malformed')
 428       return
 429     }
 430     const key = channel.nativeToKey.get(nativeId)
 431     const commands = parseNativeCommands(update)
 432     if (!key) {
 433       if (channel.creatingSessions > 0 && !channel.commandsDown) {
 434         // A native Agent may publish its catalog before session/new replies. Keep only a bounded
 435         // same-channel candidate; it is promoted solely for the native id returned by that reply.
 436         if (!channel.pendingCommands.has(nativeId) && channel.pendingCommands.size >= 8)
 437           channel.pendingCommands.delete(channel.pendingCommands.keys().next().value!)
 438         channel.pendingCommands.set(nativeId, commands)
 439       }
 440       return
 441     }
 442     if (this.activeCommandSession.get(channel.handle.connectionId) !== key) return
 443     this.nativeCommands.set(key, commands === null
 444       ? Object.freeze({ kind: 'unknown', reason: 'malformed' })
 445       : Object.freeze({ kind: 'available', connectionId: channel.handle.connectionId,
 446         nativeSessionId: nativeId, commands }))
 447     this.publish()
 448   }
 449 
 450   // ——— AgentClient surface ————————————————————————————————————————————————
 451 
 452   async openWorkspace(id: string): Promise<AgentWorkspaceInfo> {
 453     const channel = await this.ensureChannel(id)
 454     this.selectedWorkspaceId = id
 455     this.publish()
 456     return channel.binding
 457   }
 458 
 459   async refreshWorkspaces(): Promise<void> {
 460     const projects = await this.spec.listProjects()
 461     this.projects.splice(0, this.projects.length, ...projects)
 462     this.publish()
 463   }
 464 
 465   private selectedChannelId(): string {
 466     const id = this.selectedWorkspaceId
 467     if (!id) throw new Error('ACP project gate: no project is bound')
 468     return id
 469   }
 470 
 471   private async createNativeSession(channel: Channel) {
 472     channel.creatingSessions += 1
 473     let returnedId: string | undefined
 474     try {
 475       const created = await channel.conn.newSession({ cwd: channel.binding.normalizedPath, mcpServers: [] })
 476       returnedId = created.sessionId
 477       return created
 478     } finally {
 479       channel.creatingSessions -= 1
 480       if (channel.creatingSessions === 0) {
 481         for (const nativeId of channel.pendingCommands.keys())
 482           if (nativeId !== returnedId) channel.pendingCommands.delete(nativeId)
 483       }
 484     }
 485   }
 486 
 487   /** First send of a draft: lazily create the native session under the channel's authoritative cwd,
 488    * prompt once, and resolve on acceptance — session created + first send accepted by the Harness,
 489    * NOT the whole turn (R27). An unknown outcome rejects; the caller keeps its requestId. */
 490   async createAndSend(workspaceId: string, text: string, requestId: string): Promise<{ sessionId: string }> {
 491     this.assertLive()
 492     void requestId // the ACP wire carries no client request id; idempotence is held by the caller until acceptance
 493     const channel = await this.ensureChannel(workspaceId)
 494     const created = await this.createNativeSession(channel)
 495     const nativeId = created.sessionId
 496     const key = this.keyFor(channel, nativeId)
 497     this.activateCommandSession(channel, key)
 498     this.upsertSession({ id: key, title: 'New session', workspaceId: channel.binding.id })
 499     this.selectedWorkspaceId = workspaceId
 500     this.appendMessage(key, { id: this.nextId('user'), role: 'user', text })
 501     const acceptance = this.holdAcceptance(channel, nativeId)
 502     const turn = this.startTurn(channel, key, nativeId, text)
 503     try { await acceptance.promise }
 504     catch (error) { void turn.catch(() => undefined); throw error }
 505     return { sessionId: key }
 506   }
 507 
 508   async newSession(): Promise<string> {
 509     const projectId = this.selectedChannelId()
 510     const channel = await this.ensureChannel(projectId)
 511     const created = await this.createNativeSession(channel)
 512     const key = this.keyFor(channel, created.sessionId)
 513     this.activateCommandSession(channel, key)
 514     this.upsertSession({ id: key, title: 'New session', workspaceId: projectId })
 515     this.publish({ selectedSessionId: key })
 516     return key
 517   }
 518 
 519   async refreshSessions(): Promise<void> {
 520     this.assertLive()
 521     const listable = [...this.channels.values()].filter(channel => channel.capabilities.sessionCapabilities?.list != null)
 522     if (!listable.length) {
 523       // Two different facts share this branch. NO channel bound is not a history failure — there is
 524       // simply no Harness to ask yet (the project gate blocks sends meanwhile), so the honest answer
 525       // is the state unchanged, not an error banner. A bound Harness that does not list IS the real
 526       // capability refusal, and stays an error.
 527       if (!this.channels.size) return
 528       this.sessionList.state = 'error'
 529       this.publish()
 530       throw new Error('ACP history: this Harness does not list sessions')
 531     }
 532     // The name version is claimed when the request is ISSUED, exactly like the background pull:
 533     // any name write issued after this refresh retires its answer, however late it lands.
 534     const seq = ++this.nameWrites
 535     this.sessionList.state = 'loading'
 536     this.publish()
 537     try {
 538       const listed = await Promise.all(listable.map(async channel => ({
 539         channel, sessions: (await channel.conn.listSessions({})).sessions,
 540       })))
 541       const mapped: AgentSessionInfo[] = listed.flatMap(({ channel, sessions }) => sessions.map(session => {
 542         const key = this.keyFor(channel, session.sessionId)
 543         const project = this.projects.find(item => item.normalizedPath === session.cwd)
 544         return {
 545           id: key, title: session.title ?? 'Session',
 546           ...(session.updatedAt ? { updatedAt: session.updatedAt } : {}),
 547           workspaceId: project?.id,
 548         }
 549       }))
 550       // A merge, never a wholesale overwrite: each entry lands only while no newer name write has
 551       // claimed its session. Locally known sessions the list does not carry yet stay visible.
 552       for (const entry of mapped) {
 553         if ((this.nameApplied.get(entry.id) ?? 0) >= seq) continue
 554         this.nameApplied.set(entry.id, seq)
 555         this.upsertSession(entry)
 556       }
 557       this.sessionList.state = 'ready'
 558     } catch (error) {
 559       this.sessionList.state = 'error'
 560       this.publish()
 561       throw error
 562     }
 563     this.publish()
 564   }
 565 
 566   /** List and load are different capabilities: an id never seen in a list is still loadable when the
 567    * wire allows it (R26); a refusal comes back as the wire's own error, never a fabricated success. */
 568   async openSession(id: string): Promise<void> {
 569     const known = this.routes.get(id)
 570     const channel = known?.channel ?? await this.ensureChannel(this.selectedChannelId())
 571     const nativeId = known?.nativeId ?? id
 572     const key = this.keyFor(channel, nativeId)
 573     await channel.conn.loadSession({ sessionId: nativeId, cwd: channel.binding.normalizedPath, mcpServers: [] })
 574     this.activateCommandSession(channel, key)
 575     this.upsertSession({ id: key, title: this.sessionTitle(key) ?? 'Session', workspaceId: channel.binding.id })
 576     this.publish({ selectedSessionId: key })
 577   }
 578 
 579   async send(sessionId: string, text: string): Promise<void> {
 580     const route = this.routeOf(sessionId)
 581     this.appendMessage(sessionId, { id: this.nextId('user'), role: 'user', text })
 582     await this.startTurn(route.channel, route.key, route.nativeId, text)
 583   }
 584 
 585   private attachmentTarget(route: Route): AcpAttachmentTarget {
 586     return { serverInstanceId: this.spec.serverInstanceId, connectionId: route.channel.handle.connectionId,
 587       projectId: route.channel.projectId, nativeSessionId: route.nativeId }
 588   }
 589 
 590   private attachmentRouteLive(route: Route, sessionId: string): boolean {
 591     return !this.isDisposed && !route.channel.commandsDown
 592       && this.channels.get(route.channel.projectId) === route.channel
 593       && this.routes.get(sessionId) === route
 594       && this.state.selectedSessionId === sessionId
 595       && this.activeCommandSession.get(route.channel.handle.connectionId) === sessionId
 596   }
 597 
 598   private validAttachmentCapabilities(caps: AcpAttachmentCapabilities): boolean {
 599     if (caps?.kind === 'available') return Number.isSafeInteger(caps.maxBytes) && caps.maxBytes > 0
 600       && Number.isSafeInteger(caps.maxCount) && caps.maxCount > 0
 601       && Array.isArray(caps.mimeTypes) && caps.mimeTypes.length > 0
 602       && caps.mimeTypes.every(item => typeof item === 'string' && !!item)
 603       && Array.isArray(caps.uriSchemes) && caps.uriSchemes.length > 0
 604       && caps.uriSchemes.every(item => typeof item === 'string' && /^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:$/.test(item) && item !== 'file:')
 605     return (caps?.kind === 'absent' || caps?.kind === 'unknown') && typeof caps.reason === 'string' && !!caps.reason
 606   }
 607 
 608   async attachmentCapabilities(sessionId: string): Promise<AcpAttachmentCapabilities> {
 609     const route = this.routeOf(sessionId)
 610     const port = route.channel.handle.attachmentPrepare
 611     if (!port || route.channel.commandsDown) return { kind: 'absent', reason: 'attachment preparation owner unavailable' }
 612     try {
 613       const caps = await port.capabilities(this.attachmentTarget(route))
 614       if (!this.attachmentRouteLive(route, sessionId) || !this.validAttachmentCapabilities(caps))
 615         return { kind: 'unknown', reason: 'attachment capability owner changed or malformed' }
 616       return caps
 617     }
 618     catch { return { kind: 'unknown', reason: 'attachment capability outcome unknown' } }
 619   }
 620 
 621   async prepareAttachment(sessionId: string, sourceId: string, idempotencyKey: string): Promise<AcpPreparedAttachment> {
 622     const route = this.routeOf(sessionId)
 623     const port = route.channel.handle.attachmentPrepare
 624     if (!port || route.channel.commandsDown || !sourceId || !idempotencyKey)
 625       throw new Error('ACP attachment preparation unavailable')
 626     const target = this.attachmentTarget(route)
 627     const caps = await this.attachmentCapabilities(sessionId)
 628     if (caps.kind !== 'available') throw new Error(`ACP attachment capability ${caps.kind}`)
 629     const result = await port.prepare(target, sourceId, idempotencyKey)
 630     if (!this.attachmentRouteLive(route, sessionId)) {
 631       if (result.kind === 'prepared' && validPreparedReference(result.reference)) {
 632         try { await port.release(target, result.reference.preparedId) }
 633         catch { throw new Error('ACP attachment preparation cleanup unknown') }
 634       }
 635       throw new Error('ACP attachment preparation owner changed')
 636     }
 637     if (result.kind === 'unknown') throw new Error(`ACP attachment preparation unknown: ${result.operationId}`)
 638     if (result.kind !== 'prepared' || !validPreparedReference(result.reference)
 639       || result.reference.byteLength > caps.maxBytes || !caps.mimeTypes.includes(result.reference.mimeType)
 640       || !caps.uriSchemes.includes(referenceScheme(result.reference.uri) ?? ''))
 641       throw new Error('ACP attachment preparation refused or malformed')
 642     return Object.freeze({ ...result.reference })
 643   }
 644 
 645   async releasePreparedAttachment(sessionId: string, preparedId: string): Promise<void> {
 646     const route = this.routeOf(sessionId)
 647     const port = route.channel.handle.attachmentPrepare
 648     if (!port || !preparedId) throw new Error('ACP attachment release unavailable')
 649     await port.release(this.attachmentTarget(route), preparedId)
 650   }
 651 
 652   /** Next-submit attachment/command path through this channel's existing ACP client.
 653    * A backend admission refusal or uncertain result sends zero prompt frames. This is an
 654    * explicit controlled surface while legacy AgentClient.send remains available; Server
 655    * relay enforcement is required before it can be called a production C5 gate.
 656    */
 657   async sendControlled(sessionId: string, submission: AcpControlledSubmission): Promise<void> {
 658     this.assertLive()
 659     const route = this.routeOf(sessionId)
 660     validateControlledSubmission(submission)
 661     // Caller-owned DTOs are mutable at runtime despite readonly TypeScript fields. Freeze the
 662     // values before the first await so authorization and ACP bytes describe one submission.
 663     const frozen: AcpControlledSubmission = { ...submission,
 664       attachments: submission.attachments.map(item => ({ ...item })) }
 665     if (frozen.nativeSessionId !== route.nativeId) throw new Error('ACP submission session changed')
 666     if (this.usedControlledSubmissionIds.has(frozen.submissionId))
 667       throw new Error('ACP submission identity already used')
 668     if (this.pendingControlledSessions.has(route.key))
 669       throw new Error('ACP session already pending controlled admission')
 670     if (this.openRunOf(route.key)) throw new Error('ACP session is already outputting')
 671     const authorize = route.channel.handle.authorizeSubmission
 672     if (!authorize) throw new Error('ACP backend submission admission unavailable')
 673     if (!route.channel.handle.subscribeDown) throw new Error('ACP channel liveness unobservable')
 674     if (route.channel.commandsDown || this.channels.get(route.channel.projectId) !== route.channel)
 675       throw new Error('ACP channel owner gone or down')
 676     if (this.state.selectedSessionId !== sessionId
 677         || this.activeCommandSession.get(route.channel.handle.connectionId) !== sessionId)
 678       throw new Error('ACP submission session changed')
 679     this.usedControlledSubmissionIds.add(frozen.submissionId)
 680     this.pendingControlledSessions.add(route.key)
 681     try {
 682       if (frozen.attachments.length) {
 683         const port = route.channel.handle.attachmentPrepare
 684         if (!port) throw new Error('ACP attachment preparation owner unavailable')
 685         const target = this.attachmentTarget(route)
 686         const caps = await this.attachmentCapabilities(sessionId)
 687         if (caps.kind !== 'available' || frozen.attachments.length > caps.maxCount
 688           || !Number.isSafeInteger(caps.maxBytes) || caps.maxBytes <= 0)
 689           throw new Error('ACP attachment capability unavailable or exceeded')
 690         const ids = new Set<string>()
 691         for (const item of frozen.attachments) {
 692           if (!validPreparedReference(item) || ids.has(item.preparedId)
 693             || item.byteLength > caps.maxBytes || !caps.mimeTypes.includes(item.mimeType)
 694             || !caps.uriSchemes.includes(referenceScheme(item.uri) ?? ''))
 695             throw new Error('ACP attachment reference invalid or unsupported')
 696           ids.add(item.preparedId)
 697           const verification = await port.verify(target, item)
 698           if (verification.kind === 'unknown') throw new Error(`ACP attachment verification unknown: ${verification.operationId}`)
 699           if (verification.kind !== 'verified' || !validPreparedReference(verification.reference)
 700             || !samePreparedReference(item, verification.reference))
 701             throw new Error('ACP attachment ownership or content changed')
 702           if (!this.attachmentRouteLive(route, sessionId)) throw new Error('ACP attachment owner changed')
 703         }
 704       }
 705       if (!this.attachmentRouteLive(route, sessionId)) throw new Error('ACP attachment owner changed')
 706       const admission = await authorize({ ...frozen, attachments: frozen.attachments.map(item => ({ ...item })) })
 707       if (admission.kind === 'refused') throw new Error(`ACP submission refused: ${admission.code}: ${admission.reason}`)
 708       if (admission.kind === 'unknown') throw new Error(`ACP submission outcome unknown: ${admission.operationId}`)
 709       if (admission.kind !== 'accepted' || admission.submissionId !== frozen.submissionId) {
 710         throw new Error('ACP submission admission identity changed')
 711       }
 712       // An accepted backend decision authorizes only the owner state observed at admission.
 713       // Session selection, another run, release and transport death can all change while awaited.
 714       if (this.isDisposed || route.channel.commandsDown
 715           || this.channels.get(route.channel.projectId) !== route.channel
 716           || this.routes.get(sessionId) !== route)
 717         throw new Error('ACP channel owner gone or down')
 718       if (this.state.selectedSessionId !== sessionId
 719           || this.activeCommandSession.get(route.channel.handle.connectionId) !== sessionId)
 720         throw new Error('ACP submission session changed')
 721       if (this.openRunOf(route.key)) throw new Error('ACP session is already outputting')
 722       const blocks: acp.ContentBlock[] = [
 723         { type: 'text', text: frozen.text },
 724         ...frozen.attachments.map(item => ({ type: 'resource_link' as const, name: item.name,
 725           uri: item.uri, ...(item.mimeType ? { mimeType: item.mimeType } : {}) })),
 726       ]
 727       this.appendMessage(sessionId, { id: this.nextId('user'), role: 'user', text: frozen.text })
 728       await this.startTurn(route.channel, route.key, route.nativeId, frozen.text, blocks)
 729     } finally {
 730       this.pendingControlledSessions.delete(route.key)
 731     }
 732   }
 733 
 734   async stop(sessionId: string, runId: string): Promise<void> {
 735     this.assertLive()
 736     const run = this.runs.get(runId)
 737     const route = this.routeOf(sessionId)
 738     // The cancel targets a session, not a run: a foreign runId under this sessionId, or a run
 739     // that already settled, would flip the wrong status and silently cancel a live turn on the
 740     // named session. Everything is validated before any mutation, so a wrong call cancels zero.
 741     if (!run || run.sessionId !== route.key || !OPEN_RUN(run.status)) throw new Error('Agent run unavailable')
 742     // `session/cancel` is a bare notification: the request is only requested, never confirmed here.
 743     run.status = 'stop-requested'
 744     // From this synchronous instant the run's open asks are un-submittable at the entry — and a
 745     // cancel send that fails below must NOT quietly re-open them: an answer already 'resolved'
 746     // stays a delivered wire fact (never pretended back), a 'pending' one strands as unknown.
 747     this.strandPendingInteractions(route.key)
 748     this.publish()
 749     await route.channel.conn.cancel({ sessionId: route.nativeId })
 750   }
 751 
 752   async respond(interactionId: string, answer: InteractionAnswer): Promise<void> {
 753     const record = this.interactions.get(interactionId)
 754     if (!record || record.item.state !== 'pending') throw new Error(`ACP interaction unavailable: ${interactionId}`)
 755     // The button is not the gate: an answer belongs to the very run that received this request,
 756     // not to "whatever this session runs now" — a late ask from a settled or stopped run is
 757     // refused even while a newer turn is live, and nothing goes out on the wire.
 758     const run = record.runId === '' ? undefined : this.runs.get(record.runId)
 759     if (!run || run.status !== 'running') throw new Error(`ACP interaction unavailable: the agent run is no longer answering: ${interactionId}`)
 760     let outcome: acp.RequestPermissionOutcome
 761     if (answer.kind === 'cancel') outcome = { outcome: 'cancelled' }
 762     else if (answer.kind === 'choice') {
 763       const choice = record.item.choices?.find(item => item.id === answer.choiceId)
 764       // The native optionId answers the wire; a foreign option never does, and the request stays pending.
 765       if (!choice) throw new Error(`ACP answer option is not part of this request: ${answer.choiceId}`)
 766       outcome = { outcome: 'selected', optionId: choice.id }
 767     } else throw new Error('ACP permission requests are answered with a native option or a cancel')
 768     record.item.state = 'responding'
 769     this.publish()
 770     record.settle({ outcome })
 771     record.item.state = 'resolved'
 772     this.publish()
 773   }
 774 
 775   /** Opt-in execution-time answer path. The native ACP option is sent only after the backend
 776    * accepts this exact channel/session/run/interaction/option; missing authority fails closed.
 777    * Legacy `respond` remains until the Server relay enforces the same decision for every path.
 778    */
 779   async respondControlled(interactionId: string, answer: InteractionAnswer): Promise<void> {
 780     this.assertLive()
 781     const record = this.interactions.get(interactionId)
 782     if (!record || record.item.state !== 'pending' || answer.kind !== 'choice') {
 783       throw new Error('ACP controlled permission needs a pending native choice')
 784     }
 785     if (!record.item.choices?.some(choice => choice.id === answer.choiceId)) {
 786       throw new Error('ACP answer option is not part of this request')
 787     }
 788     const route = this.routeOf(record.item.sessionId)
 789     const choiceId = answer.choiceId
 790     const authorize = route.channel.handle.authorizePermission
 791     if (!authorize) throw new Error('ACP backend permission admission unavailable')
 792     const admission = await authorize({ nativeSessionId: route.nativeId, interactionId,
 793       runId: record.runId, optionId: choiceId })
 794     if (admission.kind === 'refused') throw new Error(`ACP permission refused: ${admission.code}: ${admission.reason}`)
 795     if (admission.kind === 'unknown') throw new Error(`ACP permission outcome unknown: ${admission.operationId}`)
 796     if (admission.kind !== 'accepted' || admission.submissionId !== interactionId) {
 797       throw new Error('ACP permission admission identity changed')
 798     }
 799     await this.respond(interactionId, { kind: 'choice', choiceId }) // rechecks current run
 800   }
 801 
 802   async setOption(id: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
 803     void id; void value
 804     throw new Error('ACP options are managed by the Harness session state, not this client')
 805   }
 806 
 807   /** Explicit lifecycle release: view switches and transport drops never reach here (R23/R13). */
 808   dispose(): void {
 809     if (this.isDisposed) return
 810     this.isDisposed = true
 811     for (const acceptance of this.firstSends.values()) acceptance.fail(new Error('ACP connection disposed'))
 812     this.firstSends.clear()
 813     for (const run of this.runs.values()) if (OPEN_RUN(run.status)) run.status = 'unknown'
 814     for (const record of this.interactions.values()) if (record.item.state === 'pending') record.item.state = 'unknown'
 815     // dispose() is sync: a failed release stays recorded in `releaseFailures` and stays retryable
 816     // through `retryReleases()` — this is the one caller that cannot await the outcome, and a
 817     // second dispose() is blocked by isDisposed, so the host entry point is not incidental.
 818     for (const channel of this.channels.values()) void this.standDownObserved(channel.handle)
 819     // Handles whose acquire/initialize is still in flight: dispose owns them too, or the late
 820     // arrival would be a backend channel nobody can ever release.
 821     for (const handle of this.unownedHandles) void this.standDownObserved(handle)
 822     this.unownedHandles.clear()
 823     for (const channel of this.channels.values()) channel.unsubscribeDown?.()
 824     this.channels.clear()
 825     // `opening` is deliberately NOT cleared: an in-flight acquire may still land a handle after
 826     // this returns, and its stand-down must count as outstanding until the task itself finishes
 827     // (ensureChannel's finally) — that is what `releasesSettled` attests.
 828     this.maybeDrainReleases()
 829     this.state = {
 830       ...this.state,
 831       connection: { ...this.state.connection, status: 'disconnected' },
 832       runs: Object.fromEntries(this.runs),
 833       interactions: [...this.interactions.values()].map(record => record.item),
 834     }
 835     for (const listener of [...this.listeners]) listener()
 836   }
 837 
 838   // ——— turn execution —————————————————————————————————————————————————————
 839 
 840   /** Registers the run before the request leaves (R27: accepted mid-flight runs are observable as
 841    * 'running'), settles it only from the prompt's own stopReason, and verdicts 'unknown' — never a
 842    * fake 'cancelled' — when the link dies (R13/U5). A session's projection carries its current
 843    * run: a new turn supersedes that session's settled verdict, never two live runs per session
 844    * (ACP allows one in-flight prompt per session), so R17's read of "the session's run" is the
 845    * turn it just sent, not the previous turn's history. */
 846   private startTurn(channel: Channel, key: string, nativeId: string, text: string,
 847                     blocks: acp.ContentBlock[] = [{ type: 'text', text }]): Promise<acp.PromptResponse> {
 848     const runId = this.nextId('run')
 849     this.closeStream(key) // a new turn's stream never continues the previous turn's last card
 850     for (const [id, run] of this.runs) if (run.sessionId === key && !OPEN_RUN(run.status)) this.runs.delete(id)
 851     this.runs.set(runId, { id: runId, sessionId: key, status: 'running' })
 852     this.publish({ selectedSessionId: key })
 853     const turn = channel.conn.prompt({ sessionId: nativeId, prompt: blocks }).then(
 854       response => {
 855         this.accept(channel, nativeId)
 856         this.finishTurn(runId, key, STOP_REASON[response.stopReason] ?? 'unknown')
 857         return response
 858       },
 859       error => {
 860         this.accept(channel, nativeId, error)
 861         this.finishTurn(runId, key, 'unknown')
 862         throw error
 863       },
 864     )
 865     void turn.catch(() => undefined) // the caller's copy carries the rejection; the internal one must not go unhandled
 866     return turn
 867   }
 868 
 869   private finishTurn(runId: string, key: string, status: RunStatus) {
 870     this.closeStream(key) // the turn's verdict closes its stream: no chunk may append after the stop
 871     const run = this.runs.get(runId)
 872     if (run) run.status = status
 873     for (const message of this.buckets.get(key) ?? []) {
 874       if (message.status && OPEN_RUN(message.status)) this.mutateMessage(key, message.id, m => { m.status = status })
 875     }
 876     // A closed turn strands every ask it was holding — on ANY verdict, not just a dead link:
 877     // cancelled or completed leaves no submittable approval behind.
 878     this.strandPendingInteractions(key)
 879     // `session/new` carries no title; the native name lives in `session/list`. Pull it once the
 880     // turn settled (that is when the Harness has one) — the only name authority this client uses.
 881     const route = this.routes.get(key)
 882     if (route) this.pullNativeTitle(route)
 883     this.publish()
 884   }
 885 
 886   /** The run of this session key that is still open (ACP allows at most one in-flight prompt per
 887    * session, so at most one exists); approvals are answerable only while it is 'running'. */
 888   private openRunOf(sessionKey: string) {
 889     for (const run of this.runs.values()) {
 890       if (run.sessionId === sessionKey && OPEN_RUN(run.status)) return run
 891     }
 892     return undefined
 893   }
 894   private strandPendingInteractions(sessionKey: string) {
 895     for (const record of this.interactions.values()) {
 896       if (record.item.sessionId === sessionKey && record.item.state === 'pending') record.item.state = 'unknown'
 897     }
 898   }
 899   /** A background name pull through the channel's EXISTING list capability. It never blocks or
 900    * fails a turn: an absent name or an unanswered list leaves the current title exactly as it is
 901    * — no model naming, no summary substitution, nothing invented. */
 902   private pullNativeTitle(route: Route) {
 903     if (route.channel.capabilities.sessionCapabilities?.list == null) return
 904     // Issued NOW: whatever claims this session's name after this point outranks this answer,
 905     // no matter which of the two comes back first.
 906     const seq = ++this.nameWrites
 907     void (async () => {
 908       try {
 909         const listed = await route.channel.conn.listSessions({})
 910         const match = listed.sessions.find(session => session.sessionId === route.nativeId)
 911         if (!match?.title) return
 912         // Title-only merge: the local entry owns workspaceId and history; the list only names it.
 913         const existing = this.sessionList.items.find(item => item.id === route.key)
 914         if (!existing) return
 915         if ((this.nameApplied.get(route.key) ?? 0) >= seq) return // a newer write already landed
 916         this.nameApplied.set(route.key, seq)
 917         this.upsertSession({
 918           id: route.key, title: match.title,
 919           ...(match.updatedAt ? { updatedAt: match.updatedAt } : {}),
 920         })
 921         this.publish()
 922       } catch {
 923         // A failed pull is an absence of evidence, not evidence of absence: the title stands.
 924       }
 925     })()
 926   }
 927 
 928   // ——— first-send acceptance ———————————————————————————————————————————————
 929 
 930   private holdAcceptance(channel: Channel, nativeId: string): FirstSend & { promise: Promise<void> } {
 931     const slot = `${channel.projectId}|${nativeId}`
 932     let settle!: () => void
 933     let fail!: (error: unknown) => void
 934     const promise = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => { settle = resolve; fail = reject })
 935     const acceptance: FirstSend & { promise: Promise<void> } = {
 936       promise,
 937       settle: () => { this.firstSends.delete(slot); settle() },
 938       fail: error => { this.firstSends.delete(slot); fail(error) },
 939     }
 940     this.firstSends.set(slot, acceptance)
 941     return acceptance
 942   }
 943   /** Any inbound event naming the native session — an update, a reverse request, or the response —
 944    * is the proof the Harness accepted the first send. */
 945   private accept(channel: Channel, nativeId: string, failure?: unknown) {
 946     const acceptance = this.firstSends.get(`${channel.projectId}|${nativeId}`)
 947     if (!acceptance) return
 948     if (failure === undefined) acceptance.settle()
 949     else acceptance.fail(failure)
 950   }
 951 
 952   // ——— session/message projection —————————————————————————————————————————
 953 
 954   /** Name-write ordering: every title write claims a fresh sequence at ISSUE time; a background
 955    * list result commits only while its issue still outranks the last applied claim for that key.
 956    * Whichever write was issued LAST wins — arrival order can never roll a newer name back. */
 957   private nameWrites = 0
 958   private readonly nameApplied = new Map<string, number>()
 959   private claimSessionName(key: string) { this.nameApplied.set(key, ++this.nameWrites) }
 960   private sessionTitle(key: string): string | undefined {
 961     return this.sessionList.items.find(item => item.id === key)?.title
 962   }
 963   private upsertSession(session: AgentSessionInfo) {
 964     const at = this.sessionList.items.findIndex(item => item.id === session.id)
 965     if (at >= 0) this.sessionList.items[at] = { ...this.sessionList.items[at], ...session }
 966     else this.sessionList.items.push(session)
 967   }
 968   private appendMessage(key: string, message: AgentMessage) {
 969     const bucket = this.buckets.get(key) ?? []
 970     this.buckets.set(key, [...bucket, message])
 971     this.publish({ selectedSessionId: key })
 972   }
 973   private mutateMessage(key: string, messageId: string, edit: (message: AgentMessage) => void) {
 974     const bucket = this.buckets.get(key) ?? []
 975     this.buckets.set(key, bucket.map(message => {
 976       if (message.id !== messageId) return message
 977       const copy = { ...message }
 978       edit(copy)
 979       return copy
 980     }))
 981   }
 982   /** The message a chunk belongs to: by native messageId when the Harness names one (never split,
 983    * R8), else the session's open chunkless stream — created once and accumulated into until a
 984    * turn or tool boundary closes it. */
 985   private chunkMessage(key: string, messageId: string | undefined, role: 'user' | 'assistant'): AgentMessage {
 986     const bucket = this.buckets.get(key) ?? []
 987     if (messageId) {
 988       // An id-bearing chunk is always a different message than the open chunkless stream, so the
 989       // slot closes here: without it, no-id A → id B → no-id C would append C to A.
 990       this.closeStream(key)
 991       const existing = bucket.find(message => message.id === messageId)
 992       if (existing) return existing
 993       const created: AgentMessage = {
 994         id: messageId, role, text: '',
 995         ...(role === 'assistant' ? { status: 'running' as RunStatus } : {}),
 996       }
 997       this.buckets.set(key, [...bucket, created])
 998       return created
 999     }
1000     const open = this.openStreams.get(key)
1001     if (open && open.role === role) {
1002       const found = bucket.find(message => message.id === open.id)
1003       if (found) return found
1004     }
1005     const created: AgentMessage = {
1006       id: this.nextId('message'), role, text: '',
1007       ...(role === 'assistant' ? { status: 'running' as RunStatus } : {}),
1008     }
1009     this.buckets.set(key, [...bucket, created])
1010     this.openStreams.set(key, { id: created.id, role })
1011     return created
1012   }
1013   /** A turn or tool event proves the open chunkless stream ended; the next chunk opens its own card. */
1014   private closeStream(key: string) { this.openStreams.delete(key) }
1015   private textOf(content: acp.ContentBlock): string {
1016     return content.type === 'text' ? content.text : ''
1017   }
1018 
1019   private onSessionUpdate(channel: Channel, params: acp.SessionNotification) {
1020     if (this.isDisposed) return
1021     if (params.update.sessionUpdate === 'available_commands_update') {
1022       if (channel.nativeToKey.has(params.sessionId)) this.accept(channel, params.sessionId)
1023       return // The raw frame tap validates the entire list before SDK filtering.
1024     }
1025     const key = this.keyFor(channel, params.sessionId)
1026     this.accept(channel, params.sessionId)
1027     const update = params.update
1028     switch (update.sessionUpdate) {
1029       case 'agent_message_chunk': {
1030         const message = this.chunkMessage(key, update.messageId ?? undefined, 'assistant')
1031         this.mutateMessage(key, message.id, m => { m.text += this.textOf(update.content) })
1032         break
1033       }
1034       case 'agent_thought_chunk': {
1035         const message = this.chunkMessage(key, update.messageId ?? undefined, 'assistant')
1036         this.mutateMessage(key, message.id, m => { m.reasoning = (m.reasoning ?? '') + this.textOf(update.content) })
1037         break
1038       }
1039       case 'user_message_chunk': {
1040         const message = this.chunkMessage(key, update.messageId ?? undefined, 'user')
1041         this.mutateMessage(key, message.id, m => { m.text += this.textOf(update.content) })
1042         break
1043       }
1044       case 'tool_call':
1045       case 'tool_call_update':
1046         this.upsertTool(key, update)
1047         break
1048       case 'session_info_update':
1049         if (update.title) {
1050           // A live rename arrives with the newest knowledge: it claims the name immediately,
1051           // retiring any name pull still in flight.
1052           this.claimSessionName(key)
1053           this.upsertSession({ id: key, title: update.title })
1054         }
1055         break
1056       default:
1057         return // plan/usage/mode/commands updates have no slot in this product's snapshot yet
1058     }
1059     this.publish()
1060   }
1061 
1062   /** One `toolCallId`, one card (R10/R11): updates find the card by id wherever it lives in the
1063    * bucket; a never-seen id opens its own assistant message carrying the card. Any tool event also
1064    * ends the open chunkless stream — text before and after a tool call is independent content. */
1065   private upsertTool(key: string, update: acp.ToolCall | acp.ToolCallUpdate) {
1066     this.closeStream(key)
1067     const bucket = this.buckets.get(key) ?? []
1068     const cardAt = bucket.findIndex(message => message.tools?.some(tool => tool.id === update.toolCallId))
1069     if (cardAt >= 0) {
1070       const message = bucket[cardAt]
1071       const tools = (message.tools ?? []).map(tool => tool.id === update.toolCallId ? this.mergeTool(tool, update) : tool)
1072       this.buckets.set(key, bucket.map((item, index) => index === cardAt ? { ...item, tools } : item))
1073       return
1074     }
1075     const card = this.mergeTool({ id: update.toolCallId, name: 'title' in update && update.title ? update.title : 'Tool call', status: 'running' }, update)
1076     this.buckets.set(key, [...bucket, {
1077       id: update.toolCallId, role: 'assistant' as const, text: '', status: 'running' as RunStatus, tools: [card],
1078     }])
1079   }
1080   private mergeTool(tool: AgentToolCall, update: acp.ToolCall | acp.ToolCallUpdate): AgentToolCall {
1081     const title = 'title' in update ? update.title : undefined
1082     const result = this.toolResult(update)
1083     const rawInput = 'rawInput' in update ? update.rawInput : undefined
1084     return {
1085       ...tool,
1086       ...(title ? { name: title } : {}),
1087       ...(update.status ? { status: TOOL_STATUS[update.status] ?? 'unknown' } : {}),
1088       ...(result !== undefined ? { result } : {}),
1089       ...(rawInput !== undefined ? { arguments: rawInput } : {}),
1090     }
1091   }
1092   /** Tool content arrives as blocks; the UI shows `String(result)`, so flatten text out of them
1093    * (R10: '3 passed' must be readable, not '[object Object]'). */
1094   private toolResult(update: acp.ToolCall | acp.ToolCallUpdate): string | undefined {
1095     const items = update.content
1096     if (!items?.length) return undefined
1097     return items.map(item => {
1098       if (item.type === 'content') return this.textOf(item.content)
1099       if (item.type === 'diff') return `${item.path}\n${item.oldText ?? ''} → ${item.newText}`
1100       return JSON.stringify(item)
1101     }).join('\n')
1102   }
1103 
1104   // ——— reverse requests (approvals) ————————————————————————————————————————
1105 
1106   /** Interactions are keyed by the native request, never by an option it carries (R14); the answer
1107    * goes back as the wire's own optionId. */
1108   private onRequestPermission(channel: Channel, params: acp.RequestPermissionRequest): Promise<acp.RequestPermissionResponse> {
1109     if (this.isDisposed) return Promise.reject(new Error('ACP connection disposed'))
1110     const key = this.keyFor(channel, params.sessionId)
1111     const id = this.nextId('perm')
1112     // Ownership binds at arrival: the run live RIGHT NOW is the only one that may ever accept the
1113     // answer. An ask that lands while its turn has already settled or already been stopped has no
1114     // accepting run — it is projected already-stranded, never as a submittable pending.
1115     const run = this.openRunOf(key)
1116     const item: AgentInteraction = {
1117       id, sessionId: key, kind: 'approval',
1118       title: params.toolCall.title ?? 'Permission request',
1119       choices: params.options.map(option => ({ id: option.optionId, label: option.name })),
1120       state: run?.status === 'running' ? 'pending' : 'unknown',
1121     }
1122     return new Promise<acp.RequestPermissionResponse>(resolve => {
1123       this.interactions.set(id, { item, settle: resolve, runId: run?.id ?? '' })
1124       this.accept(channel, params.sessionId)
1125       this.publish({ selectedSessionId: this.state.selectedSessionId ?? key })
1126     })
1127   }
1128 }

   1 import type {
   2   AgentCapabilities, AgentClient, AgentInteraction, AgentMessage, AgentSessionInfo, AgentSnapshot,
   3   AgentToolCall, AgentWorkspaceInfo, Availability, InteractionAnswer, RunStatus,
   4 } from '@extensions/ordessa.agent-contracts/contract.js'
   5 import type { AgentNativeBridge } from '../../../../apps/desktop/renderer/agent-native'

   7 export const ORDESSA_ADAPTER_ID = 'ordessa.agent-server'
   8 /** The connector id is frozen from the authenticated hello before registration, so the Server instance is part of the identity every layer keys on. */
   9 export const connectorIdFor = (serverInstanceId: string): string => `ordessa:${serverInstanceId}`
  10 
  11 type Value = Record<string, unknown>
  12 const object = (value: unknown): Value | undefined => value && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value) ? value as Value : undefined
  13 const str = (value: unknown): string | undefined => typeof value === 'string' && value ? value : undefined
  14 const int = (value: unknown): number | undefined => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isInteger(value) ? value : undefined
  15 const errorText = (error: unknown): string => error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
  16 /** Strips only the IPC wrapper Electron adds around a native rejection; the message itself is already secret-free. */
  17 export const unwrapBridgeError = (error: unknown): string =>
  18   errorText(error).replace(/^Error invoking remote method '[^']+':\s*(?:Error:\s*)?/, '')
  19 /** The native half's prefix for a project the Server will not run in. Spelled rather than imported: the
  20  * renderer half must never reach the privileged module, which is the only holder of the Server token. */
  21 const PROJECT_INVALID = 'project-invalid'
  22 const isProjectLoss = (error: unknown) => unwrapBridgeError(error).startsWith(`${PROJECT_INVALID}:`)
  23 
  24 export interface DeclaredCapability { id: string; supported: boolean; reason?: string }
  25 /** What the authenticated `server.hello` proved before this connector was registered. */
  26 export interface ServerIdentity {
  27   serverInstanceId: string
  28   protocolVersion: string
  29   capabilities: DeclaredCapability[]
  30   harnesses: { id: string }[]
  31 }
  32 
  33 export function parseIdentity(value: unknown): ServerIdentity {
  34   const raw = object(value)
  35   const serverInstanceId = str(raw?.serverInstanceId), protocolVersion = str(raw?.protocolVersion)
  36   if (!serverInstanceId || !protocolVersion) throw new Error('Ordessa Server identity is incomplete')
  37   return {
  38     serverInstanceId, protocolVersion,
  39     capabilities: Array.isArray(raw?.capabilities) ? (raw?.capabilities as DeclaredCapability[]) : [],
  40     harnesses: Array.isArray(raw?.harnesses) ? (raw?.harnesses as { id: string }[]) : [],
  41   }
  42 }
  43 
  44 /** Only the routes this product really calls count; event kinds belong to the wire/1 projection the hello already gated. */
  45 export function capabilitiesOf(identity: ServerIdentity): AgentCapabilities {
  46   const at = (method: string): Availability =>
  47     identity.capabilities.some(item => item.id === method && item.supported) ? 'supported' : 'unsupported'
  48   const projects = at('workspaces.list') === 'supported' && at('workspaces.open') === 'supported'
  49   return {
  50     history: at('history.snapshot'),
  51     reasoning: 'supported',
  52     tools: 'supported',
  53     stop: at('runs.stop'),
  54     // FC-0041: this one enum covers approvals, so it must not be downgraded because ordinary input kinds have no route.
  55     interactions: at('approvals.decide'),
  56     models: 'unsupported',
  57     modes: 'unsupported',
  58     workspaces: projects ? 'supported' : 'unsupported',
  59   }
  60 }
  61 
  62 const RUN_STATES: Record<string, RunStatus> = {
  63   queued: 'starting', dispatched: 'starting', running: 'running', stopping: 'stop-requested',
  64   stopped: 'cancelled', completed: 'completed', failed: 'failed', unknown: 'unknown',
  65 }
  66 const TOOL_STATES: Record<string, AgentToolCall['status']> = {
  67   requested: 'running', running: 'running', awaiting_approval: 'running',
  68   completed: 'completed', failed: 'failed', denied: 'failed',
  69 }
  70 const APPROVAL_CHOICES = [{ id: 'allow', label: 'Allow' }, { id: 'deny', label: 'Deny' }] as const
  71 /** Kinds wire/1 defines that this UI has no slot for yet — known, so they never read as version drift. */
  72 const UNPROJECTED_KINDS = new Set(['usage.updated', 'plan.updated', 'mode.updated', 'queue.updated', 'config.changed', 'workspace.connection'])
  73 
  74 const operationDetail = (detail: unknown): string | undefined => {
  75   if (typeof detail === 'string') return detail || undefined
  76   if (!Array.isArray(detail)) return undefined
  77   const lines = detail.map(object).filter(Boolean)
  78     .map(item => `${str(item?.label) ?? ''}${item && str(item.label) ? ': ' : ''}${str(item?.value) ?? ''}`)
  79     .filter(line => line.trim())
  80   return lines.length ? lines.join('\n') : undefined
  81 }
  82 
  83 /** Opens the privileged adapter, performs the authenticated handshake and returns the identity to register under. */
  84 export async function requestServerIdentity(bridge: AgentNativeBridge): Promise<ServerIdentity> {
  85   const instanceId = await bridge.open(ORDESSA_ADAPTER_ID)
  86   try {
  87     return parseIdentity(await bridge.send(instanceId, { method: 'identity' }))
  88   } finally {
  89     await bridge.close(instanceId).catch(() => undefined)
  90   }
  91 }

  95   frames?: unknown[]

  97   olderCursor?: string
  98   reason?: string

 101 export class OrdessaClient implements AgentClient {
 102   isDisposed = false
 103   private instanceId = ''
 104   private identity: ServerIdentity
 105   private readonly unsubscribe: () => void
 106   private listeners = new Set<() => void>()
 107   private state: AgentSnapshot
 108   private activeAssistant = new Map<string, string>()
 109   private tools = new Map<string, Map<string, AgentToolCall>>()
 110   /** Session -> the execution id the Server reported as open; only that id may be stopped. */
 111   private openRuns = new Map<string, string>()
 112   private approvalRoutes = new Map<string, { version: number; used: boolean }>()
 113   private resumeCursors = new Map<string, string>()
 114   private streamedSession: string | undefined
 115 
 116   private constructor(private bridge: AgentNativeBridge, connectorId: string, identity: ServerIdentity) {
 117     this.identity = identity
 118     this.state = {
 119       connection: { id: connectorId, title: 'Ordessa Server', status: 'connecting',
 120         capabilities: capabilitiesOf(identity), serverInstanceId: identity.serverInstanceId },
 121       sessions: [], sessionList: 'unknown', messages: {}, runs: {}, interactions: [], options: [],
 122       workspaces: { state: 'unknown', items: [] },
 123     }
 124     this.unsubscribe = bridge.subscribe(event => {
 125       if (event.instanceId !== this.instanceId || this.isDisposed) return
 126       this.receive(event.error ? { method: 'ordessa/down', params: { reason: event.error } } : event.frame)
 127     })
 128   }
 129 
 130   /** The id was already frozen before registration; a different Server behind the same URL must never inherit it. */
 131   static async connect(bridge: AgentNativeBridge, connectorId: string, serverInstanceId: string): Promise<OrdessaClient> {
 132     const client = new OrdessaClient(bridge, connectorId, { serverInstanceId, protocolVersion: 'wire/1', capabilities: [], harnesses: [] })
 133     try {
 134       client.instanceId = await bridge.open(ORDESSA_ADAPTER_ID)
 135       const identity = parseIdentity(await client.request('identity'))
 136       if (identity.serverInstanceId !== serverInstanceId) throw new Error('Ordessa Server identity changed; reconnect the new Server as its own connection')
 137       client.identity = identity
 138       client.state = { ...client.state, connection: { ...client.state.connection, status: 'connected', capabilities: capabilitiesOf(identity) } }
 139       for (const listener of client.listeners) listener()
 140       return client
 141     } catch (error) {
 142       client.dispose()
 143       throw new Error(unwrapBridgeError(error))
 144     }
 145   }
 146 
 147   getSnapshot = () => this.state
 148   subscribe = (listener: () => void) => { this.listeners.add(listener); return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener) } }
 149   private publish(patch: Partial<AgentSnapshot>) {
 150     if (this.isDisposed) return
 151     this.state = { ...this.state, ...patch }
 152     for (const listener of this.listeners) listener()
 153   }
 154   private request<T>(method: string, params: Value = {}): Promise<T> {
 155     if (this.isDisposed || !this.instanceId) return Promise.reject(new Error('Ordessa connection unavailable'))
 156     let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>
 157     return Promise.race([this.bridge.send(this.instanceId, { method, params }) as Promise<T>,
 158       // A timeout never proves the request failed, so callers must keep the request id instead of re-issuing.
 159       new Promise<T>((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`Ordessa operation timed out: ${method}`)), 30_000) }),
 160     ]).finally(() => clearTimeout(timer))
 161   }
 162 
 163   private upsert(sessionId: string, message: AgentMessage) {
 164     const current = this.state.messages[sessionId] ?? []
 165     const index = current.findIndex(item => item.id === message.id), next = [...current]
 166     if (index < 0) next.push(message); else next[index] = message
 167     this.publish({ messages: { ...this.state.messages, [sessionId]: next } })
 168   }
 169   private drop(sessionId: string, id: string) {
 170     const current = this.state.messages[sessionId]
 171     if (!current?.some(item => item.id === id)) return
 172     this.publish({ messages: { ...this.state.messages, [sessionId]: current.filter(item => item.id !== id) } })
 173   }
 174   private patch(sessionId: string, id: string, change: (previous: AgentMessage) => AgentMessage) {
 175     const previous = this.state.messages[sessionId]?.find(item => item.id === id) ?? { id, role: 'assistant' as const, text: '' }
 176     this.upsert(sessionId, change(previous))
 177   }
 178   private mergeTool(sessionId: string, update: { id: string; name?: string; result?: string; status?: AgentToolCall['status'] }): AgentMessage {
 179     const cards = this.tools.get(sessionId) ?? new Map<string, AgentToolCall>()
 180     this.tools.set(sessionId, cards)
 181     const previous = cards.get(update.id)
 182     // A late running update must not regress a card the Server already settled.
 183     const sticky = previous && (previous.status === 'completed' || previous.status === 'failed') && update.status === 'running'
 184     const next: AgentToolCall = { id: update.id, name: update.name ?? previous?.name ?? 'Tool',
 185       arguments: previous?.arguments, result: sticky ? previous.result : update.result ?? previous?.result,
 186       status: sticky ? previous.status : update.status ?? previous?.status ?? 'running' }
 187     cards.set(update.id, next)
 188     return { id: `ordessa-tool-${update.id}`, role: 'assistant', text: '', tools: [next] }
 189   }
 190   private updateRun(id: string, sessionId: string, status: RunStatus, reason?: string) {
 191     const previous = this.state.runs[id]
 192     if (previous && !['starting', 'running', 'stop-requested'].includes(previous.status)) return
 193     const stoppable = this.state.connection.capabilities.stop === 'supported' && (status === 'starting' || status === 'running')
 194     this.publish({ runs: { ...this.state.runs, [id]: { id, sessionId, status, stoppable } } })
 195     if (status === 'completed' || status === 'failed' || status === 'cancelled' || status === 'unknown') {
 196       if (this.openRuns.get(sessionId) === id) this.openRuns.delete(sessionId)
 197       // The execution's own terminal state is the only verdict the wire offers, and no assistant
 198       // card may keep streaming after it; `stopReason` is absent from wire/1 by design.
 199       const messages = this.state.messages[sessionId]
 200       if (messages?.some(message => message.status === 'running' || message.status === 'starting')) {
 201         this.publish({ messages: { ...this.state.messages, [sessionId]: messages.map(message =>
 202           message.status === 'running' || message.status === 'starting' ? { ...message, status } : message) } })
 203       }
 204       if (status === 'failed' && reason) this.publish({ diagnostic: `Ordessa run failed (${reason})` })
 205     }
 206   }
 207   private setInteraction(interactionId: string, state: AgentInteraction['state']) {
 208     this.publish({ interactions: this.state.interactions.map(item => item.id === interactionId ? { ...item, state } : item) })
 209   }
 210 
 211   private receive(frame: unknown) {
 212     const wrapper = object(frame)
 213     if (!wrapper || !str(wrapper.method)) { this.publish({ diagnostic: 'Unknown Ordessa native event' }); return }
 214     if (wrapper.method === 'ordessa/down') { this.channelDown(str(object(wrapper.params)?.reason) ?? 'closed'); return }
 215     if (wrapper.method !== 'ordessa/event') { this.publish({ diagnostic: 'Unknown Ordessa native event' }); return }
 216     const params = object(wrapper.params), envelope = object(params?.frame), body = object(envelope?.event)
 217     const sessionId = str(params?.sessionId)
 218     if (!sessionId || !body || !str(body.kind)) { this.publish({ diagnostic: 'Unroutable Ordessa event' }); return }
 219     const cursor = str(envelope?.cursor)
 220     // Only the live `w1:` cursor may resume; an older-history cursor is deliberately never stored here.
 221     if (cursor) this.resumeCursors.set(sessionId, cursor)
 222     this.project(sessionId, body)
 223   }
 224 
 225   private channelDown(reason: string) {
 226     this.streamedSession = undefined
 227     // Every booked run loses its only settlement channel at once, so none may keep claiming a verdict.
 228     for (const [sessionId, runId] of [...this.openRuns]) this.updateRun(runId, sessionId, 'unknown')
 229     this.openRuns.clear()
 230     // With no settlement channel, a pending approval can no longer be shown as answerable or re-answered safely.
 231     const pending = this.state.interactions.filter(item => item.state === 'pending' || item.state === 'responding')
 232     for (const item of pending) this.approvalRoutes.delete(item.id)
 233     this.publish({ interactions: this.state.interactions.map(item =>
 234       item.state === 'pending' || item.state === 'responding' ? { ...item, state: 'unknown' } : item) })
 235     this.publish({ diagnostic: `Ordessa event stream stopped (${reason}); an unconfirmed run outcome stays unknown` })
 236   }
 237 
 238   private project(sessionId: string, body: Value) {
 239     const kind = str(body.kind) ?? ''
 240     if (kind === 'message.delta') {
 241       const messageId = str(body.messageId)
 242       if (!messageId) return
 243       this.activeAssistant.set(sessionId, messageId)
 244       this.patch(sessionId, messageId, previous => ({ ...previous, role: 'assistant', text: previous.text + (str(body.text) ?? ''), status: 'running' }))
 245       return
 246     }
 247     if (kind === 'message.final') {
 248       const messageId = str(body.messageId)
 249       if (!messageId) return
 250       if (str(body.displayKind) === 'hidden') { this.drop(sessionId, messageId); return }
 251       const role = str(body.role)
 252       // The contract has no slot for a system message, so one is not silently relabelled as an assistant turn.
 253       if (role !== 'user' && role !== 'assistant') return
 254       this.activeAssistant.delete(sessionId)
 255       this.upsert(sessionId, { id: messageId, role, text: str(body.text) ?? '',
 256         status: role === 'assistant' ? this.state.runs[messageId]?.status : undefined })
 257       return
 258     }
 259     if (kind === 'thought.delta') {
 260       const id = this.activeAssistant.get(sessionId)
 261       if (id) this.patch(sessionId, id, previous => ({ ...previous, reasoning: (previous.reasoning ?? '') + (str(body.text) ?? '') }))
 262       return
 263     }
 264     if (kind === 'tool.update') {
 265       const toolCallId = str(body.toolCallId)
 266       if (!toolCallId) return
 267       this.upsert(sessionId, this.mergeTool(sessionId, { id: toolCallId,
 268         name: typeof body.tool === 'string' ? body.tool : str(object(body.tool)?.name),
 269         result: str(body.resultExcerpt) ?? str(body.summary), status: TOOL_STATES[str(body.state) ?? ''] ?? 'unknown' }))
 270       return
 271     }
 272     if (kind === 'approval.requested') { this.approvalRequested(sessionId, body); return }
 273     if (kind === 'approval.settled') {
 274       const approvalId = str(body.approvalId)
 275       if (!approvalId) return
 276       const outcome = str(body.outcome)
 277       const state: AgentInteraction['state'] = outcome === 'allowed' || outcome === 'denied' ? 'resolved' : outcome === 'expired' ? 'expired' : 'unknown'
 278       this.approvalRoutes.delete(approvalId)
 279       this.publish({ interactions: this.state.interactions.map(item =>
 280         item.id === approvalId && item.state !== 'resolved' ? { ...item, state } : item) })
 281       return
 282     }
 283     if (kind === 'execution.state') {
 284       const executionId = str(body.executionId)
 285       const state = str(body.state)
 286       if (!executionId || !state) return
 287       const status = RUN_STATES[state] ?? 'unknown'
 288       if (['running', 'queued', 'dispatched'].includes(state)) this.openRuns.set(sessionId, executionId)
 289       this.updateRun(executionId, sessionId, status, str(body.reason))
 290       return
 291     }
 292     if (!UNPROJECTED_KINDS.has(kind)) this.publish({ diagnostic: `Unknown Ordessa event kind: ${kind}` })
 293   }
 294 
 295   private approvalRequested(sessionId: string, body: Value) {
 296     const approval = object(body.approval)
 297     const approvalId = str(approval?.approvalId), version = int(approval?.version)
 298     if (!approvalId) { this.publish({ diagnostic: 'An Ordessa approval arrived without an identity' }); return }
 299     const operation = object(approval?.operation)
 300     const answerable = this.state.connection.capabilities.interactions === 'supported' && version !== undefined
 301     const item: AgentInteraction = { id: approvalId, sessionId, turnId: str(approval?.executionId), kind: 'approval',
 302       title: str(operation?.title) ?? 'Approval required', detail: operationDetail(operation?.detail),
 303       ...(answerable ? { choices: APPROVAL_CHOICES.map(choice => ({ ...choice })), state: 'pending' as const } : { state: 'unknown' as const }) }
 304     if (answerable) this.approvalRoutes.set(approvalId, { version: version as number, used: false })
 305     else this.publish({ diagnostic: 'An approval arrived but this Server cannot record a decision; it is not answerable' })
 306     this.publish({ interactions: [...this.state.interactions.filter(previous => previous.id !== approvalId), item] })
 307   }
 308 
 309   async refreshSessions() {
 310     this.publish({ sessionList: 'loading' })
 311     try {
 312       const sessions = await this.request<AgentSessionInfo[]>('sessions')
 313       this.publish({ sessions, sessionList: 'ready' })
 314     } catch (error) { this.publish({ sessionList: 'error', diagnostic: errorText(error) }); throw error }
 315   }
 316 
 317   /** A session exists only once the Server has accepted a first send, so there is nothing to create here. */
 318   async newSession(): Promise<string> {
 319     throw new Error('An Ordessa session is created by the first send into a project')
 320   }
 321 
 322   async openSession(id: string) {
 323     this.publish({ selectedSessionId: id })
 324     // Rebuilding from the Server's own log, so a reopen cannot inherit a half-projected live run.
 325     this.tools.delete(id)
 326     this.activeAssistant.delete(id)
 327     await this.loadHistory(id)
 328     await this.subscribeStream(id)
 329   }
 330 
 331   private async loadHistory(sessionId: string) {
 332     let snapshot = await this.request<HistoryResult>('history', { sessionId })
 333     if (snapshot.outcome !== 'snapshot') {
 334       this.publish({ diagnostic: `Ordessa history asked for a resync (${snapshot.reason ?? 'no reason'}); reading from the newest event` })
 335       this.resumeCursors.delete(sessionId)
 336       snapshot = await this.request<HistoryResult>('history', { sessionId })
 337       if (snapshot.outcome !== 'snapshot') throw new Error('Ordessa history could not be read; the session stays unopened')
 338     }
 339     this.publish({ messages: { ...this.state.messages, [sessionId]: [] } })
 340     for (const item of snapshot.frames ?? []) this.receive({ method: 'ordessa/event', params: { sessionId, frame: item } })
 341     // The snapshot's join point is the newest cursor of the read; replayed frames must not pull it backwards,
 342     // or the subscription would re-deliver what was just projected.
 343     this.resumeCursors.set(sessionId, snapshot.resumeCursor ?? '')
 344   }
 345 
 346   private async subscribeStream(sessionId: string) {
 347     if (this.streamedSession && this.streamedSession !== sessionId) {
 348       await this.request('closeStream', { sessionId: this.streamedSession }).catch(() => undefined)
 349       this.streamedSession = undefined
 350     }
 351     const cursor = this.resumeCursors.get(sessionId)
 352     await this.request('stream', { sessionId, ...(cursor ? { cursor } : {}) })
 353     this.streamedSession = sessionId
 354   }
 355 
 356   async refreshWorkspaces() {
 357     const previous = this.state.workspaces
 358     this.publish({ workspaces: { state: 'loading', items: previous?.items ?? [] } })
 359     try {
 360       const items = await this.request<AgentWorkspaceInfo[]>('projects')
 361       const selected = previous?.selectedWorkspaceId
 362       const kept = selected !== undefined && items.some(item => item.id === selected)
 363       this.publish({ workspaces: { state: 'ready', items, ...(kept ? { selectedWorkspaceId: selected } : {}) } })
 364       if (selected !== undefined && !kept) this.publish({ diagnostic: 'The selected project is no longer offered by this Server; choose a project again' })
 365     } catch (error) {
 366       this.publish({ workspaces: { state: 'error', items: [] }, diagnostic: errorText(error) })
 367       throw error
 368     }
 369   }
 370 
 371   /** The native half re-validates identity and archived state, so a stale selection can never read as openable. */
 372   async openWorkspace(id: string): Promise<AgentWorkspaceInfo> {
 373     const previous = this.state.workspaces
 374     const base = { state: 'ready' as const, items: previous?.items ?? [] }
 375     try {
 376       const opened = await this.request<AgentWorkspaceInfo>('openProject', { id })
 377       this.publish({ workspaces: { ...base, selectedWorkspaceId: opened.id } })
 378       return opened
 379     } catch (error) {
 380       // FC-0032: a refused revalidation clears the selection instead of leaving a pick that could still unlock Send.
 381       this.publish({ workspaces: base, diagnostic: errorText(error) })
 382       throw error
 383     }
 384   }
 385 
 386   async addWorkspace(path: string): Promise<AgentWorkspaceInfo> {
 387     const added = await this.request<AgentWorkspaceInfo>('addProject', { path })
 388     await this.refreshWorkspaces()
 389     return this.openWorkspace(added.id)
 390   }
 391 
 392   async createAndSend(workspaceId: string, text: string, requestId: string): Promise<{ sessionId: string }> {
 393     // The native half resolves only after an accepted turn with a real session id, so nothing below runs on a guess.
 394     let sent: Value
 395     try {
 396       sent = await this.request<Value>('createAndSend', { workspaceId, text, requestId })
 397     } catch (error) {
 398       // FC-0057: a project the Server had already dropped cannot take this turn, and leaving it selected keeps
 399       // the composer advertising a sendable draft. The text and request id belong to the upper layer, which
 400       // holds both; only the stale pick is this half's to clear.
 401       if (isProjectLoss(error)) {
 402         const previous = this.state.workspaces
 403         // A pick the user changed while this send was in flight is not the stale one this refusal names.
 404         if (previous?.selectedWorkspaceId === workspaceId) {
