 405           this.publish({ workspaces: { state: 'ready', items: previous.items }, diagnostic: unwrapBridgeError(error) })
 406         }
 407       }
 408       throw error
 409     }
 410     const sessionId = str(sent.sessionId)
 411     if (!sessionId) throw new Error('Ordessa first send returned no session identity')
 412     const listed = await this.request<AgentSessionInfo[]>('sessions').catch(() => undefined)
 413     const confirmed = listed?.find(item => item.id === sessionId)
 414     // The Server's own record owns the binding, and it names the project by id or by its path (the same
 415     // pair the F2 facade accepts). A session under any other project is not this draft's success.
 416     const project = this.state.workspaces?.items.find(item => item.id === workspaceId)
 417     if (confirmed?.workspaceId && confirmed.workspaceId !== workspaceId && confirmed.workspaceId !== project?.normalizedPath)
 418       throw new Error('Ordessa first send returned a session bound to a different project')
 419     // A list read that has not caught up yet must not erase the session the Server just confirmed.
 420     const sessions = listed?.some(item => item.id === sessionId) ? listed
 421       : [{ id: sessionId, title: sessionId, workspaceId }, ...(listed ?? [])]
 422     this.publish({ sessions, ...(listed ? { sessionList: 'ready' as const } : {}) })
 423     await this.openSession(sessionId)
 424     this.publish({ workspaces: { ...(this.state.workspaces ?? { state: 'ready', items: [] }), state: 'ready', selectedWorkspaceId: workspaceId } })
 425     return { sessionId }
 426   }
 427 
 428   async send(sessionId: string, text: string) {
 429     if (!text.trim()) return
 430     const sent = await this.request<Value>('send', { sessionId, text })
 431     const outcome = str(sent.outcome), executionId = str(sent.executionId)
 432     if (outcome !== 'accepted') { this.publish({ diagnostic: `Ordessa send outcome was ${outcome ?? 'missing'}` }); return }
 433     // A queued turn has no execution yet, so no run is booked for it here.
 434     if (executionId) { this.openRuns.set(sessionId, executionId); this.updateRun(executionId, sessionId, 'starting') }
 435   }
 436 
 437   async stop(sessionId: string, runId: string) {
 438     const run = this.state.runs[runId]
 439     if (!run || run.sessionId !== sessionId || !run.stoppable) throw new Error('Ordessa run is not available to stop')
 440     this.updateRun(runId, sessionId, 'stop-requested')
 441     try {
 442       const stopped = await this.request<Value>('stop', { sessionId, executionId: runId })
 443       const outcome = str(stopped.outcome)
 444       // `stop_requested` never verdicts; only a later execution.state can.
 445       if (outcome !== 'stop_requested') {
 446         this.updateRun(runId, sessionId, 'unknown')
 447         this.publish({ diagnostic: `Ordessa stop was not confirmed (${outcome ?? 'no outcome'})` })
 448       }
 449     } catch (error) {
 450       this.updateRun(runId, sessionId, 'unknown')
 451       throw error
 452     }
 453   }
 454 
 455   async respond(interactionId: string, answer: InteractionAnswer) {
 456     const route = this.approvalRoutes.get(interactionId)
 457     const item = this.state.interactions.find(value => value.id === interactionId)
 458     // Only a real approval with a real route is answerable; ordinary input kinds never get one (FC-0041).
 459     if (!route || !item || item.kind !== 'approval' || item.state !== 'pending' || route.used) {
 460       throw new Error('Ordessa interaction is not an answerable approval')
 461     }
 462     const decision = answer.kind === 'choice' ? answer.choiceId : undefined
 463     if ((decision !== 'allow' && decision !== 'deny') || !item.choices?.some(choice => choice.id === decision)) {
 464       throw new Error('An Ordessa approval is answered with allow or deny only')
 465     }
 466     route.used = true
 467     this.setInteraction(interactionId, 'responding')
 468     try {
 469       const result = await this.request<Value>('decide', { approvalId: interactionId, expectedVersion: route.version, decision, scope: { kind: 'once' } })
 470       const outcome = str(result.outcome)
 471       this.approvalRoutes.delete(interactionId)
 472       if (outcome === 'recorded' || outcome === 'already_recorded') this.setInteraction(interactionId, 'resolved')
 473       else if (outcome === 'invalid') {
 474         this.setInteraction(interactionId, 'expired')
 475         this.publish({ diagnostic: `Ordessa rejected the approval decision (${str(result.reason) ?? 'invalid'})` })
 476       } else {
 477         this.setInteraction(interactionId, 'unknown')
 478         this.publish({ diagnostic: 'Ordessa approval decision outcome is unknown' })
 479       }
 480     } catch (error) {
 481       // A version conflict proves the record moved elsewhere; only its settled event can say where.
 482       this.approvalRoutes.delete(interactionId)
 483       this.setInteraction(interactionId, 'unknown')
 484       this.publish({ diagnostic: errorText(error) })
 485       throw error
 486     }
 487   }
 488 
 489   /** Provider and model selection is design-only in this product, so no option is offered or faked. */
 490   async setOption(id: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
 491     throw new Error(`Ordessa does not offer the ${id} option`)
 492   }
 493 
 494   dispose() {
 495     if (this.isDisposed) return
 496     const sessionId = this.streamedSession
 497     this.isDisposed = true
 498     this.unsubscribe()
 499     this.listeners.clear()
 500     this.approvalRoutes.clear(); this.tools.clear(); this.openRuns.clear()
 501     this.activeAssistant.clear(); this.resumeCursors.clear()
 502     this.streamedSession = undefined
 503     // Released before the disposed flag, because a disposed client refuses its own requests.
 504     if (this.instanceId) {
 505       if (sessionId) void this.bridge.send(this.instanceId, { method: 'closeStream', params: { sessionId } }).catch(() => undefined)
 506       void this.bridge.close(this.instanceId).catch(() => undefined)
 507     }
 508   }
 509 }

### M:plugins/agent/sessions/src/model.ts
   1 import type { ResourceScope } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
   2 import type {
   3   AgentAttachmentPreparation, AgentClient, AgentCommandCatalog, AgentConnections, AgentPreparedAttachment,
   4   AgentSessions, AgentSnapshot, AgentSubmissionOutcome, AgentWorkspaceSnapshot,
   5 } from '@extensions/ordessa.agent-contracts/contract.js'
   6 
   7 /** Thin delegate facade: client holding, selection, and reconnection live in the connections
   8  * service workspace (P2-1 extraction); session operations project through its selected client.
   9  * CP-SESSION-001 (FC-0021): the draft and the per-Server project gate are owned here —
  10  * New session never reaches a client, and first send goes through createAndSend with one
  11  * requestId held until accepted. */
  12 export function createAgentSessions(lifetime: ResourceScope, connections: AgentConnections): AgentSessions {
  13   void lifetime // workspace ownership moved to the connections service scope (P2-1)
  14   const workspace = connections.workspace
  15   interface ConnState { draftActive: boolean; draftEndedBy?: 'discarded' | 'opened'; requestId?: string; restoreFailed: boolean }
  16   const states = new Map<string, ConnState>()
  17   const stateFor = (id: string) => { let s = states.get(id); if (!s) states.set(id, s = { draftActive: false, restoreFailed: false }); return s }
  18   // Non-secret UI selection: last valid project per authenticated Server instance (origin+serverId),
  19   // mirrored to renderer localStorage so the choice survives a desktop restart (FC-0030). Never keyed
  20   // by plugin id or bare origin; without identity there is no restore path at all.
  21   const lastProject = new Map<string, string>()
  22   // Prepared attachments owned through the selected connection (R-Z2-2 plugin
  23   // half): the registry maps the opaque token Chat carries to the full
  24   // prepared reference plus the session it was prepared against, and an entry
  25   // exists only while the port still owns the content. Keys are
  26   // connection-scoped — a reference prepared on one connection is never
  27   // resolvable on another.
  28   const preparedAttachments = new Map<string, { reference: AgentPreparedAttachment; sessionId: string }>()
  29   const preparedKey = (connectionId: string, preparedId: string) => `${connectionId}|${preparedId}`
  30   const linkDownOf = (client: AgentClient) => {
  31     const status = client.getSnapshot().connection.status
  32     return status === 'disconnected' || status === 'error'
  33   }
  34   const storageKey = (identity: string) => `ordessa.agent.project.${identity}`
  35   const rememberProject = (identity: string, id: string) => {
  36     lastProject.set(identity, id)
  37     try { globalThis.localStorage?.setItem(storageKey(identity), id) } catch { /* the gate revalidates on open regardless */ }
  38   }
  39   const forgetProject = (identity: string) => {
  40     lastProject.delete(identity)
  41     try { globalThis.localStorage?.removeItem(storageKey(identity)) } catch { /* best effort */ }
  42   }
  43   const recallProject = (identity: string) => {
  44     const remembered = lastProject.get(identity)
  45     if (remembered) return remembered
  46     try {
  47       const stored = globalThis.localStorage?.getItem(storageKey(identity))
  48       if (stored) { lastProject.set(identity, stored); return stored }
  49     } catch { /* storage unavailable: manual selection only */ }
  50     return undefined
  51   }
  52   const identityOf = (client: AgentClient) => client.getSnapshot().connection.serverInstanceId
  53   const supported = (agent: AgentSnapshot | undefined) => agent?.connection.capabilities.workspaces === 'supported'
  54 
  55   async function revalidate(connectionId: string) {
  56     const state = stateFor(connectionId)
  57     state.restoreFailed = false
  58     let client: AgentClient
  59     try { client = workspace.selected() } catch { return }
  60     if (!client.openWorkspace || !supported(client.getSnapshot())) return
  61     const identity = identityOf(client)
  62     if (!identity) return // no authenticated instance identity: require a manual selection, never a stale unlock
  63     const snapshot = client.getSnapshot()
  64     const saved = recallProject(identity)
  65     if (!saved) {
  66       const current = snapshot.workspaces?.selectedWorkspaceId
  67       if (current) rememberProject(identity, current)
  68       return
  69     }
  70     // The stored id must still be listed unarchived by this Server before it may be opened (FC-0030).
  71     if (snapshot.workspaces?.state === 'ready' && !snapshot.workspaces.items.some(item => item.id === saved)) {
  72       state.restoreFailed = true; forgetProject(identity)
  73       return
  74     }
  75     try { await client.openWorkspace(saved) }
  76     catch { state.restoreFailed = true; forgetProject(identity) } // invalid: clear the record and block sends
  77   }
  78 
  79   const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
  80   workspace.subscribe(notify)
  81   function notify() { cached = null; for (const listener of [...listeners]) listener() }
  82 
  83   let cached: { base: AgentWorkspaceSnapshot; signature: string; derived: AgentWorkspaceSnapshot } | null = null
  84   const signature = (base: AgentWorkspaceSnapshot) => {
  85     const state = base.selectedConnectionId ? stateFor(base.selectedConnectionId) : undefined
  86     return `${base.selectedConnectionId ?? '-'}|${state?.draftActive}|${state?.draftEndedBy}|${state?.restoreFailed}|${base.agent?.connection.capabilities.workspaces}|${base.agent?.workspaces?.selectedWorkspaceId}|${observedGeneration() ?? '-'}`
  87   }
  88   /** PC-5: the selected client's backend-confirmed runtime generation, when it
  89    * can observe one. An absent client member or no selection is itself the
  90    * evidence answer: no generation is invented from UI counters. */
  91   const observedGeneration = (): number | undefined => {
  92     try { return workspace.selected().admissionEvidence?.()?.runtimeGeneration } catch { return undefined }
  93   }
  94   function derive(base: AgentWorkspaceSnapshot): AgentWorkspaceSnapshot {
  95     if (!base.selectedConnectionId) return base
  96     const state = stateFor(base.selectedConnectionId)
  97     const block = !supported(base.agent) ? 'unsupported' as const
  98       : state.restoreFailed ? 'project-invalid' as const
  99       : !base.agent?.workspaces?.selectedWorkspaceId ? 'no-project' as const : undefined
 100     const generation = observedGeneration()
 101     return { ...base, ...(generation === undefined ? {} : { runtimeGeneration: generation }), draft: {
 102       active: state.draftActive,
 103       workspaceId: base.agent?.workspaces?.selectedWorkspaceId,
 104       canSend: block === undefined,
 105       ...(block ? { blockReason: block } : {}),
 106       ...(state.draftEndedBy ? { endedBy: state.draftEndedBy } : {}),
 107     } }
 108   }
 109   const gate = () => {
 110     const snapshot = derive(workspace.getSnapshot())
 111     if (!snapshot.draft?.canSend) throw Error(`Agent project gate: ${snapshot.draft?.blockReason ?? 'closed'}`)
 112     return snapshot
 113   }
 114 
 115   return {
 116     getSnapshot: () => {
 117       const base = workspace.getSnapshot()
 118       const next = signature(base)
 119       if (cached && cached.base === base && cached.signature === next) return cached.derived
 120       cached = { base, signature: next, derived: derive(base) }
 121       return cached.derived
 122     },
 123     subscribe: listener => { listeners.add(listener); return () => { listeners.delete(listener) } },
 124     selectConnection: async id => { await workspace.selectConnection(id); await revalidate(id) },
 125     reconnect: async id => { await workspace.reconnect(id); await revalidate(id) },
 126     refreshSessions: () => workspace.selected().refreshSessions(),
 127     // Legacy passthrough kept type-compatible for the direct prototypes; the CP UI never calls it (FC-0021).
 128     newSession: () => workspace.selected().newSession().then(() => undefined),
 129     openSession: id => {
 130       const connId = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId
 131       const state = connId ? stateFor(connId) : undefined
 132       // Opening a session ends an active draft as a step-away (C-0030): the draft may be
 133       // resumed by a later startDraft with its own semantics; nothing is sent on this path.
 134       if (state?.draftActive) { state.draftActive = false; state.draftEndedBy = 'opened' }
 135       return workspace.selected().openSession(id)
 136     },
 137     send: async (text, attachments?) => {
 138       const client = workspace.selected()
 139       const connectionId = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId ?? '-'
 140       const state = stateFor(connectionId)
 141       // C-0030: while a draft is active its send is exactly one createAndSend — a previously
 142       // selected session must never capture the draft's first text. Continuation only applies
 143       // outside a draft, and keeps that session's own bound project.
 144       if (!state.draftActive) {
 145         const sessionId = client.getSnapshot().selectedSessionId
 146         if (!sessionId) throw Error('No Agent session selected')
 147         // Fail-closed admission precheck (014 P-C PC-4): a client that can
 148         // observe the backend admission facts and reports them not-ready
 149         // refuses BEFORE any wire traffic — the same discipline as the
 150         // Server's controlled next-submit coordinator. A client with no
 151         // observable admission evidence cannot fail closed here; its outcome
 152         // is the legacy send mapping below (registered limitation, S-05).
 153         const evidence = client.admissionEvidence?.()
 154         if (evidence) {
 155           if (!evidence.admissionSupported || !evidence.q5Ready || !evidence.chatApiReady)
 156             return { kind: 'refused', code: 'CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED', reason: 'ACP backend admission dependencies are unavailable' }
 157           if (evidence.outputInProgress)
 158             return { kind: 'refused', code: 'BUSY', reason: 'ACP output is still in progress' }
 159         }
 160         if (attachments?.length) {
 161           if (!client.submitWithAttachments)
 162             return { kind: 'refused', code: 'CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED', reason: 'attachment carry path is unavailable on this connection' }
 163           const refs: AgentPreparedAttachment[] = []
 164           for (const token of attachments) {
 165             const entry = preparedAttachments.get(preparedKey(connectionId, token))
 166             if (!entry) return { kind: 'refused', code: 'INVALID_REQUEST', reason: 'unknown prepared attachment reference' }
 167             refs.push(entry.reference)
 168           }
 169           try {
 170             return await client.submitWithAttachments(sessionId, text, refs)
 171           } catch (cause) {
 172             if (linkDownOf(client)) return { kind: 'unknown', operationId: globalThis.crypto.randomUUID(),
 173               reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 174             return { kind: 'refused', code: 'SUBMISSION_REFUSED', reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 175           }
 176         }
 177         const outcome = await client.send(sessionId, text).catch((cause: unknown) => {
 178           // Typed mapping of the legacy path (contracts 0.2.0): evidenced
 179           // channel loss is unknown — never a fake acceptance; anything else
 180           // the client refuses arrives as a typed refusal with its reason.
 181           if (linkDownOf(client)) return { kind: 'unknown' as const, operationId: globalThis.crypto.randomUUID(),
 182             reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 183           return { kind: 'refused' as const, code: 'SUBMISSION_REFUSED',
 184             reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 185         })
 186         // Compatibility rule (contracts 0.2.0): a legacy connector resolves with
 187         // void — exactly the old send-path acceptance, never execution success.
 188         return outcome ?? { kind: 'accepted' }
 189       }
 190       if (attachments?.length)
 191         return { kind: 'refused', code: 'CAPABILITY_UNSUPPORTED', reason: 'a draft first send cannot carry prepared attachments' }
 192       gate() // first send is blocked without a revalidated project — never falls back
 193       if (!client.createAndSend) throw Error('Agent project gate: unsupported')
 194       const workspaceId = client.getSnapshot().workspaces?.selectedWorkspaceId!
 195       state.requestId ??= globalThis.crypto.randomUUID() // one requestId held across unknown outcomes; no blind second create
 196       const requestId = state.requestId
 197       try {
 198         const accepted = await client.createAndSend(workspaceId, text, requestId)
 199         // The draft ends only when the accepted real session id is in the snapshot, selected, and bound
 200         // to the chosen project (FC-0031); otherwise input and requestId stay held for an idempotent retry.
 201         const after = client.getSnapshot()
 202         const session = after.sessions.find(item => item.id === accepted?.sessionId)
 203         const project = after.workspaces?.items.find(item => item.id === workspaceId)
 204         const bound = !!session?.workspaceId
 205           && (session.workspaceId === workspaceId || session.workspaceId === project?.normalizedPath)
 206         if (!accepted?.sessionId || !bound || after.selectedSessionId !== accepted.sessionId)
 207           throw Error('Agent first-send session mismatch')
 208         state.requestId = undefined
 209         state.draftActive = false
 210         state.draftEndedBy = undefined // an accepted send ends the draft by selecting the new real session
 211         notify()
 212         return { kind: 'accepted' }
 213       } catch (cause) {
 214         notify()
 215         // The requestId stays held on anything but acceptance: an unknown
 216         // outcome keeps it for reconciliation, a refusal keeps it so the user's
 217         // explicit retry re-uses the same idempotency identity (C-0030/R-Z2-1).
 218         if (linkDownOf(client)) return { kind: 'unknown', operationId: requestId,
 219           reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 220         return { kind: 'refused', code: 'SUBMISSION_REFUSED', reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 221       }
 222     },
 223     commandCatalog: sessionKey => {
 224       let client: AgentClient
 225       try { client = workspace.selected() } catch { return { kind: 'absent' } }
 226       const catalog = client.getNativeCommands?.(sessionKey)
 227       if (!catalog) return { kind: 'absent' } // the connector projects no native catalog
 228       switch (catalog.kind) {
 229         case 'available': return catalog
 230         case 'absent': return { kind: 'absent' }
 231         case 'unknown': return { kind: 'error', reason: catalog.reason }
 232       }
 233     },
 234     attachments: (() => {
 235       const capability = (sessionId?: string) => {
 236         let client: AgentClient
 237         try { client = workspace.selected() } catch
 238           { return { kind: 'absent' as const, reason: '没有已连接的代理，附件不可用。' } }
 239         // All-or-nothing: preparation without the carry path would strand
 240         // content that can never be sent, so partial surfaces stay absent.
 241         if (!client.prepareAttachment || !client.attachmentCapabilities
 242           || !client.releasePreparedAttachment || !client.submitWithAttachments)
 243           return { kind: 'absent' as const, reason: '当前连接未提供附件传输通道（生产 prepare owner 缺席，S-05）。' }
 244         if (!sessionId)
 245           return { kind: 'absent' as const, reason: '新会话还没有原生活动会话，附件在第一条消息发出后可用。' }
 246         return { kind: 'available' as const }
 247       }
 248       return {
 249         capability,
 250         prepare: async ({ sessionId, sourceId, idempotencyKey }) => {
 251           const cap = capability(sessionId)
 252           if (cap.kind !== 'available') return { kind: 'refused', reason: cap.reason }
 253           if (sessionId === undefined) return { kind: 'refused', reason: '新会话还没有原生活动会话，附件在第一条消息发出后可用。' }
 254           const client = workspace.selected()
 255           const connectionId = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId ?? '-'
 256           try {
 257             const reference = await client.prepareAttachment!(sessionId, sourceId, idempotencyKey)
 258             preparedAttachments.set(preparedKey(connectionId, reference.preparedId), { reference, sessionId })
 259             return { kind: 'prepared', reference }
 260           } catch (cause) {
 261             const reason = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)
 262             if (linkDownOf(client)) return { kind: 'unknown', operationId: idempotencyKey }
 263             return { kind: 'refused', reason }
 264           }
 265         },
 266         release: async (preparedId, reason) => {
 267           void reason
 268           const client = workspace.selected()
 269           const connectionId = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId ?? '-'
 270           const key = preparedKey(connectionId, preparedId)
 271           const entry = preparedAttachments.get(key)
 272           if (!entry) return // nothing this facade still owns: release is idempotent
 273           // The registry entry survives a failed release: the port still owns
 274           // the content, so the caller sees the refusal and the reference stays
 275           // reconcilable instead of silently leaking.
 276           await client.releasePreparedAttachment?.(entry.sessionId, preparedId)
 277           preparedAttachments.delete(key)
 278         },
 279       }
 280     })(),
 281     stop: runId => {
 282       const client = workspace.selected(), run = client.getSnapshot().runs[runId]
 283       if (!run) throw Error('Agent run unavailable')
 284       return client.stop(run.sessionId, runId)
 285     },
 286     respond: (id, answer) => workspace.selected().respond(id, answer),
 287     setOption: (id, value) => workspace.selected().setOption(id, value),
 288     // startDraft deliberately leaves the client-side selection untouched: discarding must
 289     // restore the previously selected session (C-0030), and safety comes from send() never
 290     // routing to a selection while a draft is active.
 291     startDraft: () => { const id = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId; if (id) { const state = stateFor(id); state.draftActive = true; state.draftEndedBy = undefined; notify() } },
 292     discardDraft: () => { const id = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId; if (id) { const state = stateFor(id); const wasActive = state.draftActive; state.draftActive = false; state.requestId = undefined; if (wasActive) state.draftEndedBy = 'discarded'; notify() } },
 293     selectWorkspace: async id => {
 294       const client = workspace.selected()
 295       if (!client.openWorkspace) throw Error('Agent project gate: unsupported')
 296       const info = await client.openWorkspace(id)
 297       const identity = identityOf(client)
 298       if (identity) rememberProject(identity, info.id) // a selection without identity is live but never persisted
 299       const connectionId = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId
 300       if (connectionId) stateFor(connectionId).restoreFailed = false
 301     },
 302     addWorkspace: async path => {
 303       const client = workspace.selected()
 304       if (!client.addWorkspace) throw Error('Agent project registration: unsupported')
 305       const info = await client.addWorkspace(path)
 306       const identity = identityOf(client)
 307       if (identity) rememberProject(identity, info.id)
 308       const connectionId = workspace.getSnapshot().selectedConnectionId
 309       if (connectionId) stateFor(connectionId).restoreFailed = false
 310     },
 311     refreshWorkspaces: async () => { await workspace.selected().refreshWorkspaces?.() },
 312   }
 313 }

### M:plugins/chat/frontend/src/views/chat-page.tsx
   1 // Chat page: pane identity, draft store, send controller with stale guards,
   2 // shared +/slash input panel, fixed bottom composer, approval surface and the
   3 // project dialog entry.
   4 //
   5 // Pane discipline (ported from ordessa.agent-conversation view.tsx, the
   6 // FC-0052/FC-0060 line): the draft is a fixed pane marker no session id can
   7 // produce; the remount key, composer buffer and session identity all derive
   8 // from ONE predicate; a draft never routes to the previously selected session
   9 // (the facade owns that gate).
  10 //
  11 // Send discipline (service-adaptation §3 + input-spec C04): the submission
  12 // freezes text/items/revision at capture time; the late result only clears the
  13 // exact sent version — user typing after the send survives; a result arriving
  14 // after a pane switch updates NOTHING in the new pane (X05); unknown keeps the
  15 // draft and never auto-retries.
  16 import { createElement, useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
  17 import type { AgentSessions } from '@extensions/ordessa.agent-contracts/contract.js'
  18 import type {
  19   ChatContributionsService, ChatInputEntry, ChatInputQuery, ChatLocation, ChatPrepareResult, ChatSubmissionResult,
  20   ChatSubmissionSnapshot,
  21 } from '@extensions/ordessa.chat-api/contract.js'
  22 import { DraftStore, attachmentBlockReason } from '../state/draft'
  23 import { useViewState } from '../state/expansion'
  24 import {
  25   attachmentCapabilityOf, createFacadeGateway, interactionsForSession, paneRunState, projectConversation,
  26 } from '../adapters/agent'
  27 import type { ChatTheme } from '../components/messages/markdown-body'
  28 import { ThreadBody, makeDefaultResolver, type ErasedComponent } from './thread'
  29 import { ChatTextInput } from '../components/composer/text-input'
  30 import { InputPanel } from '../components/composer/input-panel'
  31 import { AttachmentStrip } from '../components/composer/attachments'
  32 import { ApprovalPanel } from '../components/interactions/approval-panel'
  33 import { ProjectDialog } from './project-dialog'
  34 import { ConnectionBadge } from './connection-badge'
  35 import { connectionBadgeKey } from '../state/keys'
  36 import { chatStyles } from '../styles'
  37 import { usePreferredTheme } from '../theme'
  38 import { reconcileSlashSnapshot, slashReplacementRange, type SlashTokenSnapshot } from '../components/composer/trigger'
  39 
  40 const draftPaneOf = 'draft'
  41 const sessionPane = (sessionId: string) => `session:${sessionId}`
  42 
  43 const draftBlockCopy: Record<string, string> = {
  44   unsupported: '当前连接不支持在项目中创建会话。',
  45   'no-project': '还没有选择项目，先在弹窗中选择一个项目。',
  46   'project-invalid': '所选项目在该服务上已失效，请重新选择。',
  47 }
  48 
  49 type PanelState = { readonly mode: 'plus'; readonly caret: number } | { readonly mode: 'slash'; readonly token: SlashTokenSnapshot } | null
  50 type PrepareAction = (location: ChatLocation, idempotencyKey: string) => Promise<ChatPrepareResult>
  51 
  52 export function ChatPage({ service, chat, theme: themeProp, openViaOverlay }: {
  53   service: AgentSessions
  54   chat: ChatContributionsService
  55   /** Explicit theme from the host; defaults to the OS preference. */
  56   theme?: ChatTheme
  57   /** Workbench overlay opener for the global dialog (absent → in-page render). */
  58   openViaOverlay?: () => void
  59 }) {
  60   const theme = themeProp ?? usePreferredTheme()
  61   const state = useSyncExternalStore(service.subscribe, service.getSnapshot, service.getSnapshot)
  62   const agent = state.agent
  63   const connectionId = state.selectedConnectionId
  64   const drafting = state.draft?.active === true
  65   const sessionId = agent?.selectedSessionId
  66   const pane = drafting ? draftPaneOf : sessionPane(sessionId ?? '')
  67   // contextRevision bumps on every facade notification: contributions and
  68   // actions capture it and refuse to act on stale locations (contracts.md §3).
  69   const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0)
  70   useEffect(() => service.subscribe(() => setRevision(value => value + 1)), [service])
  71   const store = useMemo(() => new DraftStore(), [])
  72   const expansion = useViewState()
  73   const resolver = useMemo(() => makeDefaultResolver({ [connectionBadgeKey.id]: ConnectionBadge as unknown as ErasedComponent }), [])
  74   const gateway = useMemo(() => createFacadeGateway(service), [service])
  75   const [dialogOpen, setDialogOpen] = useState(false)
  76   const [panel, setPanel] = useState<PanelState>(null)
  77   const [sendError, setSendError] = useState('')
  78   const [pendingPane, setPendingPane] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined)
  79   const [plusQuery, setPlusQuery] = useState('')
  80   const panelKeyRef = useRef<((event: { key: string; preventDefault(): void }) => boolean) | undefined>(undefined)
  81   // add-content retry identity: item id → the preparing action that produced it,
  82   // so a retry re-runs the SAME preparation with the SAME idempotency key (A05).
  83   const prepareActionsRef = useRef(new Map<string, PrepareAction>())
  84   const caretRef = useRef<number | null>(null)
  85   const paneRef = useRef(pane)
  86   paneRef.current = pane
  87 
  88   useEffect(() => () => { store.dispose() }, [store])
  89 
  90   // A discarded draft loses its buffered text (FC-0030 port); an opened
  91   // session keeps it so the user can step back to the draft. Discard also
  92   // releases every prepared attachment the store still owns — handed-off
  93   // content belongs to the sent record and is never released here (A05).
  94   const endedBy = state.draft?.endedBy
  95   useEffect(() => {
  96     if (!connectionId || !endedBy) return
  97     if (endedBy === 'discarded') {
  98       for (const item of store.releaseCandidates(draftPaneOf)) {
  99         if (item.phase.state === 'ready') {
 100           void service.attachments.release(item.phase.reference, 'draft-cancelled').catch(() => undefined)
 101         }
 102       }
 103       store.disposePane(draftPaneOf)
 104     }
 105   }, [connectionId, endedBy, store, service])
 106 
 107     // Live subscription: the composer is a controlled input whose value comes
 108   // from the store through useSyncExternalStore, so a text mutation re-renders.
 109   const composition = useSyncExternalStore(store.subscribe, () => store.read(pane), () => store.read(pane))
 110   const workspaceId = state.draft?.workspaceId
 111   const projectName = agent?.workspaces?.items.find(item => item.id === workspaceId)?.normalizedPath
 112   const run = agent && sessionId ? paneRunState(agent, sessionId) : undefined
 113   const running = run?.status === 'starting' || run?.status === 'running' || run?.status === 'stop-requested'
 114   const canSend = !drafting || state.draft?.canSend === true
 115   const blockReason = drafting ? state.draft?.blockReason : undefined
 116   const attachmentBlocked = attachmentBlockReason(composition.items)
 117   const sendDisabled = !composition.text.trim() || !canSend || (pendingPane !== undefined && pendingPane === pane) || attachmentBlocked !== null
 118 
 119   const location = useMemo(() => (drafting
 120     ? { kind: 'draft' as const, draftId: pane, connectionId: connectionId ?? undefined,
 121         serverInstanceId: agent?.connection.serverInstanceId, projectId: workspaceId, contextRevision: revision,
 122         ...(state.runtimeGeneration !== undefined ? { runtimeGeneration: state.runtimeGeneration } : {}) }
 123     : { kind: 'session' as const, connectionId: connectionId ?? '', sessionId: sessionId ?? '',
 124         serverInstanceId: agent?.connection.serverInstanceId, contextRevision: revision,
 125         ...(state.runtimeGeneration !== undefined ? { runtimeGeneration: state.runtimeGeneration } : {}) }),
 126   [drafting, pane, connectionId, agent?.connection.serverInstanceId, workspaceId, revision, sessionId, state.runtimeGeneration])
 127   // PC-3: the attach entry and its note are driven by the facade's REAL
 128   // capability for this location — a missing production prepare owner, a
 129   // partial client surface or a draft without a native session all render
 130   // their own honest reason (S-05), never a fake activation.
 131   const attachmentCapability = useMemo(() => attachmentCapabilityOf(service, location), [service, location])
 132 
 133   // Slash token reconciliation: any text/caret change re-derives or closes the
 134   // panel; the + panel and the slash panel are mutually exclusive.
 135   const syncSlashToken = useCallback((text: string, caret: number) => {
 136     setPanel(current => {
 137       if (current?.mode === 'plus') return current
 138       const next = reconcileSlashSnapshot(current?.mode === 'slash' ? current.token : null, text, caret)
 139       return next ? { mode: 'slash', token: next } : null
 140     })
 141   }, [])
 142 
 143   const submit = useCallback(async () => {
 144     const capturedPane = paneRef.current
 145     if (pendingPane !== undefined || !canSend) return
 146     const captured = store.read(capturedPane)
 147     if (!captured.text.trim()) return
 148     const readyItems = captured.items.filter(item => item.phase.state === 'ready')
 149     const snapshot: ChatSubmissionSnapshot = {
 150       submissionId: globalThis.crypto.randomUUID(),
 151       target: { ...location },
 152       draftId: capturedPane,
 153       draftRevision: captured.revision,
 154       text: captured.text,
 155       attachmentIds: readyItems.map(item => item.id),
 156       attachmentRefs: captured.items.flatMap(item => item.phase.state === 'ready' ? [item.phase.reference] : []),
 157     }
 158     setSendError('')
 159     setPendingPane(capturedPane)
 160     let result: ChatSubmissionResult
 161     try {
 162       result = await gateway.submit(snapshot)
 163     } catch (cause) {
 164       result = { status: 'refused', reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 165     } finally {
 166       setPendingPane(current => (current === capturedPane ? undefined : current))
 167     }
 168     if (paneRef.current !== capturedPane) return // X05: a switched pane is never touched
 169     if (result.status === 'accepted') {
 170       const now = store.read(capturedPane)
 171       // Clear exactly the sent version; typing that happened after the send
 172       // stays (C04).
 173       const text = now.text === snapshot.text ? '' : now.text
 174       store.markHandedOff(capturedPane, snapshot.attachmentIds)
 175       store.write(capturedPane, { ...now, text, revision: now.revision + 1 })
 176       setPanel(null)
 177     } else if (result.status === 'refused') {
 178       // Refusal keeps the draft AND its prepared attachments (US4-1/US3-3).
 179       setSendError(result.reason)
 180     } else {
 181       // Unknown keeps everything, never auto-retries (US4-2).
 182       setSendError('发送结果未知：连接可能已中断。草稿已保留，请核实后手动重发。')
 183     }
 184   }, [canSend, gateway, location, pendingPane, store])
 185 
 186   const stop = useCallback(() => {
 187     if (!run?.runId) return
 188     setSendError('')
 189     void service.stop(run.runId).catch(cause => setSendError(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)))
 190   }, [run?.runId, service])
 191 
 192   // Add-content preparation runner (PC-3): the item enters the draft in
 193   // `preparing`, then lands in exactly one of the four phases — ready with the
 194   // service-owned reference, failed with the refusal reason (kept, removable),
 195   // unknown (kept, send-blocking, retry with the SAME idempotency key), or
 196   // removed on `unavailable`. The item id IS the idempotency key (input-spec
 197   // A05): a retry re-uses it, so the port dedupes instead of double-preparing.
 198   const runPrepare = useCallback((targetPane: string, itemId: string, action: PrepareAction) => {
 199     const capturedLocation = { ...location }
 200     void action(capturedLocation, itemId).then(result => {
 201       if (result.status === 'accepted' && result.reference) {
 202         store.updateItem(targetPane, itemId, { phase: { state: 'ready', reference: result.reference } })
 203       } else if (result.status === 'refused') {
 204         // US3-3: a refused preparation stays visible in the draft with its reason.
 205         store.updateItem(targetPane, itemId, { phase: { state: 'failed', reason: result.message } })
 206       } else if (result.status === 'unknown') {
 207         store.updateItem(targetPane, itemId, { phase: { state: 'unknown' } })
 208       } else {
 209         store.removeItem(targetPane, itemId)
 210         setSendError('该来源暂时无法提供此内容。')
 211       }
 212       setPanel(null)
 213     })
 214   }, [location, store])
 215 
 216   const pickEntry = useCallback((entry: ChatInputEntry) => {
 217     const current = store.read(paneRef.current)
 218     if (entry.availability.kind !== 'ready') return
 219     if (entry.action.kind === 'insert-command') {
 220       const text = current.text
 221       const caret = caretRef.current ?? text.length
 222       if (panel?.mode === 'slash') {
 223         const range = slashReplacementRange(panel.token, text, caret)
 224         if (!range) {
 225           // Stale token: the caret moved away — refuse the old insert.
 226           setSendError('命令插入位置已变化，未插入。')
 227           setPanel(null)
 228           return
 229         }
 230         store.write(paneRef.current, {
 231           ...current,
 232           text: text.slice(0, range.start) + entry.action.text + text.slice(range.end),
 233           revision: current.revision + 1,
 234         })
 235       } else {
 236         // plus: insert at the caret the panel captured when it opened.
 237         const at = Math.min(panel?.caret ?? caret, text.length)
 238         const inserted = text.slice(0, at) + entry.action.text + (at === 0 ? ' ' : '') + text.slice(at)
 239         store.write(paneRef.current, { ...current, text: inserted, revision: current.revision + 1 })
 240       }
 241       setPanel(null)
 242       return
 243     }
 244     if (entry.action.kind === 'invoke') {
 245       setPanel(null)
 246       void entry.action.execute({ ...location }).then(result => {
 247         if (result.status === 'refused') setSendError(result.message)
 248       })
 249       return
 250     }
 251     // add-content: the entry's availability already proved the capability is
 252     // real (a disabled entry never reaches here); the reference is never
 253     // faked into the draft.
 254     const itemId = globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()
 255     const prepare = entry.action.prepare
 256     store.addItem(paneRef.current, {
 257       id: itemId, sourceId: entry.id, kind: 'file', displayName: '附件', phase: { state: 'preparing' },
 258     })
 259     prepareActionsRef.current.set(itemId, prepare)
 260     runPrepare(paneRef.current, itemId, prepare)
 261   }, [location, panel, runPrepare, store])
 262 
 263   const sourcesView = useMemo(() => {
 264     if (!panel) return undefined
 265     const request: ChatInputQuery = panel.mode === 'slash'
 266       ? { location: { ...location }, query: panel.token.query, surface: 'slash', signal: new AbortController().signal }
 267       : { location: { ...location }, query: plusQuery, surface: 'plus', signal: new AbortController().signal }
 268     // The query re-issues when the panel opens, the slash token changes or the
 269     // plus search string changes; per-source states live in the service.
 270     return chat.queryInputSources(request)
 271   }, [panel, location, chat, plusQuery])
 272 
 273   const panelConsumeKey = panel
 274     ? (event: { key: string; preventDefault(): void }) => {
 275         if (event.key === 'Escape') {
 276           event.preventDefault()
 277           setPanel(null)
 278           return true
 279         }
 280         if (panel.mode === 'slash' && ['Enter', 'Tab', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown'].includes(event.key)) {
 281           return panelKeyRef.current?.(event) ?? false
 282         }
 283         if (panel.mode === 'plus' && (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === 'ArrowUp' || event.key === 'ArrowDown')) {
 284           return panelKeyRef.current?.(event) ?? false
 285         }
 286         return false
 287       }
 288     : undefined
 289 
 290   // All hooks run unconditionally, BEFORE any early return (rules of hooks):
 291   // the placeholder branches must not change the hook count between renders.
 292   const toolbarSlot = useMemo(() => chat.contributionsBySlot('composer.toolbar'), [chat])
 293   const toolbarViews = useSyncExternalStore(toolbarSlot.subscribe, toolbarSlot.getSnapshot, toolbarSlot.getSnapshot)
 294 
 295   if (!connectionId) {
 296     return <div className="chat-page chat-placeholder"><h2>选择一个连接</h2><p>从连接面板选择一个已启用的代理。</p></div>
 297   }
 298   if (!agent) {
 299     return <div className="chat-page chat-placeholder"><h2>连接中</h2><p>{state.error ?? '正在等待代理连接。'}</p></div>
 300   }
 301   if (!drafting && !sessionId) {
 302     return <div className="chat-page chat-placeholder"><h2>选择一个会话</h2>
 303       <p><button type="button" className="chat-primary-action" data-testid="chat-new-session-empty"
 304         onClick={() => (openViaOverlay ? openViaOverlay() : setDialogOpen(true))}>新会话</button></p></div>
 305   }
 306 
 307   const conversation = drafting ? { conversationKey: `${connectionId}:draft`, parts: [] } : projectConversation(agent, sessionId ?? '')
 308   const interactions = drafting ? [] : interactionsForSession(agent, sessionId ?? '')
 309 
 310   return (
 311     <section className="chat-page" data-pane={pane} data-testid="chat-page" data-chat-theme={theme}>
 312       <style>{chatStyles}</style>
 313       <header className="chat-page-head">
 314         <div>
 315           <small>{drafting ? '新会话' : '会话'}</small>
 316           <h2>{drafting
 317             ? (projectName ? `你想在 ${projectName.split('/').filter(Boolean).at(-1)} 中做什么？` : '选择项目后开始')
 318             : agent.sessions.find(item => item.id === sessionId)?.title ?? sessionId}</h2>
 319         </div>
 320         <div className="chat-page-head-actions">
 321           <button type="button" className="chat-primary-action" data-testid="chat-new-session"
 322             onClick={() => (openViaOverlay ? openViaOverlay() : setDialogOpen(true))}>新会话</button>
 323           <span className="chat-run-state" data-status={run?.status ?? 'idle'}>{run?.status ?? 'idle'}</span>
 324         </div>
 325       </header>
 326       {agent.connection.status !== 'connected' && (
 327         <p role="alert" className="chat-error">连接已断开。正在运行的任务结果未知，请在连接面板重连。</p>
 328       )}
 329       {run?.status === 'stop-requested' && <p role="status" className="chat-note">已请求停止，等待代理确认。</p>}
 330       {run?.status === 'unknown' && <p role="status" className="chat-note">断连后任务结果未知。</p>}
 331       {agent.diagnostic && <p role="status" className="chat-note">{agent.diagnostic}</p>}
 332       {!drafting && conversation.parts.length === 0 && (
 333         <p className="chat-note" data-testid="chat-thread-empty">这个会话还没有消息。</p>
 334       )}
 335       <ThreadBody display={conversation} expansion={expansion} theme={theme} />
 336       <div className="chat-compose" data-testid="chat-compose">
 337         <ApprovalPanel interactions={interactions} interactionsCapability={agent.connection.capabilities.interactions}
 338           respond={(id, answer) => service.respond(id, answer)} />
 339         {drafting && !canSend && blockReason && (
 340           <p role="status" className="chat-compose-block" data-testid="chat-compose-block">
 341             {draftBlockCopy[blockReason] ?? '发送被项目门禁阻止。'}
 342           </p>
 343         )}
 344         <AttachmentStrip items={composition.items}
 345           onRemove={itemId => {
 346             const item = store.read(pane).items.find(candidate => candidate.id === itemId)
 347             if (item?.phase.state === 'ready') {
 348               void service.attachments.release(item.phase.reference, 'draft-removed')
 349                 .catch(cause => setSendError(`附件清理失败：${cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)}`))
 350             }
 351             prepareActionsRef.current.delete(itemId)
 352             store.removeItem(pane, itemId)
 353           }}
 354           onRetry={itemId => {
 355             const action = prepareActionsRef.current.get(itemId)
 356             store.updateItem(pane, itemId, { phase: { state: 'preparing' } })
 357             if (action) runPrepare(pane, itemId, action)
 358           }} />
 359         {attachmentBlocked && <p role="status" className="chat-compose-block" data-testid="chat-attachment-block">{attachmentBlocked}</p>}
 360         {panel && sourcesView && (
 361           <InputPanel mode={panel.mode} sources={sourcesView} initialPlusQuery={plusQuery}
 362             keyForwardRef={panelKeyRef}
 363             onPick={pickEntry}
 364             onComplete={entry => pickEntry(entry)}
 365             onRetry={() => setPlusQuery(query => `${query}`)}
 366             onClose={() => setPanel(null)} />
 367         )}
 368         <div className="chat-compose-toolbar" data-testid="chat-compose-toolbar">
 369           {toolbarViews.map(view => {
 370             if (!view.project) return null
 371             const projected = view.project({
 372               slot: 'composer.toolbar', location: { ...location },
 373               connection: { status: agent.connection.status, runStatus: run?.status },
 374             })
 375             if (projected.hidden) return null
 376             const Component = resolver(view.keyId)
 377             if (!Component) return null // provider absent pre-foundation: position stays empty
 378             // The erased read side hands untyped props; the pairing was
 379             // enforced at registration by chatContribution's generic.
 380             return createElement(Component, { ...(projected.props as Record<string, unknown>), key: view.id })
 381           })}
 382         </div>
 383         <div className="chat-input-row">
 384           <button type="button" className="chat-icon-action" aria-label="打开添加与操作面板" data-testid="chat-plus-button"
 385             aria-expanded={panel?.mode === 'plus' ? 'true' : 'false'}
 386             onClick={() => {
 387               caretRef.current = null
 388               setPanel(current => (current?.mode === 'plus' ? null : { mode: 'plus', caret: caretRef.current ?? composition.text.length }))
 389             }}>＋</button>
 390           <ChatTextInput
 391             value={composition.text}
 392             editable={pendingPane === undefined || pendingPane === pane}
 393             placeholder={drafting ? '向新会话发送第一条消息' : '向这个代理发送消息'}
 394             submitDisabled={sendDisabled}
 395             onTextChange={value => store.setText(pane, value)}
 396             onCaretChange={caret => { caretRef.current = caret; syncSlashToken(composition.text, caret) }}
 397             panelConsumeKey={panelConsumeKey}
 398             onSubmit={() => void submit()}
 399             testid="chat-input"
 400           />
 401           {running
 402             ? <button type="button" className="chat-primary-action" data-testid="chat-stop"
 403                 disabled={run?.status === 'stop-requested' || run?.status === 'starting'} onClick={stop}>
 404                 {run?.status === 'stop-requested' ? '停止中…' : '请求停止'}
 405               </button>
 406             : <button type="button" className="chat-primary-action" data-testid="chat-send"
 407                 disabled={sendDisabled} onClick={() => void submit()}>发送</button>}
 408         </div>
 409         {sendError && <p role="alert" className="chat-error" data-testid="chat-send-error">{sendError}</p>}
 410         {pendingPane === pane && <p role="status" className="chat-note" data-testid="chat-send-pending">发送中…</p>}
 411         <p className="chat-compose-meta">
 412           <span data-testid="chat-capability-note">{attachmentCapability.supported ? '附件通道就绪。' : attachmentCapability.reason}</span>
 413         </p>
 414       </div>
 415       {dialogOpen && !openViaOverlay && (
 416         <ProjectDialog service={service} snapshot={state} close={() => setDialogOpen(false)}
 417           onProjectSelected={() => { setDialogOpen(false); service.startDraft?.() }} />
 418       )}
 419     </section>
 420   )
 421 }

### M:plugins/chat/frontend/src/components/composer/text-input.tsx
   1 // Composer text input, ported from ZCode
   2 // packages/ui/src/components/ai-elements/prompt-input-textarea.tsx @ 29628c9a
   3 // (derived from vercel/ai-elements, Apache-2.0 — see THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md).
   4 // Ported: the isComposing + nativeEvent.isComposing double-guard (Chinese IME
   5 // Enter never submits), Shift+Enter newline, external onKeyDown runs first and
   6 // a defaultPrevented event skips internal handling (the suggestion panel
   7 // consumes Enter/Tab before the input acts), submit-button-disabled check
   8 // before requestSubmit, Backspace-removes-last-attachment on empty input,
   9 // paste-into-attachments conflation. Dropped: the upstream controller and
  10 // attachment store — Chat hands in its own callbacks (chat-api ComposerProps);
  11 // the input stores no draft of its own.
  12 import { useCallback, useState, type ClipboardEvent, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react'
  13 
  14 export interface ChatTextInputProps {
  15   readonly value: string
  16   readonly editable: boolean
  17   readonly placeholder: string
  18   readonly submitDisabled: boolean
  19   readonly onTextChange: (value: string) => void
  20   readonly onSubmit: () => void
  21   /** Suggestion panel is open; the panel decides whether to consume the key. */
  22   readonly panelConsumeKey?: (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => boolean
  23   readonly onCaretChange?: (caret: number) => void
  24   readonly removeLastAttachment?: () => void
  25   readonly addPastedFiles?: (files: readonly File[]) => void
  26   readonly testid?: string
  27 }
  28 
  29 export function ChatTextInput(props: ChatTextInputProps) {
  30   const [isComposing, setIsComposing] = useState(false)
  31 
  32   const reportCaret = (element: HTMLTextAreaElement) => props.onCaretChange?.(element.selectionStart ?? 0)
  33 
  34   const handleKeyDown = useCallback((event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
  35     // External handler first; defaultPrevented means the panel consumed it.
  36     if (props.panelConsumeKey?.(event)) return
  37     if (event.defaultPrevented) return
  38 
  39     if (event.key === 'Enter') {
  40       if (isComposing || event.nativeEvent.isComposing) return
  41       if (event.shiftKey) return
  42       event.preventDefault()
  43       if (props.submitDisabled) return
  44       props.onSubmit()
  45       return
  46     }
  47     if (event.key === 'Backspace' && event.currentTarget.value === '' && props.removeLastAttachment) {
  48       event.preventDefault()
  49       props.removeLastAttachment()
  50     }
  51   }, [props, isComposing])
  52 
  53   const handlePaste = useCallback((event: ClipboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
  54     const items = event.clipboardData?.items
  55     if (!items || !props.addPastedFiles) return
  56     const files: File[] = []
  57     for (const item of items) {
  58       if (item.kind === 'file') {
  59         const file = item.getAsFile()
  60         if (file) files.push(file)
  61       }
  62     }
  63     if (files.length > 0) {
  64       event.preventDefault()
  65       props.addPastedFiles(files)
  66     }
  67   }, [props])
  68 
  69   return (
  70     <textarea
  71       className="chat-input-text"
  72       data-testid={props.testid}
  73       aria-label={props.placeholder}
  74       placeholder={props.placeholder}
  75       value={props.value}
  76       disabled={!props.editable}
  77       onCompositionStart={() => setIsComposing(true)}
  78       onCompositionEnd={() => setIsComposing(false)}
  79       onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
  80       onPaste={handlePaste}
  81       onSelect={event => reportCaret(event.currentTarget)}
  82       onClick={event => reportCaret(event.currentTarget)}
  83       onKeyUp={event => reportCaret(event.currentTarget)}
  84       onChange={event => { props.onTextChange(event.currentTarget.value); reportCaret(event.currentTarget) }}
  85     />
  86   )
  87 }

### M:plugins/chat/frontend/src/adapters/agent.ts
   1 // Adapter: AgentSessions facade → chat display DTOs (service-adaptation.md
   2 // §1 discipline). Every mapping below is a verified field of the current
   3 // facade; nothing is inferred from tool names, titles or endpoint strings:
   4 // - No event sequence exists → the display list is a snapshot projection with
   5 //   the message-internal order reasoning → body → tools, never a timeline.
   6 // - reasoning evidence: `reasoningState` (contracts 0.2.0, R-Z2-5) when the
   7 //   connector reports one; otherwise the bare string inherits the message's
   8 //   run status and no durationMs is fabricated.
   9 // - Tool structured command display stays undefined (the facade carries no
  10 //   verified command fields) → every tool renders generic; a name like `bash`
  11 //   never fabricates command syntax (X06).
  12 // - Submission (R-Z2-1, contracts 0.2.0): the facade answers the decidable
  13 //   three-state outcome itself — a void-resolving legacy connector is mapped
  14 //   to `accepted` inside the facade; this adapter maps 1:1 and adds nothing.
  15 // - Command catalog (R-Z2-3) and attachments (R-Z2-2) are real facade
  16 //   projections: absence and errors are states with reasons, never fake menus
  17 //   or receive-then-drop items.
  18 import type {
  19   AgentCommandCatalog, AgentSessions,
  20   AgentInteraction, AgentMessage, AgentSnapshot, AgentToolCall, AgentWorkspaceSnapshot,
  21 } from '@extensions/ordessa.agent-contracts/contract.js'
  22 import type {
  23   ChatCommandCatalog, ChatContentReference, ChatContentStatus, ChatInputEntry, ChatInputSource, ChatLocation,
  24   ChatMessageBodyProps, ChatReasoningProps, ChatSessionGateway, ChatSubmissionResult, ChatSubmissionSnapshot,
  25   ChatToolActivityProps, ChatToolState,
  26 } from '@extensions/ordessa.chat-api/contract.js'
  27 
  28 export interface ConversationPart {
  29   readonly kind: 'reasoning' | 'body' | 'tool'
  30   readonly reasoning?: ChatReasoningProps
  31   readonly body?: ChatMessageBodyProps
  32   readonly tool?: ChatToolActivityProps
  33 }
  34 
  35 export interface ChatConversationDisplay {
  36   readonly conversationKey: string
  37   readonly parts: readonly ConversationPart[]
  38 }
  39 
  40 /** Display-only stable conversation key (connection + session scope). It is a
  41  * React/state key, never a backend id; business calls keep original ids. */
  42 export function conversationKeyOf(connectionId: string | undefined, sessionId: string): string {
  43   return `${connectionId ?? '-'}:${sessionId}`
  44 }
  45 
  46 function contentStatusOf(status: AgentMessage['status']): ChatContentStatus {
  47   switch (status) {
  48     case 'starting':
  49     case 'running':
  50     case 'stop-requested': return 'streaming'
  51     case 'completed': return 'complete'
  52     case 'cancelled': return 'interrupted'
  53     default: return 'unknown'
  54   }
  55 }
  56 
  57 function toolStateOf(state: AgentToolCall['status']): ChatToolState {
  58   return state
  59 }
  60 
  61 const prettyJson = (value: unknown): string | undefined => {
  62   if (value === undefined) return undefined
  63   if (typeof value === 'string') return value
  64   try { return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) } catch { return String(value) }
  65 }
  66 
  67 export function projectMessage(message: AgentMessage, conversationKey: string): ConversationPart[] {
  68   const parts: ConversationPart[] = []
  69   if (message.role === 'user') {
  70     parts.push({ kind: 'body', body: {
  71       conversationKey, messageId: message.id, role: 'user', body: message.text, status: 'complete',
  72     } })
  73     return parts
  74   }
  75   // Reasoning evidence: the connector's own reasoningState when present
  76   // (R-Z2-5); a bare string can only inherit the message status — never its
  77   // own streaming verdict, and never a fabricated duration.
  78   if (message.reasoning) {
  79     parts.push({ kind: 'reasoning', reasoning: {
  80       conversationKey, partId: `${message.id}:reasoning`, text: message.reasoning,
  81       status: message.reasoningState?.status ?? contentStatusOf(message.status),
  82       ...(message.reasoningState?.durationMs !== undefined ? { durationMs: message.reasoningState.durationMs } : {}),
  83     } })
  84   }
  85   if (message.text) {
  86     parts.push({ kind: 'body', body: {
  87       conversationKey, messageId: message.id, role: 'assistant', body: message.text,
  88       status: contentStatusOf(message.status),
  89     } })
  90   }
  91   for (const tool of message.tools ?? []) {
  92     parts.push({ kind: 'tool', tool: {
  93       conversationKey, toolId: tool.id, title: tool.name, state: toolStateOf(tool.status),
  94       paramsText: prettyJson(tool.arguments), outputText: prettyJson(tool.result),
  95       // `command` stays undefined by design: the facade exposes no verified
  96       // structured command fields, so the generic renderer is the only
  97       // honest display (service-adaptation §2, X06).
  98     } })
  99   }
 100   return parts
 101 }
 102 
 103 export function projectConversation(snapshot: AgentSnapshot, sessionId: string): ChatConversationDisplay {
 104   const connectionId = snapshot.connection.id
 105   const conversationKey = conversationKeyOf(connectionId, sessionId)
 106   const messages = snapshot.messages[sessionId] ?? []
 107   return { conversationKey, parts: messages.flatMap(message => projectMessage(message, conversationKey)) }
 108 }
 109 
 110 export function interactionsForSession(snapshot: AgentSnapshot, sessionId: string): readonly AgentInteraction[] {
 111   return snapshot.interactions.filter(item => item.sessionId === sessionId)
 112 }
 113 
 114 export interface PaneRunState {
 115   readonly runId?: string
 116   readonly status: 'idle' | 'starting' | 'running' | 'stop-requested' | 'completed' | 'cancelled' | 'failed' | 'unknown'
 117   readonly stoppable: boolean
 118 }
 119 
 120 export function paneRunState(snapshot: AgentSnapshot, sessionId: string): PaneRunState {
 121   const run = Object.values(snapshot.runs).filter(item => item.sessionId === sessionId).at(-1)
 122   return { runId: run?.id, status: run?.status ?? 'idle', stoppable: run?.stoppable ?? true }
 123 }
 124 
 125 /**
 126  * The only submission shape Chat sends through. Revision 0.2.0 migration
 127  * (R-Z2-1): the FACADE owns the three-state mapping (typed refusal,
 128  * evidenced link-down → unknown, legacy void resolve → send-path acceptance);
 129  * this adapter maps the outcome 1:1 and invents no state. `accepted` means
 130  * the send path took the submission — never execution success. The UI must
 131  * keep the draft on anything but `accepted`.
 132  */
 133 export function createFacadeGateway(service: AgentSessions): ChatSessionGateway {
 134   return {
 135     async submit(snapshot: ChatSubmissionSnapshot): Promise<ChatSubmissionResult> {
 136       const refs = snapshot.attachmentRefs ?? []
 137       let outcome
 138       try {
 139         outcome = await service.send(snapshot.text, refs.length ? refs : undefined)
 140       } catch (cause) {
 141         // Precondition failures (no session selected, closed gate) are not
 142         // submission outcomes; they surface as refusals with their reason.
 143         return { status: 'refused', reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 144       }
 145       switch (outcome.kind) {
 146         case 'accepted': return { status: 'accepted' }
 147         case 'refused': return { status: 'refused', reason: outcome.code ? `${outcome.code}: ${outcome.reason}` : outcome.reason }
 148         case 'unknown': return { status: 'unknown' }
 149       }
 150     },
 151   }
 152 }
 153 
 154 /** Facade attachment capability for one location (PC-3). The facade member
 155  * always answers; a missing production prepare owner, a partial client
 156  * surface or a draft without a native session are all honest `absent`s with
 157  * their reason — the entry renders exactly that, never a fake activation. */
 158 export function attachmentCapabilityOf(service: AgentSessions, location: ChatLocation) {
 159   const sessionId = location.kind === 'session' ? location.sessionId : undefined
 160   const cap = service.attachments.capability(sessionId)
 161   if (cap.kind === 'available') return { supported: true } as const
 162   return { supported: false, reason: cap.reason } as const
 163 }
 164 
 165 const commandReasonCopy: Record<string, string> = {
 166   malformed: '原生命令目录帧不可解析。',
 167   'stale-session': '命令目录属于已切换的会话。',
 168   'channel-down': '连接已断开，命令目录不可用。',
 169   unobservable: '连接断连结果不可观测，命令目录状态未知。',
 170 }
 171 
 172 /** Command catalog projection (R-Z2-3, PC-2): the facade's four-state catalog
 173  * mapped 1:1. `absent` is the truthful state for connections that project no
 174  * native directory — local plugin menu sources keep working, no fake native
 175  * commands are shown. `loading` is mapped when a connector declares it; no
 176  * current connector does (registered in the 014 P-C report). */
 177 export function facadeCommandCatalog(service: AgentSessions): ChatCommandCatalog {
 178   return {
 179     get: (target): ReturnType<ChatCommandCatalog['get']> => {
 180       if (target.kind !== 'session') return { status: 'absent' }
 181       let catalog: AgentCommandCatalog
 182       try { catalog = service.commandCatalog(target.sessionId) } catch { return { status: 'absent' } }
 183       switch (catalog.kind) {
 184         case 'available': return { status: 'ready', commands: catalog.commands.map(command => ({
 185           id: command.name, title: command.name, insertText: `/${command.name}`,
 186           ...(command.description ? { description: command.description } : {}),
 187         })) }
 188         case 'loading': return { status: 'loading' }
 189         case 'error': return { status: 'error', message: commandReasonCopy[catalog.reason] ?? catalog.reason }
 190         case 'absent': return { status: 'absent' }
 191       }
 192     },
 193   }
 194 }
 195 
 196 /** The slash-surface input source backed by the real catalog (US3-1): ready
 197  * commands become insert-command entries; an absent catalog shows one
 198  * disabled "no commands" entry WITH its reason; a catalog error surfaces as
 199  * this source's own error state (retry-able, other sources unaffected). */
 200 export function createNativeCommandSource(service: AgentSessions): ChatInputSource {
 201   const catalog = facadeCommandCatalog(service)
 202   return {
 203     id: 'ordessa.chat.native-commands',
 204     title: '原生命令',
 205     groups: [{ id: 'native', title: '原生命令', order: 10 }],
 206     async query(request) {
 207       if (request.surface !== 'slash') return []
 208       const state = catalog.get(request.location)
 209       if (state.status === 'ready') {
 210         return state.commands.map((command, index): ChatInputEntry => ({
 211           id: `native:${command.id}`, title: command.title,
 212           ...(command.description !== undefined ? { description: command.description } : {}),
 213           groupId: 'native', order: index, surfaces: ['slash'],
 214           availability: { kind: 'ready' },
 215           action: { kind: 'insert-command', text: command.insertText },
 216         }))
 217       }
 218       if (state.status === 'error') throw new Error(state.message)
 219       if (state.status === 'absent') {
 220         return [{
 221           id: 'native:none', title: '原生命令', groupId: 'native', order: 0, surfaces: ['slash'],
 222           availability: { kind: 'disabled', reason: '当前会话没有品牌原生命令目录（该品牌未播发）。' },
 223           action: { kind: 'insert-command', text: '' },
 224         }]
 225       }
 226       return [] // loading: the shared panel's per-source state carries it while the query is in flight
 227     },
 228   }
 229 }
 230 
 231 /** The plus-surface attachment entry (PC-3). Without a picker channel
 232  * (R-Z2-6) the entry stays disabled with that reason even when the transport
 233  * capability is real; with both present it prepares content through the
 234  * facade's attachment seam and returns the service-owned reference. */
 235 export function createAttachmentSource(service: AgentSessions, picker?: {
 236   pick(): Promise<{ sourceId: string; name: string; mimeType?: string } | undefined>
 237 }): ChatInputSource {
 238   const PICKER_REASON = '文件选择通道未接通（R-Z2-6），附件入口保持禁用。'
 239   const entry = async (location: ChatLocation): Promise<ChatInputEntry> => {
 240     const capability = attachmentCapabilityOf(service, location)
 241     const disabledReason = !picker ? PICKER_REASON : !capability.supported ? capability.reason : undefined
 242     return {
 243       id: 'attachments:add', title: '添加附件', groupId: 'attachments', order: 0, surfaces: ['plus'],
 244       ...(disabledReason !== undefined
 245         ? { availability: { kind: 'disabled' as const, reason: disabledReason } }
 246         : { availability: { kind: 'ready' as const } }),
 247       action: {
 248         kind: 'add-content',
 249         prepare: async (target, idempotencyKey) => {
 250           if (!picker || !capability.supported) return { status: 'unavailable' }
 251           const picked = await picker.pick()
 252           if (!picked) return { status: 'refused', message: '未选择任何文件。' }
 253           const sessionId = target.kind === 'session' ? target.sessionId : undefined
 254           const preparation = await service.attachments.prepare({ sessionId, sourceId: picked.sourceId, idempotencyKey })
 255           switch (preparation.kind) {
 256             case 'prepared': return { status: 'accepted', reference: preparation.reference.preparedId as ChatContentReference }
 257             case 'refused': return { status: 'refused', message: preparation.reason }
 258             case 'unknown': return { status: 'unknown' }
 259           }
 260         },
 261       },
 262     }
 263   }
 264   return {
 265     id: 'ordessa.chat.attachments',
 266     title: '附件',
 267     groups: [{ id: 'attachments', title: '附件', order: 20 }],
 268     async query(request) {
 269       if (request.surface !== 'plus') return []
 270       return [await entry(request.location)]
 271     },
 272   }
 273 }
 274 
 275 export function snapshotOf(service: AgentSessions): AgentWorkspaceSnapshot {
 276   return service.getSnapshot()
 277 }

### M:plugins/profile/integrations/chat/src/selection.ts
   1 /**
   2  * Profile selection state machine for the Chat composer (PV-10, PA01/PA03,
   3  * ux.md §3).
   4  *
   5  * Semantics locked here, and nowhere else:
   6  * - `select` only records the user's choice; the button label updates
   7  *   immediately and NO port/service call happens (apply/abort/restart stay 0).
   8  * - `applyOnSubmit` runs at the next user submit and applies the LAST
   9  *   selection only — B then C applies just C, once.
  10  * - A failed application keeps the selection and reports the reason at the
  11  *   send operation; the draft text belongs to Chat and is never touched here,
  12  *   and the send is NOT silently allowed through with the old configuration.
  13  * - Selecting the profile that is already current is a no-op selection, never
  14  *   an overlay clear (G07 lives in the backend, not in the selector).
  15  */
  16 import type {
  17   AppliedReceiptDto,
  18   ProfileCatalogGroup,
  19   ProfileSelection,
  20   ProfileServiceClient,
  21 } from '@ordessa/plugin-profile-api'
  22 
  23 export interface SelectionCounters {
  24   selections: number
  25   applyAttempts: number
  26   applyConfirmed: number
  27   applyFailed: number
  28 }
  29 
  30 export type ApplyOutcome =
  31   | { readonly ok: true; readonly applied: ProfileSelection; readonly receipt?: AppliedReceiptDto }
  32   | { readonly ok: false; readonly reason: string; readonly selection: ProfileSelection }
  33 
  34 export class ProfileSelectionStore {
  35   private readonly selections = new Map<string, ProfileSelection>()
  36   private readonly lastConfirmed = new Map<string, ProfileSelection>()
  37   private sequence = 0
  38 
  39   readonly counters: SelectionCounters = {
  40     selections: 0,
  41     applyAttempts: 0,
  42     applyConfirmed: 0,
  43     applyFailed: 0,
  44   }
  45 
  46   /** Register the user's pick for one draft/session key.  Pure bookkeeping:
  47    * no port call, no run interruption, no status label (PA01). */
  48   select(key: string, selection: ProfileSelection): void {
  49     this.counters.selections += 1
  50     this.selections.set(key, selection)
  51   }
  52 
  53   /** What the button shows right now: the user's selection, falling back to
  54    * the last confirmed profile of this session. */
  55   currentLabel(key: string, fallbackName?: string): string | null {
  56     return this.selections.get(key)?.displayName ?? fallbackName ?? null
  57   }
  58 
  59   peek(key: string): ProfileSelection | null {
  60     return this.selections.get(key) ?? null
  61   }
  62 
  63   /** Clear only what the UI subscription owns (scope teardown); stored
  64    * selections for live sessions are not business data and nothing here
  65    * aborts a run. */
  66   forget(key: string): void {
  67     this.selections.delete(key)
  68   }
  69 
  70   /**
  71    * Next-submit admission: apply the last selection through the Profile
  72    * service, then let the caller send.  Consecutive picks collapse here —
  73    * only the final one reaches the service.
  74    */
  75   async applyOnSubmit(
  76     key: string,
  77     sessionRef: { readonly realm: string; readonly harnessId: string; readonly nativeSessionKey: string; readonly sessionUid: string },
  78     profiles: Pick<ProfileServiceClient, 'beginTurnApplication'>,
  79   ): Promise<ApplyOutcome> {
  80     const selection = this.selections.get(key)
  81     if (!selection) {
  82       return { ok: true, applied: this.lastConfirmed.get(key) ?? { harnessId: sessionRef.harnessId, profileId: '', displayName: '' } }
  83     }
  84     this.counters.applyAttempts += 1
  85     try {
  86       const result = await profiles.beginTurnApplication(
  87         `profile-chat.apply:${sessionRef.sessionUid}:${selection.profileId}:${this.sequence += 1}`,
  88         sessionRef,
  89       )
  90       if (!result.appliedSwitch) {
  91         // Not a switch (same profile re-selected): nothing was applied and
  92         // nothing needed to be; the pick is simply consumed.
  93         this.selections.delete(key)
  94         this.lastConfirmed.set(key, selection)
  95         return { ok: true, applied: selection }
  96       }
  97       this.counters.applyConfirmed += 1
  98       this.selections.delete(key)
  99       this.lastConfirmed.set(key, selection)
 100       return { ok: true, applied: selection, receipt: result.receipt ?? undefined }
 101     } catch (error) {
 102       this.counters.applyFailed += 1
 103       // Keep the selection so the composer can offer retry/re-pick; never
 104       // report success and never fall back to the old configuration.
 105       const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
 106       return { ok: false, reason, selection }
 107     }
 108   }
 109 }
 110 
 111 /**
 112  * Catalog projection for the two-level picker.  An unbound draft may choose
 113  * from every available Harness group; an existing session only ever sees its
 114  * current Harness group — the picker never becomes a cross-Harness switch
 115  * (PA07, ux.md §3).
 116  */
 117 export type CatalogScope = {
 118   readonly kind: 'draft'
 119   readonly draftId?: string
 120 } | {
 121   readonly kind: 'session'
 122   readonly sessionUid?: string
 123   readonly harnessId: string
 124 }
 125 
 126 export function catalogForLocation(
 127   groups: readonly ProfileCatalogGroup[],
 128   location: CatalogScope,
 129 ): readonly ProfileCatalogGroup[] {
 130   if (location.kind === 'draft') return groups
 131   const own = groups.filter((group) => group.harnessId === location.harnessId)
 132   return own
 133 }

### M:plugins/profile/integrations/chat/src/glue.ts
   1 /**
   2  * Profile → Chat glue (PV-10).  Optional entry: without Chat (no
   3  * ChatContributionsToken) or without Profile (no ProfileServiceToken) this
   4  * glue is simply absent and both products keep working (contracts.md §4).
   5  *
   6  * The glue contributes one composer.toolbar picker.  It owns NO state of the
   7  * Chat, stores no draft text, and holds no session lifecycle: selections live
   8  * in {@link ProfileSelectionStore} and die with the UI scope (G18).
   9  */
  10 import type { ResourceScope } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
  11 import {
  12   chatContribution,
  13   defineChatComponentKey,
  14   type ChatContributionContext,
  15   type ChatContributionsService,
  16   type ChatLocation,
  17 } from '@extensions/ordessa.chat-api/contract.js'
  18 import type {
  19   ProfileCatalogGroup,
  20   ProfileSelection,
  21   ProfileServiceClient,
  22 } from '@ordessa/plugin-profile-api'
  23 import { ProfilePicker } from './picker'
  24 import type { ProfilePickerProps } from './picker-model'
  25 import { catalogForLocation, ProfileSelectionStore } from './selection'
  26 
  27 export { ProfileSelectionStore, catalogForLocation } from './selection'
  28 export type { ApplyOutcome, SelectionCounters } from './selection'
  29 export { ProfilePicker } from './picker'
  30 export { pickerOptionGroups, selectedOptionValue } from './picker-model'
  31 export type { ProfilePickerProps } from './picker-model'
  32 
  33 /** Chat-owned key factory: the glue never invents a second key identity. */
  34 export const ProfilePickerKey =
  35   defineChatComponentKey<ProfilePickerProps>('ordessa.profile.picker', 1)
  36 
  37 export interface ChatLocationLike {
  38   readonly kind?: string
  39   readonly draftId?: string
  40   readonly sessionUid?: string
  41 }
  42 
  43 /** Extract the picker's store key + catalog scope from the chat location. */
  44 export function pickerKeyFor(location: ChatLocation): {
  45   key: string
  46   scope: { readonly kind: 'draft' } | { readonly kind: 'session'; readonly harnessId: string }
  47 } {
  48   const raw = location as unknown as {
  49     kind?: string
  50     draftId?: string
  51     sessionUid?: string
  52     harnessId?: string
  53   }
  54   if (raw.kind === 'session' && raw.sessionUid) {
  55     return {
  56       key: `session:${raw.sessionUid}`,
  57       scope: { kind: 'session', harnessId: raw.harnessId ?? '' },
  58     }
  59   }
  60   return { key: `draft:${raw.draftId ?? 'unknown'}`, scope: { kind: 'draft' } }
  61 }
  62 
  63 export interface ProfileChatGlueInput {
  64   readonly store?: ProfileSelectionStore
  65   readonly profiles: Pick<ProfileServiceClient, 'beginTurnApplication'>
  66   readonly catalog: () => readonly ProfileCatalogGroup[]
  67   readonly onOpenManager?: () => void
  68 }
  69 
  70 export function createProfileChatGlue(
  71   scope: ResourceScope,
  72   contributions: ChatContributionsService,
  73   input: ProfileChatGlueInput,
  74 ) {
  75   const store = input.store ?? new ProfileSelectionStore()
  76   const registration = chatContribution<ProfilePickerProps>({
  77     id: 'ordessa.profile-chat.picker',
  78     slot: 'composer.toolbar',
  79     order: 10,
  80     key: ProfilePickerKey,
  81     project: (context: ChatContributionContext) => {
  82       if (context.slot !== 'composer.toolbar') return { hidden: true }
  83       const { key, scope: pickerScope } = pickerKeyFor(context.location)
  84       const groups = catalogForLocation(input.catalog(), pickerScope)
  85       if (groups.length === 0) return { hidden: true }
  86       return {
  87         hidden: false,
  88         props: {
  89           label: store.currentLabel(key),
  90           groups,
  91           onOpenManager: input.onOpenManager,
  92           onSelect: (selection: ProfileSelection) => store.select(key, selection),
  93         },
  94       }
  95     },
  96   })
  97   const disposeRegistration =
  98     contributions.forScope(scope).addContribution(registration)
  99   return {
 100     store,
 101     dispose() {
 102       disposeRegistration.dispose()
 103     },
 104   }
 105 }
 106 
 107 /** Read-side convenience for the Chat host outlet. */
 108 export function pickerComponent() {
 109   return ProfilePicker
 110 }
