### M:plugins/chat/api/src/contract.ts
   1 // Chat domain public contract (chat-api; r3 consumes foundation types, r4 is
   2 // the 014 P-C real-seam revision — see the ledger at the bottom of this file).
   3 //
   4 // Depends on `@ordessa/extension-api` and the shared platform component API.
   5 // Component keys are constructed by `defineChatComponentKey` through C7's
   6 // public factory. This package copies no platform Token and invents no
   7 // ServiceLocator; provider ownership always comes from the host-issued
   8 // ResourceScope, never a caller-supplied owner string (contracts.md §4).
   9 import { Token, type IDisposable, type ResourceScope } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
  10 import { defineUiComponent, type UiComponentKey } from '@ordessa/ui-components/api'
  11 import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
  12 
  13 // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  14 // Component keys (the platform's UiComponentKey)
  15 // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  16 
  17 /** Identity of one replaceable Chat display interface — the platform's own
  18  * branded `UiComponentKey` type, so a chat key satisfies every C7-typed
  19  * consumer API unchanged (contracts.md §4). */
  20 export type ChatComponentKey<P> = UiComponentKey<P>
  21 
  22 /** The only Chat construction site; delegates to the platform factory. */
  23 export function defineChatComponentKey<P>(id: string, major: number): ChatComponentKey<P> {
  24   return defineUiComponent<P>(id, major)
  25 }
  26 
  27 /** Full-width readable agent/user body (contracts.md §6 `message-body`). */
  28 export const ChatMessageBodyKey = defineChatComponentKey<ChatMessageBodyProps>('ordessa.chat.message-body', 1)
  29 /** Collapsible reasoning block; user choice beats every state transition. */
  30 export const ChatReasoningKey = defineChatComponentKey<ChatReasoningProps>('ordessa.chat.reasoning', 1)
  31 /** Compact tool summary row with lazy detail (contracts.md §6 `tool-activity`). */
  32 export const ChatToolActivityKey = defineChatComponentKey<ChatToolActivityProps>('ordessa.chat.tool-activity', 1)
  33 /** Fixed bottom input surface; owns no draft persistence (contracts.md §6 `composer`). */
  34 export const ChatComposerKey = defineChatComponentKey<ChatComposerProps>('ordessa.chat.composer', 1)

  38 // business took the operation, never that it finished)

 396     addInputSource(source: ChatInputSource): IDisposable
 397   }
 398   contributionsBySlot(slot: ChatSlot): ObservableValues<ChatContributionView>
 399   /** Aggregated live query state; drives the shared panel's per-source
 400    * loading/error/ready presentation (input-spec P04). */
 401   queryInputSources(request: ChatInputQuery): ObservableValues<ChatInputSourceView>
 402 }
 403 
 404 export const ChatContributionsToken = new Token<ChatContributionsService>('ordessa.chat.contributions.v1')
 405 
 406 // The registration factory and the in-memory service implementation live in
 407 // ./registry (single runtime direction: registry → types here, never back).
 408 export { chatContribution, createChatContributions } from './registry'

 410 // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 411 // Revision ledger (r4 rules: every semantic change carries its compatibility
 412 // note here and in the delivering package's report).
 413 //
 414 // r1 — domain contract + registry (a3ec20c046).
 415 // r2 — prepare/execute signatures narrowed to explicit location params +
 416 //      ChatPrepareResult three-state (be672a59a0); no external consumers at
 417 //      publication, compatibility recorded in the checkpoint limitations.
 418 // r3 — ChatComponentKey aligned to the platform UiComponentKey (type-only;
 419 //      runtime objects unchanged) (47459b0acb).
 420 // r4 — 014 P-C real-seam revision (all changes additive; pre-r4 consumers keep
 421 //      compiling and behaving identically):
 422 //      1. ChatLocation.runtimeGeneration (PC-5): the backend-confirmed runtime
 423 //         generation when the session service observes one. Compatibility:
 424 //         contextRevision KEEPS its UI-internal staleness meaning; it never
 425 //         stands in for a backend generation, and an absent runtimeGeneration
 426 //         means exactly "no backend generation evidence" — producers must not
 427 //         synthesize one from UI counters.
 428 //      2. ChatSubmissionSnapshot.attachmentRefs (PC-3): opaque prepared
 429 //         references riding the submission; attachmentIds stay the draft
 430 //         bookkeeping identity. Optional; gateways resolve refs through the
 431 //         owning service and must refuse unknown refs, never drop them.
 432 //      3. ChatAttachmentPhase gains `unknown` and ChatPrepareResult gains an
 433 //         `unknown` status (PC-3): the undecidable preparation outcome keeps
 434 //         the item in the draft, send-blocking, retry with the SAME
 435 //         idempotency key, never auto-reclaimed.
 436 //      4. add-content prepare gains the idempotencyKey parameter (input-spec
 437 //         A05: the key is the retry identity, not the file name); the caller
 438 //         mints it once per item and re-uses it on every retry.
 439 //      5. ready catalog commands gain an optional `description` (the brand's
 440 //         own command description; absence renders title-only).
 441 // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

### P:plugins/profile/integrations/chat/src/selection.ts
   1 /**
   2  * Profile selection state machine for the Chat composer (PV-10, PA01/PA03,
   3  * ux.md §3; click-time registration per 021 appendix §6c F1.1).
   4  *
   5  * Semantics locked here, and nowhere else:
   6  * - `select` records the user's choice and updates the label immediately; it
   7  *   performs NO port call itself (apply/abort/restart stay 0). The backend
   8  *   REGISTRATION (`selectForSession`, which stamps the session's action_seq)
   9  *   happens at click time for bound sessions — via the glue's per-session
  10  *   serializer — and the store tracks that state per pick.
  11  * - `applyOnSubmit` only ever runs `beginTurnApplication`, and only for a pick
  12  *   the backend already holds (or one the caller explicitly vouches for via
  13  *   `continuation`). It NEVER registers a selection at submit time: a
  14  *   submit-time registration would stamp a later action_seq than an earlier
  15  *   click and let a stale profile win over the user's last pick (review F1).
  16  * - An UNREGISTERED pick is never applied behind the guard's back: the store
  17  *   refuses with a reason, keeping the pick for (re-)registration.
  18  * - A failed application/registration keeps the selection and reports the
  19  *   reason; the draft text belongs to Chat and is never touched here, and the
  20  *   send is NOT silently allowed through with the old configuration.
  21  * - Ordinary bound-session submissions apply the effective configuration
  22  *   without reselecting.
  23  */
  24 import type {
  25   AppliedReceiptDto,
  26   ProfileCatalogGroup,
  27   ProfileSelection,
  28   ProfileServiceClient,
  29   SessionRef,
  30 } from '@ordessa/plugin-profile-api'
  31 
  32 export interface SelectionCounters {
  33   selections: number
  34   applyAttempts: number
  35   applyConfirmed: number
  36   applyFailed: number
  37 }
  38 
  39 export type ApplyOutcome =
  40   | { readonly ok: true; readonly applied: ProfileSelection; readonly receipt?: AppliedReceiptDto }
  41   | { readonly ok: false; readonly reason: string; readonly selection: ProfileSelection; readonly unknown?: boolean }
  42 
  43 interface StoreEntry {
  44   selection: ProfileSelection
  45   /** Whether the backend currently holds THIS pick (click-time registration
  46    * acknowledged). A new click resets it: the newest pick is the intent. */
  47   registered: boolean
  48   registrationError?: string
  49 }
  50 
  51 export class ProfileSelectionStore {
  52   private readonly entries = new Map<string, StoreEntry>()
  53   private readonly lastConfirmed = new Map<string, ProfileSelection>()
  54   private sequence = 0
  55   private revision = 0
  56   private readonly listeners = new Set<() => void>()
  57   getSnapshot = () => this.revision
  58   subscribe = (listener: () => void) => { this.listeners.add(listener); return () => { this.listeners.delete(listener) } }
  59   private publish() { this.revision += 1; for (const listener of this.listeners) listener() }
  60 
  61   readonly counters: SelectionCounters = {
  62     selections: 0,
  63     applyAttempts: 0,
  64     applyConfirmed: 0,
  65     applyFailed: 0,
  66   }
  67 
  68   /** Record the user's pick for one draft/session key (PA01). Bookkeeping
  69    * only: no port call, no run interruption. The pick starts UNREGISTERED;
  70    * the glue registers it (at click for bound sessions, at submit otherwise)
  71    * and acknowledges with {@link markRegistered}. */
  72   select(key: string, selection: ProfileSelection): void {
  73     this.counters.selections += 1
  74     this.entries.set(key, { selection, registered: false })
  75     this.publish()
  76   }
  77 
  78   /** The click-time registration reached the backend: this exact pick is now
  79    * the session's pending intent (its action_seq is the click's). */
  80   markRegistered(key: string, selection: ProfileSelection): void {
  81     const entry = this.entries.get(key)
  82     if (!entry || entry.selection !== selection) return // a newer pick owns the key
  83     this.entries.set(key, { selection, registered: true })
  84     this.publish()
  85   }
  86 
  87   /** The click-time registration failed: the pick is kept UNREGISTERED and
  88    * the reason is surfaced in the picker (never a silent drop). */
  89   markRegistrationFailed(key: string, selection: ProfileSelection, reason: string): void {
  90     const entry = this.entries.get(key)
  91     if (!entry || entry.selection !== selection) return
  92     this.entries.set(key, { selection, registered: false, registrationError: reason })
  93     this.publish()
  94   }
  95 
  96   /** Whether the store's current pick for this key is the given one AND is
  97    * backend-registered. */
  98   isRegistered(key: string, selection: ProfileSelection): boolean {
  99     const entry = this.entries.get(key)
 100     return !!entry && entry.selection === selection && entry.registered
 101   }
 102 
 103   /** The registered pending pick (the only one `applyOnSubmit` may consume
 104    * without an explicit caller vouch), or null. */
 105   registeredPending(key: string): ProfileSelection | null {
 106     const entry = this.entries.get(key)
 107     return entry && entry.registered ? entry.selection : null
 108   }
 109 
 110   /** The last registration failure reason for the current pick, if any. */
 111   registrationError(key: string): string | null {
 112     return this.entries.get(key)?.registrationError ?? null
 113   }
 114 
 115   /** What the button shows right now: the user's selection, falling back to
 116    * the last confirmed profile of this session. */
 117   currentLabel(key: string, fallbackName?: string): string | null {
 118     return this.entries.get(key)?.selection.displayName ?? this.lastConfirmed.get(key)?.displayName ?? fallbackName ?? null
 119   }
 120 
 121   peek(key: string): ProfileSelection | null {
 122     return this.entries.get(key)?.selection ?? null
 123   }
 124 
 125   /** Clear only what the UI subscription owns (scope teardown); stored
 126    * selections for live sessions are not business data and nothing here
 127    * aborts a run. */
 128   forget(key: string): void {
 129     this.entries.delete(key)
 130     this.publish()
 131   }
 132 
 133   /** A reusable composer draft is not a native session. Once accepted or
 134    * discarded, its confirmed label must not leak into the next empty draft.
 135    * Pending user picks and bound-session confirmations remain untouched. */
 136   clearDraftConfirmations(): void {
 137     let changed = false
 138     for (const key of this.lastConfirmed.keys()) {
 139       if (key.startsWith('draft:')) { this.lastConfirmed.delete(key); changed = true }
 140     }
 141     if (changed) this.publish()
 142   }
 143 
 144   /**
 145    * Next-submit application: run `beginTurnApplication` for the REGISTERED
 146    * pending pick (or the caller-vouched `continuation`) and let the caller
 147    * send. Registration never happens here — a pick that is not registered is
 148    * refused fail-closed with its reason, never applied with a submit-time
 149    * action_seq (review F1).
 150    */
 151   async applyOnSubmit(
 152     key: string,
 153     sessionRef: SessionRef,
 154     profiles: Pick<ProfileServiceClient, 'beginTurnApplication'> & Partial<Pick<ProfileServiceClient, 'inspectSessionConfig'>>,
 155     continuation?: ProfileSelection,
 156   ): Promise<ApplyOutcome> {
 157     // A captured binding is not a new user pick. In particular, a pick made
 158     // while native preparation/application waits belongs to the next turn.
 159     const entry = this.entries.get(key)
 160     const selection = continuation ?? (entry && entry.registered ? entry.selection : null)
 161     if (!selection) {
 162       if (entry && !entry.registered) {
 163         // fail-closed: the user's newest pick was never registered — passing
 164         // this off as an "ordinary turn" would send on the OLD configuration
 165         // while the pick looks pending. Registration is the click's (bound
 166         // sessions) or the guard's (drafts) job; until then: no apply, no send.
 167         return { ok: false, reason: '所选预设尚未登记到会话，已阻止本次应用；请重新选择或重试。', selection: entry.selection }
 168       }
 169       return { ok: true, applied: this.lastConfirmed.get(key) ?? { harnessId: sessionRef.harnessId, profileId: '', displayName: '' } }
 170     }
 171     if (!continuation && !this.isRegistered(key, selection)) {
 172       // fail-closed: an unregistered pick must be registered first — applying
 173       // it here would stamp the backend action_seq at SUBMIT time and let an
 174       // earlier-registered intent (or a later model pick) misorder against the
 175       // user's clicks.
 176       return { ok: false, reason: '所选预设尚未登记到会话，已阻止本次应用；请重新选择或重试。', selection }
 177     }
 178     this.counters.applyAttempts += 1
 179     try {
 180       const operationKey = `profile-chat.apply:${sessionRef.sessionUid}:${selection.profileId}:${this.sequence += 1}`
 181       const result = await profiles.beginTurnApplication(
 182         operationKey,
 183         sessionRef,
 184       )
 185       if (!result.appliedSwitch) {
 186         if (typeof profiles.inspectSessionConfig !== 'function') {
 187           throw new Error('Profile current configuration verification is unavailable')
 188         }
 189         const config = await profiles.inspectSessionConfig(sessionRef)
 190         const receipt = config.evidence?.receipt
 191         if (!sameSession(config.sessionRef, sessionRef) || config.current?.profileId !== selection.profileId
 192           || config.switchState !== 'settled' || config.pending !== null
 193           || !receipt || receipt.profileId !== selection.profileId
 194           || receipt.profileRevision !== config.current.revision || !sameSession(receipt.sessionRef, sessionRef)
 195           || !receipt.operationId || !receipt.configDigest || !receipt.confirmedAt
 196           || !receipt.evidenceKind || receipt.evidenceKind === 'legacy-unverified'
 197           || config.evidence.evidenceKind !== receipt.evidenceKind
 198           || config.evidence.journal?.state !== 'confirmed'
 199           || config.evidence.journal.operationId !== receipt.operationId) {
 200           throw new Error('Profile current configuration is not confirmed for this selection')
 201         }
 202         if (this.entries.get(key)?.selection === selection) this.entries.delete(key)
 203         this.lastConfirmed.set(key, selection)
 204         this.publish()
 205         return { ok: true, applied: selection, receipt }
 206       }
 207       const receipt = result.receipt
 208       if (!receipt || receipt.profileId !== selection.profileId || !sameSession(receipt.sessionRef, sessionRef)
 209           || !receipt.operationId || !receipt.configDigest || !receipt.confirmedAt
 210           || !receipt.evidenceKind || receipt.evidenceKind === 'legacy-unverified') {
 211         throw Object.assign(new Error('Profile application receipt is unproven for this selection'), { kind: 'Unknown' })
 212       }
 213       this.counters.applyConfirmed += 1
 214       if (this.entries.get(key)?.selection === selection) this.entries.delete(key)
 215       this.lastConfirmed.set(key, selection)
 216       this.publish()
 217       return { ok: true, applied: selection, receipt }
 218     } catch (error) {
 219       this.counters.applyFailed += 1
 220       // Keep the selection so the composer can offer retry/re-pick; never
 221       // report success and never fall back to the old configuration.
 222       const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
 223       return { ok: false, reason, selection,
 224         ...(error && typeof error === 'object' && 'kind' in error && error.kind === 'Unknown' ? { unknown: true } : {}) }
 225     }
 226   }
 227 }
 228 
 229 function sameSession(actual: SessionRef | undefined, expected: SessionRef): boolean {
 230   return !!actual && actual.realm === expected.realm && actual.harnessId === expected.harnessId
 231     && actual.nativeSessionKey === expected.nativeSessionKey && actual.sessionUid === expected.sessionUid
 232 }
 233 
 234 /**
 235  * Catalog projection for the two-level picker.  An unbound draft may choose
 236  * from every available Harness group; an existing session only ever sees its
 237  * current Harness group — the picker never becomes a cross-Harness switch
 238  * (PA07, ux.md §3).
 239  */
 240 export type CatalogScope = {
 241   readonly kind: 'draft'
 242   readonly draftId?: string
 243 } | {
 244   readonly kind: 'session'
 245   readonly sessionUid?: string
 246   readonly harnessId: string
 247 }
 248 
 249 export function catalogForLocation(
 250   groups: readonly ProfileCatalogGroup[],
 251   location: CatalogScope,
 252 ): readonly ProfileCatalogGroup[] {
 253   if (location.kind === 'draft') return groups
 254   const own = groups.filter((group) => group.harnessId === location.harnessId)
 255   return own
 256 }

### P:plugins/profile/integrations/chat/src/glue.ts
   1 /**
   2  * Profile → Chat glue (PV-10).  Optional entry: without Chat (no
   3  * ChatContributionsToken) or without Profile (no ProfileServiceToken) this
   4  * glue is simply absent and both products keep working (contracts.md §4).
   5  *
   6  * The glue contributes one composer.toolbar picker.  It owns NO state of the
   7  * Chat, stores no draft text, and holds no session lifecycle: selections live
   8  * in {@link ProfileSelectionStore} and die with the UI scope (G18).
   9  */
  10 import type { ResourceScope } from '@ordessa/extension-api'
  11 import {
  12   chatContribution,
  13   defineChatComponentKey,
  14   type ChatContributionContext,
  15   type ChatContributionsService,
  16   type ChatLocation,
  17 } from '@extensions/ordessa.chat-api/contract.js'
  18 import type {
  19   ProfileCatalogGroup,
  20   ProfileSelection,
  21   ProfileServiceClient,
  22 } from '@ordessa/plugin-profile-api'
  23 import { ProfilePicker } from './picker'
  24 import type { ProfilePickerProps } from './picker-model'
  25 import { catalogForLocation, ProfileSelectionStore } from './selection'
  26 import type { ApplyOutcome } from './selection'
  27 import type { ProfileCatalog } from './catalog'
  28 import type { AgentSessions } from '@extensions/ordessa.agent-contracts/contract.js'
  29 
  30 export { ProfileSelectionStore, catalogForLocation } from './selection'
  31 export type { ApplyOutcome, SelectionCounters } from './selection'
  32 export { ProfilePicker } from './picker'
  33 export { pickerOptionGroups, selectedOptionValue } from './picker-model'
  34 export type { ProfilePickerProps } from './picker-model'
  35 
  36 /** Chat-owned key factory: the glue never invents a second key identity. */
  37 export const ProfilePickerKey =
  38   defineChatComponentKey<ProfilePickerProps>('ordessa.profile.picker', 1)
  39 
  40 export interface ChatLocationLike {
  41   readonly kind?: string
  42   readonly draftId?: string
  43   readonly sessionId?: string
  44 }
  45 
  46 /** Extract the picker's store key + catalog scope from the chat location. */
  47 export function pickerKeyFor(location: ChatLocation): {
  48   key: string
  49   scope: { readonly kind: 'draft' } | { readonly kind: 'session'; readonly harnessId: string }
  50 } {
  51   if (location.kind === 'session') {
  52     return {
  53       key: `session:${location.sessionId}`,
  54       scope: { kind: 'session', harnessId: location.harnessId ?? '' },
  55     }
  56   }
  57   return { key: `draft:${location.draftId}`, scope: { kind: 'draft' } }
  58 }
  59 
  60 export interface ProfileChatGlueInput {
  61   readonly store?: ProfileSelectionStore
  62   readonly profiles: Pick<ProfileServiceClient, 'beginTurnApplication'> & Partial<Pick<ProfileServiceClient, 'selectForSession' | 'inspectSessionConfig' | 'registerNativeSession' | 'reconcile' | 'prepareNativeCreation'>>
  63   readonly catalog: () => readonly ProfileCatalogGroup[]
  64   readonly catalogState?: Pick<ProfileCatalog, 'message' | 'subscribe' | 'getSnapshot' | 'refresh'>
  65   readonly onOpenManager?: () => void
  66   readonly nativeSessions?: Pick<AgentSessions, 'prepareSubmission'> & Partial<Pick<AgentSessions, 'getSnapshot' | 'subscribe'>>
  67 }
  68 
  69 export function createProfileChatGlue(
  70   scope: ResourceScope,
  71   contributions: ChatContributionsService,
  72   input: ProfileChatGlueInput,
  73 ) {
  74   const store = input.store ?? new ProfileSelectionStore()
  75   const subscribe = (listener: () => void) => {
  76     const stopStore = store.subscribe(listener)
  77     const stopCatalog = input.catalogState?.subscribe(listener)
  78     return () => { stopStore(); stopCatalog?.() }
  79   }
  80   const getSnapshot = () => store.getSnapshot() + (input.catalogState?.getSnapshot() ?? 0)
  81   const scoped = contributions.forScope(scope)
  82   const bindings = new Map<string, { ref: import('@ordessa/plugin-profile-api').SessionRef; selection: ProfileSelection; uiSessionId: string; serverId: string }>()
  83   const retainedSelections = new Map<string, { selection: ProfileSelection; connectionId?: string }>()
  84 
  85   // -- click-order seam (021 appendix §6c F1.2): registration wire calls for
  86   //    one picker key (selectForSession here, chooseForSession on the model
  87   //    side via the projected `serializeRegistration` hook) run strictly one
  88   //    after another, so the backend's action_seq stamps CLICK order, never
  89   //    network race order. One failed task never poisons the queue.
  90   const clickQueues = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>()
  91   const enqueueClick = <T>(key: string, task: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
  92     const tail = clickQueues.get(key) ?? Promise.resolve()
  93     const run = tail.then(task, task)
  94     const settled: Promise<unknown> = run.then(() => undefined, () => undefined)
  95     clickQueues.set(key, settled)
  96     void settled.then(() => {
  97       if (clickQueues.get(key) === settled) clickQueues.delete(key)
  98     })
  99     return run
 100   }
 101 
 102   /** Raw backend registration of one pick (click handler task and the submit
 103    * guard both use it — NEVER enqueue it from inside another queued task). */
 104   const registrationTask = async (key: string, ref: import('@ordessa/plugin-profile-api').SessionRef, selection: ProfileSelection): Promise<void> => {
 105     try {
 106       if (typeof input.profiles.selectForSession !== 'function')
 107         throw new Error('Profile selection registration is unavailable')
 108       if (selection.harnessId !== ref.harnessId)
 109         throw new Error(`所选预设属于另一 Harness（${selection.harnessId}），不能登记到此会话`)
 110       await input.profiles.selectForSession(
 111         `profile-chat.register:${ref.sessionUid}:${selection.profileId}:${++registrationSequence}`,
 112         { sessionRef: ref, profileId: selection.profileId })
 113       store.markRegistered(key, selection)
 114     } catch (error) {
 115       // surfaced in the picker (registrationError), pick kept for retry
 116       store.markRegistrationFailed(key, selection,
 117         error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error))
 118     }
 119   }
 120   let registrationSequence = 0
 121 
 122   const stopNative = input.nativeSessions?.subscribe?.(() => {
 123     const native = input.nativeSessions?.getSnapshot?.()
 124     if (native?.draft?.active === false && native.draft.endedBy !== 'opened') store.clearDraftConfirmations()
 125     if (native?.draft?.endedBy !== 'discarded') return
 126     for (const [key, retained] of retainedSelections)
 127       if (retained.connectionId === native.selectedConnectionId) retainedSelections.delete(key)
 128   })
 129   const nativeLifetime = scope.add({ isDisposed: false as boolean, dispose() {
 130     if (this.isDisposed) return
 131     this.isDisposed = true
 132     stopNative?.(); retainedSelections.clear()
 133   } })
 134   const guard = scoped.addSubmissionGuard({ id: 'ordessa.profile-chat.application', order: 10,
 135     async prepare(snapshot) {
 136       const { key } = pickerKeyFor(snapshot.target)
 137       const target = snapshot.target
 138       const native = input.nativeSessions?.getSnapshot?.()
 139       const retainedKey = JSON.stringify([target.serverInstanceId, native?.selectedConnectionId ?? null, key])
 140       const selected = store.peek(key) ?? (target.kind === 'draft' ? retainedSelections.get(retainedKey)?.selection : undefined)
 141       const currentBinding = target.kind === 'session' ? [...bindings.values()].find(binding =>
 142         binding.uiSessionId === target.sessionId && binding.serverId === target.serverInstanceId) : undefined
 143       if (!selected && !currentBinding) return { status: 'ready' }
 144       if (!input.nativeSessions?.prepareSubmission || !input.profiles.registerNativeSession)
 145         return selected ? { status: 'refused', reason: 'Profile 会话应用尚未接入：缺少后端会话身份与生产应用端口。所选预设和草稿已保留。' } : { status: 'ready' }
 146       const offeredHarness = input.nativeSessions.getSnapshot?.().agent?.connection.harnessId
 147       if (selected && offeredHarness && offeredHarness !== selected.harnessId)
 148         return { status: 'refused', reason: '所选 Profile 属于另一 Harness，请先选择对应连接；没有创建会话。' }
 149       // Brand differences belong to the Harness, not to this frontend: whether a
 150       // given Harness needs pre-creation native parameters is the backend's own
 151       // per-harness verdict (`prepareNativeCreation` answers `not-required` for
 152       // the ones that apply live). The renderer therefore demands the port for
 153       // EVERY draft selection and forwards the call unchanged; it never encodes
 154       // which Harness may skip it.
 155       if (selected && target.kind === 'draft' && !input.profiles.prepareNativeCreation)
 156         return { status: 'refused', reason: '所选 Harness 缺少 Profile 创建前准备服务；草稿和选择已保留。' }
 157       try {
 158         if (selected && target.kind === 'draft') retainedSelections.set(retainedKey, { selection: selected, connectionId: native?.selectedConnectionId })
 159         const owner = await input.nativeSessions.prepareSubmission(selected && input.profiles.prepareNativeCreation ? {
 160           configurationProfileId: selected.profileId,
 161           beforeCreate: async context => {
 162             if (context.serverInstanceId !== target.serverInstanceId || context.harnessId !== selected.harnessId)
 163               throw new Error('Profile preparation context changed')
 164             const operationId = `create:${context.attemptId}`
 165             const result = await input.profiles.prepareNativeCreation!({ connectionId: context.connectionId,
 166               profileId: selected.profileId, operationId })
 167             if (!result || !['prepared', 'not-required'].includes(result.state)
 168                 || result.operationId !== operationId || result.connectionId !== context.connectionId
 169                 || result.profileId !== selected.profileId || result.harnessId !== context.harnessId
 170                 || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.profileRevision) || result.profileRevision < 1
 171                 || Object.keys(result).sort().join(',') !== 'connectionId,harnessId,operationId,profileId,profileRevision,state')
 172               throw new Error('Profile native preparation is unknown or malformed')
 173           },
 174         } : undefined)
 175         if (snapshot.target.serverInstanceId !== owner.serverInstanceId)
 176           return { status: 'refused', reason: 'Profile Server identity changed' }
 177         const identity = JSON.stringify([owner.serverInstanceId, owner.connectionId, owner.nativeSessionId])
 178         let binding = bindings.get(identity)
 179         const selection = selected ?? binding?.selection
 180         if (!selection) return { status: 'ready' }
 181         if (selection.harnessId !== owner.harnessId) return { status: 'refused', reason: 'Profile belongs to another Harness' }
 182         if (!binding) {
 183           const result = await input.profiles.registerNativeSession({ connectionId: owner.connectionId,
 184             nativeSessionId: owner.nativeSessionId, profileId: selection.profileId })
 185           if (result.sessionRef.harnessId !== owner.harnessId || result.sessionRef.nativeSessionKey !== owner.nativeSessionId
 186               || !result.sessionRef.sessionUid || !result.sessionRef.realm) return { status: 'unknown' }
 187           binding = { ref: result.sessionRef, selection, uiSessionId: owner.sessionId, serverId: owner.serverInstanceId }
 188           bindings.set(identity, binding)
 189         }
 190         const inspected = await input.profiles.inspectSessionConfig?.(binding.ref)
 191         if (inspected?.switchState === 'needs_recovery') {
 192           const recovered = await input.profiles.reconcile?.(`profile-reconcile:${snapshot.submissionId}`, binding.ref)
 193           if (!recovered || recovered.state === 'unknown') return { status: 'unknown' }
 194           if (recovered.state === 'confirmed-current') {
 195             // The next explicit send can apply a new selection. This call only reconciles.
 196             return { status: 'refused', reason: 'Profile 应用结果已核实，请再次点击发送；未自动重发。' }
 197           }
 198           return { status: 'refused', reason: 'Profile 已核实为未应用，请再次点击发送重试；未自动重发。' }
 199         }
 200         // Registration + application run as ONE serialized task per session
 201         // key (§6c F1.2): clicks queued around it keep their wire order, and
 202         // the apply never races a click registration.
 203         const capturedRegistered = !!selected && store.isRegistered(key, selected)
 204         return await enqueueClick(key, async (): Promise<
 205           { readonly status: 'ready' } | { readonly status: 'refused'; readonly reason: string }
 206           | { readonly status: 'unknown' }
 207         > => {
 208           const live = store.peek(key)
 209           let outcome: ApplyOutcome
 210           if (live && store.isRegistered(key, live)) {
 211             // the backend already holds the newest click's intent: apply it
 212             outcome = await store.applyOnSubmit(key, binding.ref, input.profiles)
 213           } else if (selected && capturedRegistered) {
 214             // the click-registered pick is the backend's pending even though a
 215             // store-level re-pick arrived meanwhile (it stays for next submit)
 216             outcome = await store.applyOnSubmit(key, binding.ref, input.profiles, selected)
 217           } else if (selected) {
 218             // never registered at click (draft flow / failed click): register
 219             // now, inside this serialized task, then apply the vouched pick
 220             await registrationTask(key, binding.ref, selected)
 221             if (store.peek(key) === selected && !store.isRegistered(key, selected))
 222               return { status: 'refused',
 223                 reason: `所选预设未能登记到会话：${store.registrationError(key) ?? '未知原因'}；草稿与选择已保留，可重试。` }
 224             outcome = await store.applyOnSubmit(key, binding.ref, input.profiles, selected)
 225           } else {
 226             // ordinary bound turn: apply the effective configuration, no reselect
 227             outcome = await store.applyOnSubmit(key, binding.ref, input.profiles, binding.selection)
 228           }
 229           if (!outcome.ok) {
 230             if (outcome.unknown) return { status: 'unknown' }
 231             // Wire/1 currently hides business error details. Query the domain's
 232             // explicit safety state instead of guessing uncertainty from text.
 233             const failed = await input.profiles.inspectSessionConfig?.(binding.ref)
 234             return failed?.switchState === 'needs_recovery' ? { status: 'unknown' }
 235               : { status: 'refused', reason: outcome.reason }
 236           }
 237           binding.selection = outcome.applied
 238           retainedSelections.delete(retainedKey)
 239           return { status: 'ready' }
 240         })
 241       } catch (error) {
 242         // Transport loss cannot be turned into a safe retry or fallback send.
 243         return { status: 'unknown' }
 244       }
 245     },
 246   })
 247   const registration = chatContribution<ProfilePickerProps>({
 248     id: 'ordessa.profile-chat.picker',
 249     slot: 'composer.toolbar',
 250     order: 10,
 251     key: ProfilePickerKey,
 252     project: (context: ChatContributionContext) => {
 253       if (context.slot !== 'composer.toolbar') return { hidden: true }
 254       const { key, scope: pickerScope } = pickerKeyFor(context.location)
 255       const liveProps = (): ProfilePickerProps => {
 256         const location = context.location
 257         const bound = location.kind === 'session' ? [...bindings.values()].find(binding =>
 258           binding.uiSessionId === location.sessionId && binding.serverId === location.serverInstanceId) : undefined
 259         const scope = pickerScope.kind === 'session' && bound ? { ...pickerScope, harnessId: bound.ref.harnessId } : pickerScope
 260         const groups = catalogForLocation(input.catalog(), scope)
 261         return {
 262           label: store.currentLabel(key, bound?.selection.displayName), groups,
 263           statusMessage: input.catalogState?.message || (groups.length ? undefined : '当前 Harness 没有可用预设。'),
 264           // §6c F1.1: a click registration that failed is surfaced here, never
 265           // swallowed — the pick stays unregistered and the user can retry.
 266           registrationError: store.registrationError(key),
 267           onRefresh: input.catalogState ? () => { void input.catalogState!.refresh() } : undefined,
 268           onOpenSelector: input.catalogState ? () => { void input.catalogState!.refresh() } : undefined,
 269           onOpenManager: input.onOpenManager,
 270           // §6c F1.1: the explicit selection REGISTERS at click time for a
 271           // bound session (the backend stamps the click's action_seq); it
 272           // never applies or restarts anything (FR-04). A draft keeps its
 273           // pick in the store; registration happens at submit.
 274           onSelect: (selection: ProfileSelection) => {
 275             store.select(key, selection)
 276             if (location.kind !== 'session') return
 277             void enqueueClick(key, async () => {
 278               try {
 279                 let registered = [...bindings.values()].find(binding =>
 280                   binding.uiSessionId === location.sessionId && binding.serverId === location.serverInstanceId)
 281                 if (!registered) {
 282                   if (!input.nativeSessions?.prepareSubmission || !input.profiles.registerNativeSession)
 283                     throw new Error('原生会话登记端口不可用')
 284                   const owner = await input.nativeSessions.prepareSubmission()
 285                   if (owner.sessionId !== location.sessionId || owner.serverInstanceId !== location.serverInstanceId
 286                     || owner.harnessId !== selection.harnessId) throw new Error('原生会话身份已变化')
 287                   const result = await input.profiles.registerNativeSession({ connectionId: owner.connectionId,
 288                     nativeSessionId: owner.nativeSessionId, profileId: selection.profileId })
 289                   if (result.sessionRef.harnessId !== owner.harnessId
 290                     || result.sessionRef.nativeSessionKey !== owner.nativeSessionId
 291                     || !result.sessionRef.sessionUid || !result.sessionRef.realm) throw new Error('会话登记未确认')
 292                   registered = { ref: result.sessionRef, selection, uiSessionId: owner.sessionId, serverId: owner.serverInstanceId }
 293                   bindings.set(JSON.stringify([owner.serverInstanceId, owner.connectionId, owner.nativeSessionId]), registered)
 294                 }
 295                 await registrationTask(key, registered.ref, selection)
 296               } catch (error) {
 297                 store.markRegistrationFailed(key, selection, error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error))
 298               }
 299             })
 300           },
 301           onClearSelection: () => store.forget(key),
 302           // 021 T04: the registered binding fact of THIS chat session, for the
 303           // model route's bound/unbound dispatch (appendix §3.3). Purely a
 304           // read-side projection; the model guard never applies through it.
 305           boundNativeSession: bound
 306             ? { serverInstanceId: bound.serverId, sessionId: bound.uiSessionId }
 307             : null,
 308           // §6c F1.2: the per-session click-order seam. The model side runs its
 309           // chooseForSession call through this hook (absent → unbound → the
 310           // model side calls directly, semantics unchanged). Read-only
 311           // projection, same channel as `boundNativeSession`.
 312           serializeRegistration: location.kind === 'session'
 313             ? (task: () => Promise<void>) => enqueueClick(key, task)
 314             : null,
 315         }
 316       }
 317       return {
 318         hidden: false,
 319         props: {
 320           ...liveProps(), subscribe, getSnapshot, getLiveProps: liveProps,
 321         },
 322       }
 323     },
 324   })
 325   const disposeRegistration =
 326     scoped.addContribution(registration)
 327   return {
 328     store,
 329     dispose() {
 330       disposeRegistration.dispose()
 331       guard.dispose()
 332       bindings.clear()
 333       nativeLifetime.dispose()
 334     },
 335   }
 336 }
 337 
 338 /** Read-side convenience for the Chat host outlet. */
 339 export function pickerComponent() {
 340   return ProfilePicker
 341 }

### P:plugins/assets/model-provider/chat-contribution/src/next-turn-client.ts
   1 // authored in this line (Z3 T05) — consumer-side next-turn client, E1.
   2 // The submit permit gate itself is C0/Z2 production wiring (api-requests
   3 // REQ-Z3-2/REQ-Z3-4); until it lands, this module is the contribution's
   4 // honest consumer: it queues through the frozen ``modelProvider.*`` wire and
   5 // applies ONLY results whose identity matches its own session and operation.
   6 //
   7 // 021 T04: the client also carries the frozen appendix §2 submit gate
   8 // (``modelProvider.beginTurnApplication``) and the read-only reconcile the
   9 // bound-mode UI sync uses. Semantics locked here:
  10 // - ``submitTurn`` sends the queued intent for THIS exact target with a fresh
  11 //   operation key per explicit attempt (§6b.2: a retry is a new key; the same
  12 //   key only replays a recorded outcome);
  13 // - ``ready`` consumes the local intent (the server consumed its own);
  14 // - ``refused``/``unknown-outcome`` keep the intent (an explicit retry gets a
  15 //   new key; an unknown keeps blocking until an observation settles it);
  16 // - ``no-intent`` passes through: the Profile pipeline owns a bound session's
  17 //   apply, and an empty queue needs none;
  18 // - ``reconcile`` is observe-only (MP-07): it never applies or retries.
  19 //
  20 // 021 §6c F2 (codex review F2): every queue answer also carries the intent's
  21 // handover ROUTE (``intentRoute``). The client records that fate —
  22 // profile-overlay | independent | handover-failed, plus an UNDECIDED latch
  23 // when the answer carries no valid route — and the send gate adjudicates it
  24 // (``intentFate``); a bound fact alone is no longer a sufficient permit.
  25 import type { ComposerLocation, QueuedChoice, ObservedModel } from './selector'
  26 
  27 export const TRANSPORT_MISSING = 'MODEL_PROVIDER_CHAT_TRANSPORT_MISSING'
  28 export const TARGET_UNAVAILABLE = 'CHAT_TARGET_UNAVAILABLE'
  29 
  30 export type WireInvoke = (method: string, params: object) => Promise<unknown>
  31 
  32 export type QueueOutcome =
  33   | { state: 'pending-next-turn'; sequence: number }
  34   | { state: 'refused'; code: string }
  35   | { state: 'unknown-outcome'; code: string }
  36 
  37 /** The frozen appendix §2 submit-gate outcomes a client consumes. */
  38 export type SubmitOutcome =
  39   | { state: 'ready'; sequence: number; application: TurnApplication }
  40   | { state: 'refused'; code: string }
  41   | { state: 'unknown-outcome'; code: string }
  42   | { state: 'no-intent' }
  43 
  44 export interface TurnApplication {
  45   operationId: string
  46   nativeTarget: { owner: string; canonicalUid: string; generation: number }
  47   appliedRevision: string | null
  48   evidenceRef: string
  49 }
  50 
  51 export type ReconcileOutcome =
  52   | { state: 'confirmed'; turnFact: QueuedChoice }
  53   | { state: 'refused'; code: string }
  54   | { state: 'unknown-outcome'; code: string }
  55 
  56 /** The handover route a queue/inspect answer reports (021 §6c F2.2). */
  57 export type IntentRoute = 'profile-overlay' | 'independent' | 'handover-failed'
  58 
  59 /** The newest intent's fate, as the send gate must see it (§6c F2.3):
  60  * - ``routed``: a bound Profile session's overlay owns it — the Profile
  61  *   pipeline applies it on the send; the gate passes only while the binding
  62  *   is still projected;
  63  * - ``independent``: the model queue owns it — an unbound send submits it;
  64  * - ``handover-failed``: the target WAS bound but the overlay handover failed
  65  *   or is unknown — the send is blocked (zero prompt, intent kept);
  66  * - ``undecided``: a queue answer without a valid route — blocked until an
  67  *   observation settles the queue (fail-closed, never read as independent);
  68  * - ``none``: nothing pending — the gate passes. */
  69 export type IntentFate =
  70   | { kind: 'none' }
  71   | { kind: 'routed' }
  72   | { kind: 'independent' }
  73   | { kind: 'handover-failed'; code: string }
  74   | { kind: 'undecided'; code: string }
  75 
  76 const INTENT_ROUTES: readonly IntentRoute[] = ['profile-overlay', 'independent', 'handover-failed']
  77 
  78 function routeOf(answer: { intentRoute?: unknown; intentReason?: unknown }): IntentRoute | null {
  79   return typeof answer.intentRoute === 'string' && INTENT_ROUTES.includes(answer.intentRoute as IntentRoute)
  80     ? answer.intentRoute as IntentRoute : null
  81 }
  82 
  83 /** The frozen wire contract of the queue call (t00-freeze.md §8). */
  84 export const CHOOSE_METHOD = 'modelProvider.chooseForSession'
  85 export const QUERY_METHOD = 'modelProvider.queryChoice'
  86 export const SUBMIT_METHOD = 'modelProvider.beginTurnApplication'
  87 export const RECONCILE_METHOD = 'modelProvider.reconcileChoice'
  88 export type ChoiceObservation =
  89   | { state: 'observed'; desired: QueuedChoice | null; lastConfirmed: QueuedChoice | null; current?: ObservedModel | null }
  90   | { state: 'unknown-outcome'; code: string }
  91 
  92 function targetOf(location: ComposerLocation):
  93   | { ok: true; target: Record<string, string> }
  94   | { ok: false; code: string } {
  95   if (location.kind === 'session') {
  96     const { serverInstanceId, harnessId, acpSessionId } = location.session
  97     if (![serverInstanceId, harnessId, acpSessionId].every(value => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim()))
  98       return { ok: false, code: TARGET_UNAVAILABLE }
  99     return { ok: true, target: { serverInstanceId, harnessId, acpSessionId } }
 100   }
 101   // A draft is not yet a native session: there is nothing to queue a
 102   // next-turn intent against. The caller keeps its draft; nothing is sent.
 103   return { ok: false, code: TARGET_UNAVAILABLE }
 104 }
 105 
 106 export interface NextTurnClient {
 107   queue(choice: QueuedChoice): Promise<QueueOutcome>
 108   query(): Promise<ChoiceObservation>
 109   /** The appendix §2 submit gate for this session's queued intent. A fresh
 110    * operation key per call (§6b.2); the caller decides what a refusal or an
 111    * unknown means for the send — this client only reports and keeps its own
 112    * bookkeeping consistent (a ready consumes the local intent). */
 113   submitTurn(expectedSequence?: number): Promise<SubmitOutcome>
 114   /** Read-only observation of one queued operation (bound-mode UI sync). */
 115   reconcile(operationId?: number): Promise<ReconcileOutcome>
 116   /** A resolution arriving later: only the exact session/operation identity
 117    * is applied; anything else is rejected and leaves the state untouched
 118    * (MP-07: an unattributable outcome is never a success). */
 119   applyResolution(resolution: { session: Record<string, string>; sequence: number;
 120                                 outcome: string; reason?: string }): QueueOutcome | { rejected: true }
 121   readonly pending: QueuedChoice | null
 122   /** The newest intent's handover fate (§6c F2.2/F2.3): the send gate's
 123    * verdict input. A bound fact alone is not a sufficient permit. */
 124   intentFate(): IntentFate
 125   /** Live bookkeeping changes (submit/reconcile outcomes) for the owner UI. */
 126   onUpdate(listener: () => void): () => void
 127 }
 128 
 129 export function createNextTurnClient(
 130   invoke: WireInvoke | undefined, location: ComposerLocation, isCurrent: () => boolean = () => true,
 131 ): NextTurnClient {
 132   if (invoke === undefined) {
 133     // No transport: a typed refusal, never a selector that silently no-ops
 134     // (same discipline as the settings side TRANSPORT_MISSING).
 135     return {
 136       get pending() { return null },
 137       queue: async () => ({ state: 'refused', code: TRANSPORT_MISSING }),
 138       query: async () => ({ state: 'unknown-outcome', code: TRANSPORT_MISSING }),
 139       submitTurn: async () => ({ state: 'refused', code: TRANSPORT_MISSING }),
 140       reconcile: async () => ({ state: 'unknown-outcome', code: TRANSPORT_MISSING }),
 141       applyResolution: () => ({ rejected: true }),
 142       intentFate: () => ({ kind: 'none' as const }),
 143       onUpdate: () => () => undefined,
 144     }
 145   }
 146   let pending: QueuedChoice | null = null
 147   let sequence = 0
 148   let attempts = 0
 149   // §6c F2.2: the newest intent's handover route + the undecided latch.
 150   let route: IntentRoute | null = null
 151   let handoverCode = 'HANDOVER_UNRESOLVED'
 152   let undecided: string | null = null
 153   const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
 154   const notify = () => { for (const listener of [...listeners]) listener() }
 155   const fate = (): IntentFate => {
 156     if (undecided !== null) return { kind: 'undecided', code: undecided }
 157     if (pending === null) return { kind: 'none' }
 158     if (route === 'handover-failed') return { kind: 'handover-failed', code: handoverCode }
 159     if (route === 'profile-overlay') return { kind: 'routed' }
 160     if (route === 'independent') return { kind: 'independent' }
 161     // a queued intent with no known route is an unsafe state, never "independent"
 162     return { kind: 'undecided', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 163   }
 164   const target = targetOf(location)
 165   return {
 166     get pending() { return pending },
 167     intentFate: fate,
 168     onUpdate(listener: () => void) {
 169       listeners.add(listener)
 170       return () => { listeners.delete(listener) }
 171     },
 172     async query(): Promise<ChoiceObservation> {
 173       if (!target.ok || !isCurrent()) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 174       const capturedAttempts = attempts
 175       try {
 176         const answer = await invoke(QUERY_METHOD, { target: target.target }) as {
 177           current?: { modelId?: unknown; provider?: unknown } | null
 178           desired?: { providerConfigId?: string; modelId?: string } | null
 179           lastConfirmed?: { harnessId?: string; providerConfigId?: string; modelId?: string;
 180             providerConfigVersion?: number; evidence?: { applied?: string } } | null
 181           revision?: number | null
 182           intentRoute?: unknown
 183           intentReason?: unknown
 184         }
 185         if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 186         if (attempts !== capturedAttempts) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OBSERVATION_STALE' }
 187         if (!answer || !('desired' in answer) || !('lastConfirmed' in answer)
 188           || (answer.revision !== null && !Number.isSafeInteger(answer.revision)))
 189           return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 190         const choiceOf = (value: { providerConfigId?: string; modelId?: string }) =>
 191           typeof value.providerConfigId === 'string' && !!value.providerConfigId.trim()
 192           && typeof value.modelId === 'string' && !!value.modelId.trim()
 193             ? { harnessId: target.target.harnessId, providerConfigId: value.providerConfigId, modelId: value.modelId } : undefined
 194         const desired = answer.desired === null ? null : answer.desired && choiceOf(answer.desired)
 195         if (desired === undefined || (desired && !Number.isSafeInteger(answer.revision)))
 196           return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 197         if (desired) {
 198           // an observed pending intent must name its handover route (§6c F2.2)
 199           const observedRoute = routeOf(answer)
 200           if (observedRoute === null) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 201           route = observedRoute
 202           handoverCode = typeof answer.intentReason === 'string' && answer.intentReason
 203             ? answer.intentReason : 'HANDOVER_UNRESOLVED'
 204         } else {
 205           route = null
 206         }
 207         // an observation settles whatever the queue latch was holding
 208         undecided = null
 209         const fact = answer.lastConfirmed
 210         const lastConfirmed = fact && fact.harnessId === target.target.harnessId
 211           && Number.isSafeInteger(fact.providerConfigVersion)
 212           && ['read-back-verified', 'reconcile-verified'].includes(fact.evidence?.applied ?? '')
 213           ? choiceOf(fact) ?? null : null
 214         pending = desired
 215         if (typeof answer.revision === 'number') sequence = answer.revision
 216         notify()
 217         const current = answer.current && typeof answer.current.modelId === 'string' && answer.current.modelId.trim()
 218           && (answer.current.provider === null || typeof answer.current.provider === 'string')
 219           ? { modelId: answer.current.modelId, provider: answer.current.provider as string | null } : null
 220         return { state: 'observed', desired, lastConfirmed, ...(Object.hasOwn(answer, 'current') ? { current } : {}) }
 221       } catch (error) {
 222         return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: !isCurrent() ? 'TARGET_STALE'
 223           : (error as { details?: { internalCode?: string } })?.details?.internalCode ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 224       }
 225     },
 226     async queue(choice: QueuedChoice): Promise<QueueOutcome> {
 227       if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'refused', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 228       if (!target.ok) return { state: 'refused', code: target.code }
 229       if (choice.harnessId !== target.target.harnessId) return { state: 'refused', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 230       attempts += 1
 231       try {
 232         const answer = await invoke(CHOOSE_METHOD, {
 233           target: target.target,
 234           choice: { providerConfigId: choice.providerConfigId, modelId: choice.modelId },
 235           operationKey: `chat-queue-${location.kind === 'session' ? location.session.acpSessionId : 'draft'}-${attempts}-${globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()}`,
 236         }) as { outcome?: string; sequence?: number; intentRoute?: unknown; intentReason?: unknown }
 237         if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'refused', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 238         if (answer?.outcome !== 'pending-next-turn' || !Number.isSafeInteger(answer.sequence)) {
 239           undecided = undecided ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN'
 240           return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 241         }
 242         const ackSequence = answer.sequence as number
 243         // a late acknowledgement of an older click never overrides a newer
 244         // recorded sequence (exact-sequence fencing, M12)
 245         if (sequence > 0 && ackSequence < sequence) {
 246           return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OBSERVATION_STALE' }
 247         }
 248         const answerRoute = routeOf(answer)
 249         if (answerRoute === null) {
 250           // §6c F2.2: an answer that names no handover route is an unsafe
 251           // state — undecided, never "independent"
 252           undecided = 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN'
 253           return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 254         }
 255         sequence = ackSequence
 256         route = answerRoute
 257         handoverCode = typeof answer.intentReason === 'string' && answer.intentReason
 258           ? answer.intentReason : 'HANDOVER_UNRESOLVED'
 259         undecided = null
 260         pending = choice
 261         notify()
 262         return { state: 'pending-next-turn', sequence }
 263       } catch (error) {
 264         if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'refused', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 265         const fact = error as { outcome?: string; details?: { internalCode?: string } }
 266         const code = fact?.details?.internalCode
 267         // a definitive refusal did not change the server's queue
 268         if (fact.outcome === 'Refused' || (code && fact.outcome !== 'Unknown'))
 269           return { state: 'refused', code: code ?? 'SELECTION_REFUSED' }
 270         undecided = code ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN'
 271         return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: code ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 272       }
 273     },
 274     async submitTurn(expectedSequence?: number): Promise<SubmitOutcome> {
 275       if (!target.ok) return { state: 'refused', code: target.code }
 276       if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'refused', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 277       // §6b.2: one operation key per explicit attempt; the next attempt (an
 278       // explicit retry after refused/unknown) carries a NEW key, and the same
 279       // key only ever replays a recorded outcome.
 280       attempts += 1
 281       const operationKey = `chat-submit-${target.target.acpSessionId}-${attempts}-${globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()}`
 282       const frozen = Number.isSafeInteger(expectedSequence) ? expectedSequence
 283         : (Number.isSafeInteger(sequence) && sequence > 0 ? sequence : undefined)
 284       // only the intent this submit actually froze may be consumed by its
 285       // ready answer; a pick made while the submit was in flight belongs to
 286       // the NEXT turn (FR-04/M12)
 287       const tracked = pending
 288       try {
 289         const answer = await invoke(SUBMIT_METHOD, {
 290           target: target.target, operationKey,
 291           ...(frozen !== undefined ? { expectedSequence: frozen } : {}),
 292         }) as {
 293           outcome?: string; sequence?: number | null; reason?: string | null
 294           application?: TurnApplication | null
 295         }
 296         if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'refused', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 297         const reason = typeof answer?.reason === 'string' && answer.reason
 298           ? answer.reason : 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN'
 299         if (answer?.outcome === 'no-intent') return { state: 'no-intent' }
 300         if (answer?.outcome === 'ready') {
 301           const application = answer.application
 302           if (!application || typeof application.operationId !== 'string'
 303             || !application.operationId
 304             || typeof application.evidenceRef !== 'string' || !application.evidenceRef
 305             || !application.nativeTarget
 306             || typeof application.nativeTarget.canonicalUid !== 'string'
 307             || !Number.isSafeInteger(application.nativeTarget.generation)) {
 308             // a ready answer without real evidence references is not ready
 309             return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 310           }
 311           pending = tracked === null ? pending : null
 312           if (Number.isSafeInteger(answer.sequence)) sequence = answer.sequence!
 313           route = null
 314           undecided = null
 315           notify()
 316           return { state: 'ready', sequence: answer.sequence ?? frozen ?? 0, application }
 317         }
 318         if (answer?.outcome === 'refused') {
 319           notify()
 320           return { state: 'refused', code: reason }
 321         }
 322         notify()
 323         return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: reason }
 324       } catch (error) {
 325         if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'refused', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 326         const fact = error as { outcome?: string; details?: { internalCode?: string } }
 327         const code = fact?.details?.internalCode
 328         notify()
 329         return fact.outcome === 'Refused' || (code && fact.outcome !== 'Unknown')
 330           ? { state: 'refused', code: code ?? 'SELECTION_REFUSED' }
 331           : { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: code ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 332       }
 333     },
 334     async reconcile(operationId?: number): Promise<ReconcileOutcome> {
 335       if (!target.ok) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: TARGET_UNAVAILABLE }
 336       if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 337       const id: number = Number.isSafeInteger(operationId) ? operationId as number : sequence
 338       if (!Number.isSafeInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
 339         return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 340       }
 341       try {
 342         const answer = await invoke(RECONCILE_METHOD, {
 343           target: target.target, operationId: id,
 344         }) as {
 345           outcome?: string; reason?: string
 346           turnFact?: { providerConfigId?: string; modelId?: string } | null
 347         }
 348         if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 349         const fact = answer?.turnFact
 350         if (answer?.outcome === 'confirmed' && fact
 351           && typeof fact.providerConfigId === 'string' && typeof fact.modelId === 'string') {
 352           pending = null
 353           route = null
 354           undecided = null
 355           notify()
 356           return { state: 'confirmed', turnFact: {
 357             harnessId: target.target.harnessId,
 358             providerConfigId: fact.providerConfigId, modelId: fact.modelId } }
 359         }
 360         const code = typeof answer?.reason === 'string' && answer.reason
 361           ? answer.reason : 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN'
 362         notify()
 363         return answer?.outcome === 'refused'
 364           ? { state: 'refused', code } : { state: 'unknown-outcome', code }
 365       } catch (error) {
 366         if (!isCurrent()) return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: 'TARGET_STALE' }
 367         const failure = error as { details?: { internalCode?: string } }
 368         return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: failure?.details?.internalCode ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 369       }
 370     },
 371     applyResolution(resolution): QueueOutcome | { rejected: true } {
 372       if (!isCurrent()) return { rejected: true }
 373       if (!target.ok || !pending) return { rejected: true }
 374       const sameSession = Object.entries(target.target)
 375         .every(([key, value]) => resolution.session[key] === value)
 376       if (!sameSession || resolution.sequence !== sequence) return { rejected: true }
 377       if (resolution.outcome === 'confirmed') {
 378         pending = null
 379         route = null
 380         undecided = null
 381         notify()
 382         return { state: 'pending-next-turn', sequence }  // applied; queue empty again
 383       }
 384       notify()
 385       return { state: resolution.outcome === 'refused' ? 'refused' : 'unknown-outcome',
 386                code: resolution.reason ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' }
 387     },
 388   }
 389 }

### P:plugins/assets/model-provider/chat-contribution/src/chat-api-entry.tsx

  44 /** A per-session click-order serializer (021 §6c F1.2): tasks run one after
  45  * another, in submission order, so both contributions' registration wire
  46  * calls reach the Server in click order. */
  47 export type RegistrationEnqueue = <T>(task: () => Promise<T>) => Promise<T>

  49 export interface SelectorWiring {
  50   service: ModelProviderService
  51   /** The Server wire invoke; undefined = typed refusal at queue time
  52    * (REQ-Z3-5 still OPEN), never a silent no-op. */
  53   invoke?: WireInvoke
  54   sessions?: Pick<AgentSessions, 'getSnapshot' | 'subscribe' | 'prepareSubmission'>
  55   /** The bound/unbound dispatch fact for one chat location, read from the
  56    * Profile glue's registered bindings (see `boundFactOf`). Absent -> the
  57    * guard still dispatches correctly (the server decides), but the selector
  58    * skips its bound-mode read-only reconcile. */
  59   boundProbe?: (location: ChatLocation) => boolean
  60   /** The §6c F1.2 click-order seam: returns the Profile glue's per-session
  61    * serializer for this location, or null when the glue is absent/unbound
  62    * (the choose call then goes out directly, semantics unchanged). */
  63   registrationSeam?: (location: ChatLocation) => RegistrationEnqueue | null
  64 }
  65 
  66 const absentSnapshot: AgentWorkspaceSnapshot = { available: [] }
  67 const absentSubscribe = () => () => undefined
