  69 /** Renders the migrated selector against the published ChatLocation shape,
  70  * owning a per-location next-turn client (queue → pending-next-turn;
  71  * late/foreign resolutions are rejected inside the client). */
  72 export function ChatModelSelector({ wiring, location }: {
  73   wiring: SelectorWiring
  74   location: ChatLocation
  75 }) {
  76   const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(wiring.sessions?.subscribe ?? absentSubscribe,
  77     wiring.sessions?.getSnapshot ?? (() => absentSnapshot), wiring.sessions?.getSnapshot ?? (() => absentSnapshot))
  78   const identity = JSON.stringify([location.kind, location.connectionId,
  79     location.kind === 'session' ? location.sessionId : location.draftId,
  80     location.runtimeGeneration, snapshot.selectedConnectionId, snapshot.agent?.selectedSessionId,
  81     snapshot.draft?.active, snapshot.selectionRevision, snapshot.runtimeGeneration])
  82   return <NativeSessionSelector key={identity} wiring={wiring} location={location} snapshot={snapshot} />
  83 }
  84 
  85 function NativeSessionSelector({ wiring, location, snapshot }: {
  86   wiring: SelectorWiring; location: ChatLocation; snapshot: AgentWorkspaceSnapshot
  87 }) {
  88   const [owner, setOwner] = useState<ComposerLocation | undefined>()
  89   const [reason, setReason] = useState('正在确认原生会话身份。')
  90   const [queued, setQueued] = useState<QueuedChoice | null>(null)
  91   const [lastConfirmed, setLastConfirmed] = useState<QueuedChoice | null>(null)
  92   const [current, setCurrent] = useState<import('./selector').ObservedModel | null>(null)
  93   const [queueError, setQueueError] = useState('')
  94   const [queueing, setQueueing] = useState(false)
  95   const live = useRef(true)
  96   const busy = useRef(false)
  97   const sessionId = location.kind === 'session' ? location.sessionId : undefined
  98   const isCurrent = useCallback(() => {
  99     const current = wiring.sessions?.getSnapshot()
 100     return live.current && location.kind === 'session' && !!current && !current.draft?.active
 101       && current.selectedConnectionId === location.connectionId
 102       && current.agent?.selectedSessionId === location.sessionId
 103       && current.agent?.connection.status === 'connected'
 104       && current.selectionRevision === snapshot.selectionRevision
 105       && current.runtimeGeneration === snapshot.runtimeGeneration
 106       && (location.runtimeGeneration === undefined || current.runtimeGeneration === location.runtimeGeneration)
 107   }, [wiring.sessions, location.kind, location.connectionId, sessionId, location.runtimeGeneration,
 108     snapshot.selectionRevision, snapshot.runtimeGeneration])
 109   useEffect(() => {
 110     live.current = true
 111     let active = true
 112     if (location.kind !== 'session') setReason('选择模型需要已有原生会话；草稿不会创建会话。')
 113     else if (!isCurrent() || !wiring.sessions?.prepareSubmission) setReason('当前原生会话身份不可用。')
 114     else {
 115       // Only the already-selected session branch is entered; never prepare a draft on render.
 116       void wiring.sessions.prepareSubmission().then(value => {
 117         if (!active || !isCurrent()) return
 118         if (value.sessionId !== location.sessionId
 119           || (location.serverInstanceId && value.serverInstanceId !== location.serverInstanceId)
 120           || ![value.serverInstanceId, value.harnessId, value.nativeSessionId].every(part => typeof part === 'string' && part.trim())) {
 121           setReason('原生会话身份不匹配。'); return
 122         }
 123         setOwner({ kind: 'session', session: { serverInstanceId: value.serverInstanceId,
 124           harnessId: value.harnessId, acpSessionId: value.nativeSessionId } })
 125         setReason('')
 126       }, cause => { if (active && isCurrent()) setReason(cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause)) })
 127     }
 128     return () => { active = false; live.current = false }
 129   }, [wiring.sessions, location.kind, sessionId, location.serverInstanceId, isCurrent])
 130   const client = useMemo(() => createNextTurnClient(wiring.invoke, owner ?? { kind: 'draft' }, isCurrent),
 131     [wiring.invoke, owner, isCurrent])
 132   // The submission guard of this wiring reads exactly this client per session,
 133   // so the send gate and the composer UI share one queue bookkeeping.
 134   useEffect(() => {
 135     if (!owner || owner.kind !== 'session') return
 136     const identity = JSON.stringify(owner.session)
 137     const clients = clientRegistryOf(wiring)
 138     clients.set(identity, { client, isCurrent })
 139     return () => { clients.delete(identity) }
 140   }, [client, owner, isCurrent, wiring])
 141   // Submit outcomes and reconciles update the projection live: a ready
 142   // consumed the intent (queue empty), a refusal keeps it visible.
 143   useEffect(() => client.onUpdate(() => {
 144     setQueued(client.pending)
 145     if (client.pending === null) setQueueError('')
 146   }), [client])
 147   const queryEpoch = useRef(0)
 148   const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
 149     if (!owner || busy.current || !isCurrent()) return
 150     const epoch = ++queryEpoch.current
 151     const observation = await client.query()
 152     if (!isCurrent() || epoch !== queryEpoch.current) return
 153     if (observation.state === 'observed') {
 154       setQueued(observation.desired); setLastConfirmed(observation.lastConfirmed); setCurrent(observation.current ?? null); setQueueError('')
 155     } else {
 156       setCurrent(null)
 157       setQueueError(`模型选择状态未知：${observation.code}；重新打开可查询，不会重试选择。`)
 158     }
 159   }, [owner, isCurrent, client])
 160   const observedOwner = useRef<ComposerLocation | undefined>(undefined)
 161   const runStatus = Object.values(snapshot.agent?.runs ?? {})
 162     .filter(run => location.kind === 'session' && run.sessionId === location.sessionId).at(-1)?.status ?? 'idle'
 163   // Chat reprojects location on every keystroke. Its object identity is not a
 164   // turn/application event: observe only the derived binding fact here.
 165   const profileBound = wiring.boundProbe?.(location) === true
 166   useEffect(() => {
 167     const firstObservation = owner !== undefined && observedOwner.current !== owner
 168     observedOwner.current = owner
 169     if (['running', 'starting', 'stop-requested'].includes(runStatus)) {
 170       // One read-only initial observation, never a mutation of the active turn.
 171       if (firstObservation) void refresh()
 172       return
 173     }
 174     // Bound mode (appendix §3.3): the Profile pipeline applied the intent, so
 175     // this side only syncs its projection with a read-only observation after
 176     // the send; unbound mode's submit already consumed the queue.
 177     if (profileBound && client.pending) {
 178       const epoch = ++queryEpoch.current
 179       void client.reconcile().then(outcome => {
 180         if (!isCurrent() || epoch !== queryEpoch.current) return
 181         if (outcome.state === 'refused') {
 182           setQueueError(`模型选择被核实为未应用：${outcome.code}；意图已保留。`)
 183         } else if (outcome.state === 'unknown-outcome') {
 184           setQueueError(`模型选择结果未知：${outcome.code}；请先核实。`)
 185         }
 186         return refresh()
 187       })
 188       return
 189     }
 190     void refresh()
 191   }, [refresh, runStatus, profileBound, client, isCurrent, owner])
 192   const inspect = useCallback(async (choice: QueuedChoice, version?: number) => {
 193     if (!owner || owner.kind !== 'session' || !wiring.invoke || !isCurrent())
 194       return { eligibility: 'unknown' as const, reason: '原生会话身份不可用。' }
 195     try {
 196       const answer = await wiring.invoke('modelProvider.inspectChoice', { target: owner.session,
 197         choice: { providerConfigId: choice.providerConfigId, modelId: choice.modelId } }) as {
 198           verdict?: string; reason?: string; providerConfigId?: string; configVersion?: number
 199         }
 200       if (!isCurrent()) return { eligibility: 'unknown' as const, reason: '会话已切换。' }
 201       if (answer?.providerConfigId !== choice.providerConfigId || !Number.isSafeInteger(answer.configVersion)
 202         || (version !== undefined && answer.configVersion !== version))
 203         return { eligibility: 'unknown' as const, reason: '模型判断的配置身份或版本不可核实。' }
 204       return { eligibility: answer.verdict === 'supported' ? 'ready' as const
 205         : answer.verdict === 'unsupported' ? 'unsupported' as const : 'unknown' as const,
 206         reason: answer.reason ?? (answer.verdict === 'supported' ? undefined : '模型可选择性未获确认。') }
 207     } catch (cause) {
 208       return { eligibility: 'unknown' as const, reason: cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause) }
 209     }
 210   }, [owner, wiring.invoke, isCurrent])
 211   const queue = async (choice: QueuedChoice) => {
 212     if (busy.current || !isCurrent()) return
 213     ++queryEpoch.current // a query begun before this explicit choice cannot overwrite its receipt
 214     busy.current = true; setQueueing(true); setQueueError('')
 215     try {
 216       const evidence = await inspect(choice, choice.configVersion)
 217       if (!isCurrent()) return
 218       if (evidence.eligibility !== 'ready') { setQueueError(evidence.reason ?? '此模型暂不可选。'); return }
 219       // §6c F1.2: the click's chooseForSession joins the Profile glue's
 220       // per-session click-order queue when one is projected, so the two
 221       // contributions' registration calls reach the Server in click order;
 222       // without the seam (no Profile glue / unbound) it goes out directly.
 223       const enqueue = wiring.registrationSeam?.(location) ?? null
 224       const outcome = enqueue ? await enqueue(() => client.queue(choice)) : await client.queue(choice)
 225       if (!isCurrent()) return
 226       if (outcome.state === 'pending-next-turn') setQueued(client.pending)
 227       else setQueueError(outcome.state === 'refused' ? `模型选择被拒绝：${outcome.code}` : `模型选择结果未知：${outcome.code}；请先核实。`)
 228     } finally {
 229       busy.current = false
 230       if (live.current) setQueueing(false)
 231     }
 232   }
 233   return <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4, minWidth: 0, maxWidth: '100%' }}>
 234     {reason && <span role="status" data-testid="model-owner-note" title={reason}
 235       aria-label={`${reason}；打开模型菜单查看完整原因。`}
 236       style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--ordessa-color-muted,#77777e)', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
 237       {location.kind === 'draft' ? '需先建会话' : '身份待确认'}
 238     </span>}
 239     {queueError && <span role="alert" data-testid="model-queue-error" title={queueError}
 240       style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--ordessa-color-danger,#b3261e)', minWidth: 64, maxWidth: 220,
 241         overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{queueError}</span>}
 242     <ModelSelector service={wiring.service} location={owner ?? adaptLocation(location)}
 243       inspectChoice={inspect} disabled={!owner || queueing || !isCurrent()} queued={queued}
 244       lastConfirmed={lastConfirmed} current={current} onOpen={() => { void refresh() }}
 245       statusReason={reason}
 246       onQueue={choice => { void queue(choice) }} />
 247   </span>
 248 }
 249 
 250 /** The component bound to {@link ModelSelectorKey}. Chat resolves a
 251  * contribution by binding its key through `ui.bind(key, { required: false })`,
 252  * so the contribution registration alone renders nothing: the provider for
 253  * this key must be registered too. The service and the wire port are closed
 254  * over here, never passed through Chat's props. */
 255 export function modelSelectorComponent(wiring: SelectorWiring): ComponentType<{ location: ChatLocation }> {
 256   return function ModelSelectorForLocation(props: { location: ChatLocation }) {
 257     return <ChatModelSelector wiring={wiring} location={props.location} />
 258   }
 259 }
 260 
 261 function adaptLocation(location: ChatLocation) {
 262   // ChatLocation → the selector's session reference; a draft has no native
 263   // session id, so the selector shows its honest "pick a harness/session
 264   // first" degradation and the client refuses to queue (TARGET_UNAVAILABLE).
 265   if (location.kind === 'session') {
 266     return { kind: 'session' as const, session: {
 267       serverInstanceId: location.serverInstanceId ?? '',
 268       harnessId: location.harnessId ?? '',
 269       acpSessionId: location.sessionId,
 270     } }
 271   }
 272   return { kind: 'draft' as const, target: {
 273     serverInstanceId: location.serverInstanceId ?? '',
 274     harnessId: location.harnessId ?? '',
 275     projectId: location.projectId ?? '',
 276   } }
 277 }
 278 
 279 /** Registers the selector under `scope`; disposing the returned handle (or
 280  * closing the scope) revokes exactly this registration — Chat keeps working
 281  * without it (US-4 absence comparison). Nothing here touches Chat's internal
 282  * store: the wiring travels as projection props keyed by ModelSelectorKey.
 283  *
 284  * 021 T04: the same scope also carries this route's submission guard. The
 285  * guard and the selector component share one client registry, so the send
 286  * gate reads exactly the queue bookkeeping the composer UI shows. */
 287 export function registerModelSelector(
 288   chat: ChatContributionsService, scope: ResourceScope, wiring: SelectorWiring,
 289 ): IDisposable {
 290   const registration = chatContribution<{ location: ChatLocation }>({
 291     id: MODEL_SELECTOR_ID,
 292     slot: 'composer.toolbar',
 293     order: 100,
 294     key: ModelSelectorKey,
 295     project: (context) => {
 296       if (context.slot !== 'composer.toolbar') return { hidden: true as const }
 297       return {
 298         hidden: false as const,
 299         props: { location: context.location },
 300       }
 301     },
 302   })
 303   // The wiring travels through the KEY-BOUND COMPONENT (see
 304   // `modelSelectorComponent`), not through these projection props: Chat's
 305   // contribution context is a read-only display context and must not be handed
 306   // a service instance.
 307   void wiring
 308   const scoped = chat.forScope(scope)
 309   const clients = clientRegistryOf(wiring)
 310   const guard = scoped.addSubmissionGuard({
 311     id: TURN_APPLICATION_GUARD_ID,
 312     order: TURN_APPLICATION_GUARD_ORDER,
 313     async prepare(snapshot) {
 314       const target = snapshot.target
 315       if (target.kind !== 'session') return { status: 'ready' }
 316       const identity = targetIdentityOf(target)
 317       if (identity === undefined) return { status: 'ready' }
 318       // §6c F2.3 (codex review F2): the bound fact alone is no longer a
 319       // sufficient send permit — the NEWEST intent's handover fate gates the
 320       // send. The bound fact is re-projected fresh on every send, so a
 321       // registration/unregistration between click and send re-evaluates here.
 322       const bound = boundFactOf(chat, target)
 323       const registered = clients.get(JSON.stringify(identity))
 324       let fate = registered?.client.intentFate?.() ?? null
 325       if (bound && fate?.kind === 'independent') {
 326         // First Profile registration may have adopted this queue since the
 327         // click. Observe that handover; never apply it a second time here.
 328         const observation = await registered!.client.query()
 329         if (observation.state !== 'observed') return { status: 'unknown' }
 330         fate = registered!.client.intentFate()
 331       }
 332       if (bound && (!registered || fate?.kind === 'independent')) {
 333         try {
 334           if (!wiring.invoke) return { status: 'unknown' }
 335           const state = await wiring.invoke('modelProvider.queryChoice', { target: identity }) as {
 336             desired?: unknown; intentRoute?: string | null
 337           }
 338           if (state?.desired != null && state.intentRoute !== 'profile-overlay') {
 339             return { status: 'refused', reason: '模型意图尚未完成 Profile 交接；草稿与选择已保留。' }
 340           }
 341           if (!state || !Object.hasOwn(state, 'desired')) return { status: 'unknown' }
 342         } catch { return { status: 'unknown' } }
 343       }
 344       if (fate?.kind === 'handover-failed') {
 345         // the intent never reached the Profile overlay (or the handover is
 346         // unknown): zero prompt, draft and intent kept, explicit re-pick
 347         return { status: 'refused',
 348           reason: `模型选择转交 Profile 会话失败（${fate.code}）；本次发送已阻止，草稿与所选模型已保留，请重新选择模型。` }
 349       }
 350       if (fate?.kind === 'undecided') {
 351         // the queue answer named no route: an unsafe state is never read as
 352         // "independent" — blocked until an observation settles it
 353         return { status: 'refused',
 354           reason: `模型选择结果未知（${fate.code}）；本次发送已阻止，草稿与所选模型已保留，请重新打开模型选择器核实。` }
 355       }
 356       if (fate?.kind === 'routed' && !bound) {
 357         // the intent was handed to a Profile session whose binding has since
 358         // vanished: passing would send with zero evidence about the model
 359         return { status: 'refused',
 360           reason: '模型选择已转交 Profile 会话，但该绑定在发送前消失了；无证据不放行，请重新选择模型。' }
 361       }
 362       if (!bound) {
 363         const outcome = registered
 364           ? await registered.client.submitTurn()
 365           : await submitBlindly(wiring.invoke, identity)
 366         if (outcome.state === 'ready' || outcome.state === 'no-intent') return { status: 'ready' }
 367         if (outcome.state === 'refused') {
 368           return { status: 'refused', reason: `模型应用被拒绝：${outcome.code}；所选模型与草稿已保留，可显式重试。` }
 369         }
 370         return { status: 'unknown' }
 371       }
 372       // bound: the Profile guard (order 10, already run) performed the one
 373       // real application; this side only syncs its projection afterwards.
 374       return { status: 'ready' }
 375     },
 376   })
 377   const handle = scoped.addContribution(registration)
 378   let disposed = false
 379   return {
 380     get isDisposed() { return disposed },
 381     dispose() {
 382       if (disposed) return
 383       disposed = true
 384       guard.dispose()
 385       handle.dispose()
 386     },
 387   } as IDisposable
 388 }
 389 
 390 /** The live client registry one wiring shares between its composer components
 391  * (writers) and its submission guard (reader). Keyed by the wiring object, so
 392  * two registrations never share bookkeeping. */
 393 const clientRegistries = new WeakMap<SelectorWiring, Map<string, { client: NextTurnClient; isCurrent: () => boolean }>>()
 394 export function clientRegistryOf(wiring: SelectorWiring):
 395   Map<string, { client: NextTurnClient; isCurrent: () => boolean }> {
 396   let clients = clientRegistries.get(wiring)
 397   if (!clients) {
 398     clients = new Map()
 399     clientRegistries.set(wiring, clients)
 400   }
 401   return clients
 402 }
 403 
 404 /** The three native reference keys of a session location, or undefined when
 405  * the location cannot honestly name one (a missing key is never guessed). */
 406 export function targetIdentityOf(target: ChatLocation): Record<string, string> | undefined {
 407   if (target.kind !== 'session') return undefined
 408   const { serverInstanceId, harnessId, sessionId } = target
 409   if (![serverInstanceId, harnessId, sessionId].every(
 410     value => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim())) return undefined
 411   return { serverInstanceId: serverInstanceId!, harnessId: harnessId!, acpSessionId: sessionId! }
 412 }
 413 
 414 /** The bound fact of one chat session, read through the published read-side
 415  * contribution view of the Profile glue. The glue projects its registered
 416  * binding (a backend fact it learned at registerNativeSession) into its live
 417  * picker props; its absence — no Profile extension, no binding — reads as
 418  * unbound. Never a guess from the UI session id alone. */
 419 export function boundFactOf(chat: ChatContributionsService, target: ChatLocation): boolean {
 420   if (target.kind !== 'session') return false
 421   for (const view of chat.contributionsBySlot('composer.toolbar').getSnapshot()) {
 422     if (view.id !== PROFILE_BINDING_CONTRIBUTION_ID || !view.project) continue
 423     try {
 424       const projected = view.project({
 425         slot: 'composer.toolbar', location: target, connection: { status: 'unknown' },
 426       })
 427       if (projected.hidden) continue
 428       const bound = (projected.props as { boundNativeSession?: { serverInstanceId?: string; sessionId?: string } | null })
 429         ?.boundNativeSession
 430       if (bound && bound.serverInstanceId === target.serverInstanceId
 431         && bound.sessionId === target.sessionId) return true
 432     } catch {
 433       // an unreadable projection is absence, never a bound verdict
 434     }
 435   }
 436   return false
 437 }
 438 
 439 /** The §6c F1.2 click-order seam of one chat session, read through the same
 440  * published read-side projection as {@link boundFactOf}: the Profile glue's
 441  * per-session registration serializer. Null — no Profile extension, no
 442  * binding, unreadable projection — means the caller invokes its registration
 443  * wire call directly, exactly as before the seam existed. */
 444 export function profileRegistrationSeamOf(chat: ChatContributionsService,
 445                                           target: ChatLocation): RegistrationEnqueue | null {
 446   if (target.kind !== 'session') return null
 447   for (const view of chat.contributionsBySlot('composer.toolbar').getSnapshot()) {
 448     if (view.id !== PROFILE_BINDING_CONTRIBUTION_ID || !view.project) continue
 449     try {
 450       const projected = view.project({
 451         slot: 'composer.toolbar', location: target, connection: { status: 'unknown' },
 452       })
 453       if (projected.hidden) continue
 454       const hook = (projected.props as { serializeRegistration?: RegistrationEnqueue | null })
 455         ?.serializeRegistration
 456       if (typeof hook === 'function') return hook
 457     } catch {
 458       // an unreadable projection is absence, never a seam
 459     }
 460   }
 461   return null
 462 }
 463 
 464 async function submitBlindly(invoke: WireInvoke | undefined,
 465                               target: Record<string, string>) {
 466   // The composer component did not register a client (e.g. the picker was
 467   // never opened). The server still owns the truth: a queued intent applies,
 468   // an empty queue answers no-intent and the send passes.
 469   if (!invoke) return { state: 'refused', code: 'MODEL_PROVIDER_CHAT_TRANSPORT_MISSING' } as const
 470   try {
 471     const answer = await invoke('modelProvider.beginTurnApplication', {
 472       target, operationKey: `chat-submit-${target.acpSessionId}-${globalThis.crypto.randomUUID()}`,
 473     }) as { outcome?: string; reason?: string | null }
 474     if (answer?.outcome === 'ready' || answer?.outcome === 'no-intent') return { state: 'ready' } as const
 475     if (answer?.outcome === 'refused') return { state: 'refused', code: answer.reason ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' } as const
 476     return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: answer?.reason ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' } as const
 477   } catch (error) {
 478     const fact = error as { details?: { internalCode?: string } }
 479     return { state: 'unknown-outcome', code: fact?.details?.internalCode ?? 'OPERATION_UNKNOWN' } as const
 480   }
 481 }
 482 
 483 export { ModelSelector }

### P:plugins/assets/model-provider/chat-contribution/src/selector.tsx
   1 import { useCallback, useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
   2 import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
   3 import { ELIGIBILITY_GLYPHS, ELIGIBILITY_LABELS, type Eligibility } from '@extensions/ordessa.model-provider/contract.js'
   4 import type { ModelProviderService, ProviderConfigView } from '@extensions/ordessa.model-provider/contract.js'
   5 
   6 /** This domain's own view of where the composer is: a bound session, or a draft
   7  * that has no native session yet. It is deliberately NOT the Chat contract's
   8  * `ChatLocation` — `chat-api-entry.tsx` adapts that onto this shape, so the
   9  * selector and the queue client never import a contract, a stub or a host
  10  * internals. */
  11 export type ComposerLocation =
  12   | { kind: 'session'; session: { serverInstanceId: string; harnessId: string; acpSessionId: string } }
  13   | { kind: 'draft'; target?: { serverInstanceId: string; harnessId: string; projectId: string } }
  14 
  15 /** Per-session eligibility evidence: the harness adapter's verdict for
  16  * (config, model) in THIS session. In this baseline the seam behind it is not
  17  * wired (integration dependency #2), so the adapter is usually absent - the
  18  * selector then answers "此会话暂不可选模型", never a fabricated ready. */
  19 export interface SessionEligibilitySource {
  20   eligibility(harnessId: string, providerConfigId: string, modelId: string): Eligibility
  21   reason?(harnessId: string, providerConfigId: string, modelId: string): string | undefined
  22 }
  23 
  24 const ABSENT_SOURCE: SessionEligibilitySource = {
  25   eligibility: () => 'unknown',
  26   reason: (_h, _c, _m) => '尚未取得此会话的模型应用证据，无法证明可在本会话下轮使用',
  27 }
  28 
  29 /** One queued "next turn" choice, atomic (Provider config + model). */
  30 export interface QueuedChoice {
  31   harnessId: string
  32   providerConfigId: string
  33   modelId: string
  34   configVersion?: number
  35 }
  36 
  37 export interface ObservedModel {
  38   modelId: string
  39   provider: string | null
  40 }
  41 
  42 export interface ModelSelectorProps {
  43   service: ModelProviderService
  44   location: ComposerLocation
  45   eligibilitySource?: SessionEligibilitySource
  46   queued?: QueuedChoice | null
  47   onQueue?(choice: QueuedChoice): void
  48   inspectChoice?(choice: QueuedChoice, version: number): Promise<{ eligibility: Eligibility; reason?: string }>
  49   disabled?: boolean
  50   lastConfirmed?: QueuedChoice | null
  51   current?: ObservedModel | null
  52   onOpen?(): void
  53   /** Display-only explanation supplied by the native session owner. */
  54   statusReason?: string
  55 }
  56 
  57 /** D04: a load failure is its own visible state with an explicit retry. It is
  58  * never rendered as an empty-but-plausible list — "this Harness has no
  59  * Provider config" and "the catalogue could not be read" are different facts
  60  * and the user must be able to retry the second one. */
  61 type Catalog =
  62   | { readonly state: 'loading' }
  63   | { readonly state: 'ready'; readonly configs: readonly ProviderConfigView[] }
  64   | { readonly state: 'error'; readonly message: string }
  65 
  66 const describe = (cause: unknown): string =>
  67   cause instanceof Error && cause.message ? cause.message : String(cause)
  68 
  69 /** The composer model selector: one compact `Provider › Model` control.
  70  * The harness is already bound by the session (or the draft target) - there
  71  * is deliberately no harness dropdown here; new sessions pick their harness
  72  * at creation, not in the composer. */
  73 export function ModelSelector({ service, location, eligibilitySource,
  74                                 queued, onQueue, inspectChoice, disabled, lastConfirmed, current, onOpen, statusReason }: ModelSelectorProps) {
  75   const source = eligibilitySource ?? ABSENT_SOURCE
  76   const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  77   const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<Catalog>({ state: 'loading' })
  78   const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0)
  79   const [inspectionAttempt, setInspectionAttempt] = useState(0)
  80   const [evidence, setEvidence] = useState<Record<string, { eligibility: Eligibility; reason?: string }>>({})
  81   const trigger = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  82   const popup = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  83   const reposition = useRef<(() => void) | null>(null)
  84   const [position, setPosition] = useState({ left: 8, top: 8, width: 320, maxHeight: 360 })
  85   const close = useCallback(() => { setOpen(false); trigger.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true }) }, [])
  86   useLayoutEffect(() => {
  87     if (!open) return
  88     const place = () => {
  89       const rect = trigger.current?.getBoundingClientRect()
  90       if (!rect) return
  91       if (rect.bottom < 0 || rect.top > window.innerHeight || rect.right < 0 || rect.left > window.innerWidth) {
  92         close()
  93         return
  94       }
  95       const width = Math.min(344, Math.max(0, window.innerWidth - 16))
  96       const above = rect.top - 16
  97       const below = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom - 16
  98       const upward = above > below
  99       const maxHeight = Math.max(0, Math.min(400, upward ? above : below))
 100       const contentHeight = popup.current ? popup.current.scrollHeight + popup.current.offsetHeight - popup.current.clientHeight : maxHeight
 101       const next = { width, maxHeight, left: Math.max(8, Math.min(rect.left, window.innerWidth - width - 8)),
 102         top: upward ? Math.max(8, rect.top - Math.min(contentHeight, maxHeight) - 8) : rect.bottom + 8 }
 103       setPosition(current => current.width === next.width && current.maxHeight === next.maxHeight
 104         && current.left === next.left && current.top === next.top ? current : next)
 105     }
 106     reposition.current = place
 107     place()
 108     const initial = popup.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[aria-selected="true"]')
 109       ?? popup.current?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role="option"]') ?? popup.current
 110     initial?.focus()
 111     const outside = (event: PointerEvent) => {
 112       if (event.target instanceof Node && !popup.current?.contains(event.target) && !trigger.current?.contains(event.target)) close()
 113     }
 114     window.addEventListener('resize', place)
 115     window.addEventListener('scroll', place, true)
 116     document.addEventListener('pointerdown', outside)
 117     // Width constraints and late evidence can change wrapping/height without
 118     // changing the anchor. Re-measure locally, without refocusing the menu.
 119     const observer = typeof ResizeObserver === 'undefined' ? null : new ResizeObserver(place)
 120     if (popup.current) observer?.observe(popup.current)
 121     if (trigger.current) observer?.observe(trigger.current)
 122     return () => {
 123       window.removeEventListener('resize', place)
 124       window.removeEventListener('scroll', place, true)
 125       document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', outside)
 126       observer?.disconnect()
 127       reposition.current = null
 128     }
 129   }, [open, close, catalog])
 130   useLayoutEffect(() => {
 131     if (open) reposition.current?.()
 132   }, [open, evidence])
 133   useEffect(() => {
 134     let live = true
 135     setCatalog({ state: 'loading' })
 136     service.list().then(
 137       configs => { if (live) setCatalog({ state: 'ready', configs }) },
 138       cause => { if (live) setCatalog({ state: 'error', message: describe(cause) }) })
 139     return () => { live = false }
 140   }, [service, attempt])
 141   const retry = useCallback(() => setAttempt(value => value + 1), [])
 142 
 143   const harnessId = location.kind === 'session'
 144     ? location.session.harnessId
 145     : (location.target?.harnessId ?? '')
 146   const configs = catalog.state === 'ready' ? catalog.configs : []
 147   const scoped = configs.filter((config) => config.harness === harnessId && config.archivedAt === null)
 148   useEffect(() => {
 149     let live = true
 150     setEvidence({})
 151     if (inspectChoice && catalog.state === 'ready' && !disabled) {
 152       for (const config of catalog.configs.filter(item => item.harness === harnessId && item.archivedAt === null)) {
 153         for (const model of config.models.filter(item => item.availability === 'available')) {
 154           const key = `${config.id}\u0000${model.modelId}`
 155           void inspectChoice({ harnessId, providerConfigId: config.id, modelId: model.modelId }, config.version)
 156             .then(value => { if (live) setEvidence(current => ({ ...current, [key]: value })) },
 157               cause => { if (live) setEvidence(current => ({ ...current, [key]: { eligibility: 'unknown', reason: describe(cause) } })) })
 158         }
 159       }
 160     }
 161     return () => { live = false }
 162   }, [inspectChoice, catalog, harnessId, disabled, inspectionAttempt])
 163   const rows = scoped.flatMap((config) =>
 164     config.models.map((model) => {
 165       // The backend's per-model availability is authoritative and can never be
 166       // overridden by a session eligibility verdict.
 167       const available = model.availability === 'available'
 168       return {
 169         config, modelId: model.modelId, displayName: model.displayName || model.modelId,
 170         available,
 171         unavailableReason: model.unavailableReason ?? undefined,
 172         eligibility: inspectChoice ? evidence[`${config.id}\u0000${model.modelId}`]?.eligibility ?? 'unknown'
 173           : source.eligibility(harnessId, config.id, model.modelId),
 174         reason: inspectChoice ? (evidence[`${config.id}\u0000${model.modelId}`]
 175           ? evidence[`${config.id}\u0000${model.modelId}`].reason : location.kind === 'draft'
 176             ? '创建会话后才能核实模型。' : disabled ? '当前会话暂不能选择模型。' : '正在核实此会话的模型可选择性。')
 177           : source.reason?.(harnessId, config.id, model.modelId),
 178       }
 179     }))
 180   const selectable = (row: (typeof rows)[number]) => !disabled && row.available && row.eligibility === 'ready'
 181   const chosen = queued
 182   const chosenConfig = configs.find(config => config.id === chosen?.providerConfigId)
 183   const chosenModel = chosenConfig?.models.find(model => model.modelId === chosen?.modelId)
 184   const currentRows = current ? rows.filter(row => row.modelId === current.modelId && row.config.provider === current.provider) : []
 185 
 186   return (
 187     <div data-testid="model-selector" style={{ display: 'inline-flex', minWidth: 0, maxWidth: '100%' }}>
 188       <style>{selectorStyles}</style>
 189       <button type="button" ref={trigger} className="mp-selector-trigger" aria-haspopup="listbox" aria-expanded={open}
 190         data-testid="model-selector-trigger" onClick={() => {
 191         if (!open) {
 192           // Native readiness may change without a new session identity. Opening
 193           // is an explicit fresh read, not a poll or a configuration mutation.
 194           setEvidence({})
 195           setInspectionAttempt(value => value + 1)
 196           onOpen?.()
 197         }
 198         setOpen(!open)
 199       }}>
 200         <span>{chosen ? `${chosenConfig?.displayName ?? '已选供应商'} › ${chosenModel?.displayName || chosen.modelId}`
 201           : current ? `${current.provider ? `${current.provider} › ` : ''}${current.modelId}` : '模型'}</span>
 202         {queued && <small>下轮使用</small>}<span aria-hidden="true">⌄</span>
 203       </button>
 204       {open && createPortal(
 205         <div ref={popup} className="mp-selector-popover" style={{ position: 'fixed', ...position }} tabIndex={-1}
 206           role="listbox" aria-label="Provider › Model" data-testid="model-selector-list" onKeyDown={event => {
 207             if (event.key === 'Escape') { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation(); close() }
 208             if (event.key === 'Tab') close()
 209             if (['ArrowDown', 'ArrowUp', 'Home', 'End'].includes(event.key)) {
 210               event.preventDefault()
 211               const options = Array.from(popup.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[role="option"]') ?? [])
 212               const index = options.indexOf(document.activeElement as HTMLButtonElement)
 213               const next = event.key === 'Home' ? 0 : event.key === 'End' ? options.length - 1
 214                 : (index + (event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : -1) + options.length) % options.length
 215               options[next]?.focus()
 216             }
 217           }}>
 218           <div className="mp-selector-heading">模型<span>{location.kind === 'draft' ? '新建会话' : '当前会话'} · {harnessId || '未选择 Harness'}</span></div>
 219           {statusReason && <p role="status" data-testid="model-owner-note-details">{statusReason}</p>}
 220           {catalog.state === 'loading' && <p data-testid="model-load-pending">正在加载模型列表…</p>}
 221           {catalog.state === 'error' && (
 222             <div role="alert" data-testid="model-load-error">
 223               <p>模型列表加载失败：{catalog.message}</p>
 224               <button type="button" data-testid="model-load-retry" onClick={retry}>重试</button>
 225             </div>
 226           )}
 227           {catalog.state === 'ready' && harnessId === '' && <p>新建会话请先选择 Harness。</p>}
 228           {catalog.state === 'ready' && harnessId !== '' && rows.length === 0 && <p>此 Harness 暂无 Provider 配置。</p>}
 229           {rows.map((row, index) => {
 230             const isSelectable = selectable(row)
 231             return (
 232               <div key={`${row.config.id}/${row.modelId}`}>
 233               {(index === 0 || rows[index - 1].config.id !== row.config.id) && <div className="mp-selector-group">{row.config.displayName}</div>}
 234               <button type="button" className="mp-selector-option" role="option"
 235                 aria-selected={queued ? queued.modelId === row.modelId && queued.providerConfigId === row.config.id
 236                   : currentRows.length === 1 && currentRows[0] === row}
 237                 aria-disabled={!isSelectable}
 238                 data-testid={`model-option-${row.config.id}-${row.modelId}`}
 239                 onClick={() => {
 240                   if (!isSelectable) return
 241                   onQueue?.({ harnessId, providerConfigId: row.config.id, modelId: row.modelId,
 242                     ...(inspectChoice ? { configVersion: row.config.version } : {}) })
 243                   close()
 244                 }}>
 245                 <span className="mp-selector-model">{row.displayName}<span aria-hidden="true">{queued?.modelId === row.modelId && queued?.providerConfigId === row.config.id ? '✓' : ''}</span></span>
 246                 <span className="mp-selector-evidence">
 247                   {!row.available
 248                     ? `${ELIGIBILITY_GLYPHS.unsupported} ${row.unavailableReason ?? ELIGIBILITY_LABELS.unsupported}`
 249                     : `${ELIGIBILITY_GLYPHS[row.eligibility]} ${ELIGIBILITY_LABELS[row.eligibility]}`}
 250                   {row.reason ? `：${row.reason}` : ''}
 251                 </span>
 252               </button>
 253               </div>
 254             )
 255           })}
 256           {catalog.state === 'ready' && harnessId !== '' && rows.length > 0
 257             && rows.every((row) => !selectable(row)) && (
 258             <p data-testid="no-selectable">此会话暂不可选模型（尚无可用证据）。</p>
 259           )}
 260         </div>, document.body,
 261       )}
 262     </div>
 263   )
 264 }
